-- [dbo].[ApifnCheckDuplicateDocNumber]





/*RZ990519*/
Create Function [dbo].[ApifnCheckDuplicateDocNumber]
(		
	/*
		Select * from dbo.ApifnCheckDuplicateDocNumber(1, 10, 1)
	*/
@DocNumber INT, 
@CurrDocID INT, 
@OprId INT 
)
RETURNS TABLE
AS
RETURN
(
	SELECT DocNumber
	FROM dbo.WSDoc
	WHERE DocNumber = @DocNumber
	AND DocID <> @CurrDocID
	AND OprId = @OprId
)




GO
-- [dbo].[ApifnCheckDuplicateOrderNumber]






/*RZ980416*/
/*RZ990811- Change type from int to bigint*/
Create Function [dbo].[ApifnCheckDuplicateOrderNumber]
(			
/* 
	Select * from dbo.ApifnCheckDuplicateOrderNumber(98190001, 0, 28,1)
*/
@OrderNumber BIGINT,/*RZ990811- Change type from int to bigint*/ 
@CurrOrderID INT, 
@FinancialPeriod INT,
@OrderOprId INT 
)
RETURNS TABLE
AS
RETURN
(
	SELECT ordNumber
	FROM dbo.WSOrder
	WHERE ordNumber = @OrderNumber
	AND OrderID <> @CurrOrderID
	AND FPId = @FinancialPeriod
	And OrderOprID = @OrderOprId 
)







GO
-- [dbo].[ApifnReturnDocNumber]





/*RZ990519*/
Create Function [dbo].[ApifnReturnDocNumber]
(
	/*		
		Select dbo.ApifnReturnDocNumber(1)
	*/
	 
	@OprId INT 
)
RETURNS INT
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @DocNumber INT 
	SELECT  @DocNumber = ISNULL(MAX( DocNumber), 0)
	FROM dbo.WSDoc
	WHERE OprId = @OprId
	RETURN(@DocNumber + 1)
/**/
END





GO
-- [dbo].[ApifnReturnOrderDailyCounter]







/*RZ14011109*/
Create Function [dbo].[ApifnReturnOrderDailyCounter]
(			
/* 
	Select dbo.ApifnReturnOrderDailyCounter(1, '2023-01-29')
*/
@OrderOprId INT,
@CurrentDate DATETIME 
)
RETURNS BIGINT		
AS
BEGIN
/**/

DECLARE @DailyCounter BIGINT, @ordDailyCounter BIGINT

SET @DailyCounter = ISNULL((SELECT TOP (1) [DailyCounter] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr] WHERE [OrderOprID] = @OrderOprId),0)
IF(@DailyCounter > 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @DailyCounter = @DailyCounter - 1
/**/
END
/**/
SELECT 
	@ordDailyCounter = ISNULL(MAX([ordDailyCounter]), @DailyCounter)
FROM [dbo].[WSOrder]
WHERE OrderOprID = @OrderOprId 
AND CAST([ordDateTime] AS DATE) = CAST(@CurrentDate AS DATE)

SET @ordDailyCounter = @ordDailyCounter + 1

RETURN(@ordDailyCounter)

/**/
END





GO
-- [dbo].[ApifnReturnOrderNumber]






/*RZ980416*/
/*RZ990811- Change type from int to bigint*/
Create Function [dbo].[ApifnReturnOrderNumber]
(			
/*
	Select dbo.ApifnReturnOrderNumber(28, 1)
*/
@FinancialPeriod INT,
@OrderOprId INT 
)
RETURNS BIGINT/*RZ990811- Change type from int to bigint*/
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	/*DECLARE @Number INT, @CurrentYear INT, @BeginNumber INT */
	DECLARE @Number BIGINT, @CurrentYear INT, @BeginNumber BIGINT /*RZ990811- Change type from int to bigint*/
	SELECT @CurrentYear = SUBSTRING(CAST(FDate AS VARCHAR(4)), 3, 2)
	FROM dbo.WSFinancialPeriod 
	Where Id = @FinancialPeriod

	/*SET @BeginNumber = CAST(CAST(@CurrentYear AS VARCHAR(4))+CAST(@OrderOprId AS VARCHAR(5)) + '0000' AS INT) */
	SET @BeginNumber = CAST(CAST(@CurrentYear AS VARCHAR(4))+CAST(@OrderOprId AS VARCHAR(5)) + '0000' AS BIGINT)/*RZ990811- Change type from int to bigint*/

	SELECT @Number = ISNULL(MAX( ordNumber), @BeginNumber)
	FROM dbo.WSOrder
	WHERE OrderOprID = @OrderOprId 
	AND FPId = @FinancialPeriod

	SET @Number = @Number + 1

	RETURN(@Number)
/**/
END







GO
-- [dbo].[ApifnReturnOrderSerialNumber]





/*RZ980417*/
Create Function [dbo].[ApifnReturnOrderSerialNumber]
(			
/*
	 Select dbo.ApifnReturnOrderSerialNumber(1, 2)
*/
@FinancialPeriod INT,
@OrderOprId INT 
)
RETURNS INT	
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @SNumber INT, @CurrentYear INT, @BeginNumber INT 

	SELECT @CurrentYear = SUBSTRING(CAST(FDate AS VARCHAR(4)), 3, 2)
	FROM dbo.WSFinancialPeriod 
	Where Id = @FinancialPeriod

	SET @BeginNumber = CAST(CAST(@CurrentYear AS VARCHAR(4))+
			CAST(@OrderOprId AS VARCHAR(5)) + '0000' AS INT) 

	SELECT @SNumber = ISNULL(MAX( ordSerial), @BeginNumber)
	FROM dbo.WSOrder
	WHERE OrderOprID = @OrderOprId 
	AND FPId = @FinancialPeriod

	SET @SNumber = @SNumber + 1

	RETURN(@SNumber)
/**/
END





GO
-- [dbo].[ApispAutoCloseTicketAfterDay]







/*NKR14021117*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispAutoCloseTicketAfterDay]

(
	/*
		exec CRMspAutoCloseTicketAfterDay 'dasti'
	*/
	@AppType VARCHAR(20)
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*=========================== Set variabels =============================*/
DECLARE @TicketAutoCloseAfterDay INT,
@TicketAutoCloseAfterDate DATETIME 

SELECT TOP 1
	@TicketAutoCloseAfterDay = [TicketAutoCloseAfterDay] 
FROM [dbo].[CrmFoSetting]
/*-----------------------------------------------------------------*/
IF(@TicketAutoCloseAfterDay IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
/**/
	/*-------------------------------------------------------*/
	SET @TicketAutoCloseAfterDate = DATEADD(DAY, @TicketAutoCloseAfterDay, GETDATE())
	/*----------------------------*/
	DECLARE @RegisterDate DATETIME,@UserId INT,
	@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
	@prjFinishedDate NVARCHAR(8),
	@prjFinishedDay NVARCHAR(50),
	@prjFinishedTime NVARCHAR(15),
	@prjFinishedDateTime DATETIME,
	@Year1 SMALLINT,
	@Month1 SMALLINT
	
	SET @RegisterDate = GETDATE()
	SET @RegisterDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](CAST(@RegisterDate AS DATE)))
	SET @prjFinishedDateTime = @RegisterDate
	SET @prjFinishedDate = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](CAST(@prjFinishedDateTime AS DATE)))
	SET @prjFinishedTime = (SELECT CONVERT(CHAR(8), @prjFinishedDateTime, 108))
	SET @prjFinishedDay = (SELECT DATEPART(dw,@prjFinishedDateTime) )
	SET @Year1 = CAST((SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM WSFinancialPeriod ORDER BY [ID] DESC)  AS SMALLINT)
	SET @Month1 = CAST(SUBSTRING(@RegisterDatePersian,5,2) AS INT)	
	SET @UserId = (SELECT TOP 1 UserId FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserId > 0 ORDER BY UserId)
	
	UPDATE [dbo].[CrmProjects]
	SET  [prjIsFinished] = 1
		,[prjFinishedDate] = @prjFinishedDate
		,[prjFinishedDay] = @prjFinishedDay
		,[prjFinishedTime] = @prjFinishedTime
		,[prjFinishedDateTime] = @prjFinishedDateTime
		,[prjFinishedUserId] = @UserId			
		,[UserId] = @UserId
		,[Year1] = @Year1
		,[Month1] = @Month1
		,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
		,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian
		,[AppType] = @AppType			
	WHERE ProjectID IN(SELECT [PrjId] FROM
						(
							SELECT 								
								(SELECT TOP 1  CC.FirstRegisterUserId FROM [dbo].[CrmConversation] CC 
								WHERE CC.[cnvsProjectId] = P.[ProjectID] 
								AND ISNULL(CC.[cnvsFOCmpCanSee], 0)  = 1
								AND ISNULL(CC.[cnvsIsTicket], 0)  = 1
								ORDER BY ISNULL(CC.[cnvsDateTime], CC.[ConversationID]) DESC) [cnvsFirstRegisterUserId]
								,(SELECT TOP 1  CC.FirstRegisterDate FROM [dbo].[CrmConversation] CC 
								WHERE CC.[cnvsProjectId] = P.[ProjectID] 
								AND ISNULL(CC.[cnvsFOCmpCanSee], 0)  = 1
								AND ISNULL(CC.[cnvsIsTicket], 0)  = 1
								ORDER BY ISNULL(CC.[cnvsDateTime], CC.[ConversationID]) DESC) [cnvsFirstRegisterDate]							
								,P.ProjectID [PrjId]
							FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] P
							WHERE ISNULL(P.[prjFOCmpCanSee], 0)  = 0
							AND ISNULL(P.[prjIsTicket], 0)  = 1
							AND ISNULL(P.[prjIsFinished],0) = 0
						) AS Tmp
						WHERE [cnvsFirstRegisterUserId] NOT IN (-10,-20) /*User site*/
						AND (DATEDIFF(DAY, [cnvsFirstRegisterDate], GETDATE())) >=  @TicketAutoCloseAfterDay

					)
	
/**/
END
/**/
END







GO
-- [dbo].[ApispBuyOrderVerify]





/*NKR14020802*/
CREATE Procedure [dbo].[ApispBuyOrderVerify]
(
	@BuyOrderId INT,
	@VerifierUserId INT,
	@VerifyDate DATETIME,
	@DefLangCollation NVARCHAR(50) = 'fa-IR'
)
As
BEGIN
	/**/
	UPDATE dbo.WSBuyOrder
	   SET bordIsVerified = 1,
		   bordVerifierId = @VerifierUserId,
		   bordIsFixed = 1,
		   bordVerifiedRegisterDate = @VerifyDate,
		   bordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian = FORMAT(@VerifyDate, 'yyyyMMdd', @DefLangCollation)
	 WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId
	/**/
END






GO
-- [dbo].[ApispBuyOrderVerifyExit]





/*NKR14020802*/
CREATE Procedure [dbo].[ApispBuyOrderVerifyExit]
(
	@BuyOrderId INT
)
As
BEGIN
	/**/
	UPDATE dbo.WSBuyOrder
	   SET bordIsVerified = 0,
	       bordVerifierId = NULL,
		   bordIsFixed = 0,
		   bordVerifiedRegisterDate = NULL,
		   bordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian = NULL
	 WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId
	/**/
END

-- [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateBuyOrderAmounts]





/*RZ14030301*/
/*RZ14030308- Add fields*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateBuyOrderAmounts]
/* 	
	exec ApispCalcAndUpdateBuyOrderAmounts 292,3,1065,1065	
*/
(
@BuyOrderId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
/*------------------------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @cuUnitIsDecimal BIT
SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = (SELECT bordCurrencyUnitId 
												  FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder] 
												  WHERE BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderId))
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
DECLARE @SumPriceAmount NUMERIC(18,2)
DECLARE @SumQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@TotalDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TotalTaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
SELECT  
		@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(CAST(SUM(ISNULL([bordICostSum],0)  + ISNULL([bordITaxSum],0)) AS NUMERIC(18,2)),0)
		,@SumQuantity = SUM(bordIQuantity)
		,@TotalDiscountSum = SUM(bordIDiscountSum)
		,@TotalTaxSum = SUM(bordITaxSum)		
FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]
WHERE BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderID
AND ISNULL(bordINotInSum, 0) = 0
/**/

IF(ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) >= 0)
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @bordItemCount INT, @bordItemsAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@bordDiscount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@bordSumAmount NUMERIC(18,2), @bordTax NUMERIC(18,2), @bordTaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2),
	@bordCalculateTax BIT,@bordTotalAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@bordTransportation NUMERIC(18,2),
	@bordDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2),@bordRound NUMERIC(18,2),@bordStatusId INT,
	@bordTotalAmountStr NVARCHAR(1000)
	/*RZ14030308- Add fields*/
	,@bordFacilityCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordFacilityCostPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
	,@bordFreightCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordFreightCurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL,@bordFinalFreightCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
	,@bordCustomsFee NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordCustomsCurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL,@bordFinalCustomsFee NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
	,@bordCommission NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordCommissionCurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL,@bordFinalCommission NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
	,@bordTotalFinalCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
	/*RZ14030308- End*/
	/**/
	SELECT 
       @bordDiscount = [bordDiscount],
       @bordTaxIndex = [bordTaxIndex],
       @bordCalculateTax = [bordCalculateTax],
       @bordDiscountPercent = [bordDiscountPercent],
       @bordTransportation = [bordTransportation],
       @bordRound = [bordRound],
	   @bordStatusId= [bordStatusId],
	   /*RZ14030308- Add fields*/
	   @bordFacilityCost = [bordFacilityCost],
	   @bordFacilityCostPercent = [bordFacilityCostPercent],
	   @bordFreightCost = [bordFreightCost],
	   @bordFreightCurrencyRate = [bordFreightCurrencyRate],
	   @bordFinalFreightCost = [bordFinalFreightCost],
	   @bordCustomsFee = [bordCustomsFee],
	   @bordCustomsCurrencyRate = [bordCustomsCurrencyRate],
	   @bordFinalCustomsFee = [bordFinalCustomsFee],
	   @bordCommission = [bordCommission],
	   @bordCommissionCurrencyRate = [bordCommissionCurrencyRate],
	   @bordFinalCommission = [bordFinalCommission],
	   @bordTotalFinalCost = [bordTotalFinalCost]
	   /*RZ14030308- End*/
	FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder] O
	WHERE O.[BuyOrderID] = @BuyOrderId
	/**/
	SET @bordItemsAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0)
	IF(@bordDiscountPercent IS NOT NULL AND @bordDiscountPercent > 0)
	BEGIN
		SET @bordDiscount = (ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) * ISNULL(@bordDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
	END
	/**/
	SET @bordSumAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) - ISNULL(@bordDiscount,0)
	/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@bordCalculateTax,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
		SET @bordTax = ISNULL(@bordSumAmount,0) * ISNULL(@bordTaxIndex,0) / 100
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @bordTax = 0
	END
	/**/
	SET @bordTotalAmount = ISNULL(ISNULL(@bordSumAmount,0) + ISNULL(@bordTax,0) + ISNULL(@bordTransportation,0) - ISNULL(@bordRound,0),0)
	/**/
	SET @bordItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId)
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	/*RZ14030308- Add fields*/	 
	IF(ISNULL(@bordFacilityCostPercent,0) <> 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @bordFacilityCost = (ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) * ISNULL(@bordFacilityCostPercent,0)) / 100
	/**/
	END
	/*------*/
	SET @bordFreightCost = ISNULL(@bordFreightCost,0)
	SET @bordFreightCurrencyRate = ISNULL(@bordFreightCurrencyRate,1)
	SET @bordFinalFreightCost = (ISNULL(@bordFreightCost,0) * ISNULL(@bordFreightCurrencyRate,1))
	/**/
	SET @bordCustomsFee = ISNULL(@bordCustomsFee,0)
	SET @bordCustomsCurrencyRate = ISNULL(@bordCustomsCurrencyRate,1) 
	SET @bordFinalCustomsFee = (ISNULL(@bordCustomsFee,0) * ISNULL(@bordCustomsCurrencyRate,1))
	/**/
	SET @bordCommission = ISNULL(@bordCommission,0)
	SET @bordCommissionCurrencyRate = ISNULL(@bordCommissionCurrencyRate,1)
	SET @bordFinalCommission = (ISNULL(@bordCommission,0) * ISNULL(@bordCommissionCurrencyRate,1))
	/**/
	SET @bordTotalFinalCost =  ISNULL(@bordTotalAmount,0) + ISNULL(@bordFacilityCost,0) + ISNULL(@bordFinalFreightCost,0) + ISNULL(@bordFinalCustomsFee,0) + ISNULL(@bordFinalCommission,0)
	/*RZ14030308- End*/
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
	BEGIN	
		SET @bordItemsAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordItemsAmount))					
		SET @bordSumAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordSumAmount))		
		SET @bordTax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordTax))		
		SET @bordTotalAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordTotalAmount))		
		SET @bordDiscount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordDiscount))
		SET @bordTransportation = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordTransportation))
		SET @bordRound = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordRound))
		SET @TotalDiscountSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)))	
		SET @TotalTaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)))			
	END	
	/*************** TotalAmountStr *******************/
	DECLARE @TotalAmoutI NVARCHAR(MAX),@TotalAmoutII NVARCHAR(MAX)
	IF(@bordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN			
		SET @TotalAmoutI = SUBSTRING(CAST(@bordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), 1, CHARINDEX('.',CAST(@bordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))-1)                                    
		SET @TotalAmoutII = SUBSTRING(CAST(@bordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), CHARINDEX('.', CAST(@bordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))+1, 2)
		/**/
		IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1065)
		BEGIN
			SET @bordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @bordTotalAmountStr = @bordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @bordTotalAmountStr = @bordTotalAmountStr 
		END
		ELSE IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1025)
		BEGIN
			SET @bordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @bordTotalAmountStr = @bordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @bordTotalAmountStr = @bordTotalAmountStr
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SET @bordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @bordTotalAmountStr = @bordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
			END
			SET @bordTotalAmountStr = REPLACE(@bordTotalAmountStr,'-',' ') 
		END
	END
	/**************************************************/
	/*Update*/
	UPDATE [dbo].[WSBuyOrder]
	SET  [bordItemCount] = ISNULL(@bordItemCount,0)
		,[bordItemsAmount] = ISNULL(@bordItemsAmount,0)
		,[bordSumAmount] = ISNULL(@bordSumAmount,0)
		,[bordTax] = ISNULL(@bordTax,0)
		,[bordTotalAmount] = @bordTotalAmount
		,[bordTotalAmountStr] = @bordTotalAmountStr
		,[bordDiscount] = ISNULL(@bordDiscount,0)
		,[bordTransportation] = ISNULL(@bordTransportation,0)
		,[bordRound] = ISNULL(@bordRound,0)
		,[Month1] = @Month1
		,[bordItemsQty] = ISNULL(@SumQuantity,0)
		,[bordItemsDiscount] = ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)
		,[bordItemsTax] = ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)	
		/*RZ14030308- Add fields*/
		,[bordFacilityCost] = @bordFacilityCost
		,[bordFacilityCostPercent] = @bordFacilityCostPercent
		,[bordFreightCost] = @bordFreightCost
		,[bordFreightCurrencyRate] = @bordFreightCurrencyRate 
		,[bordFinalFreightCost] = @bordFinalFreightCost 
		,[bordCustomsFee] = @bordCustomsFee
		,[bordCustomsCurrencyRate] = @bordCustomsCurrencyRate
		,[bordFinalCustomsFee] = @bordFinalCustomsFee
		,[bordCommission] = @bordCommission
		,[bordCommissionCurrencyRate] = @bordCommissionCurrencyRate 
		,[bordFinalCommission] = @bordFinalCommission
		,[bordTotalFinalCost] = @bordTotalFinalCost
		/*RZ14030308- End*/
	WHERE BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderId
	/**/
	IF(@bordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @LangId INT
		/**/
		DECLARE Crsr CURSOR FOR
			SELECT LanguageID
			FROM [dbo].[Languages]		
			WHERE LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			AND ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1
		/**/
		OPEN Crsr
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			While @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN	 
				DECLARE @ordTotalAmountStrLang NVARCHAR(1000)	
				/*-------------------------------------------*/				
				IF(@LangId = 1065)
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang 
				END
				ELSE IF(@LangId = 1025)
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
					END							
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = REPLACE(@ordTotalAmountStrLang,'-',' ')
				END
				/**/
				DECLARE @OrdIdLang INT
				SET @OrdIdLang = (SELECT [WSBuyOrderLangID] FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder_Lang] WHERE BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderId AND LanguageID = @LangId)
				IF(@OrdIdLang IS NOT NULL)
				BEGIN
					UPDATE [dbo].[WSBuyOrder_Lang]
						SET [bordTotalAmountStr] = @ordTotalAmountStrLang
					WHERE [WSBuyOrderLangID] = @OrdIdLang						
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSBuyOrder_Lang]
						([BuyOrderID]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[bordTotalAmountStr])
					VALUES
						(@BuyOrderId
						,@LangId
						,@ordTotalAmountStrLang)
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			END
		CLOSE Crsr
		DEALLOCATE Crsr	
	END							
	/*==================================================*/
	/*================= Update status ==================*/	
	DECLARE @SumOrdPayAmount NUMERIC(18,2)
	SET @SumOrdPayAmount = ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL( SUM(bordpayAmount), 0) sumordpayAmount
									FROM dbo.WSBuyOrderPayments
									WHERE bordpayOrderId = @BuyOrderId
									AND ISNULL(bordpayDeActive,0) = 0),0)
	
	IF(ISNULL(@SumOrdPayAmount,0) <> 0 AND (@bordStatusId <> 4 AND @bordStatusId <> 5))
	BEGIN
	/**/
		IF(@SumOrdPayAmount = 0 AND @bordStatusId <>1)
			SET @bordStatusId = 1
		/**/
		ELSE IF(@SumOrdPayAmount < @bordTotalAmount)
			SET @bordStatusId = 2
		/**/
		ELSE IF(@SumOrdPayAmount >= @bordTotalAmount)
			SET @bordStatusId = 3
		/**/	 		
		/**/
		UPDATE [dbo].[WSBuyOrder]
		SET [bordStatusId] = @bordStatusId 
		WHERE [BuyOrderID] = @BuyOrderId
	/**/
	END
	/*==================================================*/
END	

/*==================================================*/	

EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateBuyOrderAmountsCurrency]	
@BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId,
@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID

/*==================================================*/
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateBuyOrderAmountsCurrency]







/*RZ14030301*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateBuyOrderAmountsCurrency]
/* 	
	exec ApispCalcAndUpdateBuyOrderAmountsCurrency 397,1033,1065	
*/
(
@BuyOrderId INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @bordItemsAmount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@bordDiscount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@bordSumAmount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@bordTax NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,	
@bordTotalAmount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,	@bordTransportation NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,	
@bordRound NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@bordCurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,
@bordItemsDiscount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@bordItemsTax NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@bordCurrencyUnitIdI INT = NULL,@bordCurrencyUnitIRate FLOAT = NULL,
@bordItemsAmountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@bordDiscountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@bordSumAmountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@bordTaxCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@bordTotalAmountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@bordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@bordTransportationCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@bordRoundCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@bordItemsDiscountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@bordItemsTaxCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@CurrencyUnitIsDecimal BIT = NULL,@IsDivisionCurrencyRate BIT = NULL,@RialCurrencyUnitId INT = NULL
/*-------------------------------------*/
SELECT TOP (1) 
     @bordItemsAmount = [bordItemsAmount]
    ,@bordDiscount = [bordDiscount]
    ,@bordSumAmount = [bordSumAmount]
    ,@bordTax = [bordTax]
    ,@bordTotalAmount = [bordTotalAmount]
    ,@bordTransportation = [bordTransportation]
    ,@bordRound = [bordRound]
    ,@bordCurrencyUnitId = [bordCurrencyUnitId]
    ,@bordItemsDiscount = [bordItemsDiscount]
    ,@bordItemsTax = [bordItemsTax]
    ,@bordCurrencyUnitIdI = [bordCurrencyUnitIdI]
    ,@bordCurrencyUnitIRate = [bordCurrencyUnitIRate]   
FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder] BO
WHERE BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderId
/*-----------------------------------*/
SET @CurrencyUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
							  FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
							  WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = @bordCurrencyUnitIdI)
SET @RialCurrencyUnitId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyUnitID]            
						   FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits]
						   WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST([CurrencyUnit] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
								  NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
								= (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(N'����' AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
								   NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
							)

IF(ISNULL(@DefaultLanguageID,0) = 0)
BEGIN
	SET @DefaultLanguageID = (SELECT TOP(1) [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])
END
/*-----------------------------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitId,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitIdI,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitIRate,0) <> 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitId,0) <> ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitIdI,0))
	BEGIN
	/**/	
		/*--------------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@RialCurrencyUnitId,0) = ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitId,0))
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*Change rate when main currency unit is rial*/
			SET @bordCurrencyUnitIRate = CAST((1.0/ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitIRate,1)) AS FLOAT)
		/**/
		END		
		/*--------------------------------------------------*/			
		IF(ISNULL(@bordItemsAmount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @bordItemsAmountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@bordItemsAmount,0) * ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@bordDiscount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @bordDiscountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@bordDiscount,0) * ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@bordSumAmount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @bordSumAmountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@bordSumAmount,0) * ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@bordTax,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @bordTaxCurrencyI = ISNULL(@bordTax,0) * ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@bordTotalAmount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @bordTotalAmountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@bordTotalAmount,0) * ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@bordTransportation,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @bordTransportationCurrencyI = ISNULL(@bordTransportation,0) * ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@bordRound,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @bordRoundCurrencyI = ISNULL(@bordRound,0) * ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@bordItemsDiscount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @bordItemsDiscountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@bordItemsDiscount,0) * ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@bordItemsTax,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @bordItemsTaxCurrencyI = ISNULL(@bordItemsTax,0) * ISNULL(@bordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		/*--------------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@CurrencyUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
		BEGIN	
		/**/
			SET @bordItemsAmountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordItemsAmountCurrencyI))	
			SET @bordDiscountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordDiscountCurrencyI))
			SET @bordSumAmountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordSumAmountCurrencyI))
			SET @bordTaxCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordTaxCurrencyI))
			SET @bordTotalAmountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordTotalAmountCurrencyI))
			SET @bordTransportationCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordTransportationCurrencyI))
			SET @bordRoundCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordRoundCurrencyI))
			SET @bordItemsDiscountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordItemsDiscountCurrencyI))
			SET @bordItemsTaxCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordItemsTaxCurrencyI))
		/**/
		END
		/*--------------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END
/**/
END
/*-----------------------------------*/
/*************** TotalAmountStr *******************/
DECLARE @TotalAmoutI NVARCHAR(MAX),@TotalAmoutII NVARCHAR(MAX)
IF(@bordTotalAmountCurrencyI IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN			
	SET @TotalAmoutI = SUBSTRING(CAST(@bordTotalAmountCurrencyI AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), 1, CHARINDEX('.',CAST(@bordTotalAmountCurrencyI AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))-1)                                    
	SET @TotalAmoutII = SUBSTRING(CAST(@bordTotalAmountCurrencyI AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), CHARINDEX('.', CAST(@bordTotalAmountCurrencyI AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))+1, 2)
	/**/
	IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1065)
	BEGIN
		SET @bordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
		IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
		BEGIN
			SET @bordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = @bordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
		END
		SET @bordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = @bordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr 
	END
	ELSE IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1025)
	BEGIN
		SET @bordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
		IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
		BEGIN
			SET @bordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = @bordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
		END
		SET @bordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = @bordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @bordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
		IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
		BEGIN
			SET @bordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = @bordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
		END
		SET @bordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = REPLACE(@bordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr,'-',' ') 
	END
END
/*-----------------------------------*/
UPDATE [dbo].[WSBuyOrder]
SET  [bordItemsAmountCurrencyI] = @bordItemsAmountCurrencyI
    ,[bordDiscountCurrencyI] = @bordDiscountCurrencyI
    ,[bordSumAmountCurrencyI] = @bordSumAmountCurrencyI
    ,[bordTaxCurrencyI] = @bordTaxCurrencyI
    ,[bordTotalAmountCurrencyI] = @bordTotalAmountCurrencyI
    ,[bordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr] = @bordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr
    ,[bordTransportationCurrencyI] = @bordTransportationCurrencyI
    ,[bordRoundCurrencyI] = @bordRoundCurrencyI
    ,[bordItemsDiscountCurrencyI] = @bordItemsDiscountCurrencyI
    ,[bordItemsTaxCurrencyI] = @bordItemsTaxCurrencyI
WHERE [BuyOrderID] = @BuyOrderId
/*-----------------------------------*/
/*************** TotalAmountStr in other language *******************/
IF(@bordTotalAmountCurrencyI IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @LangId INT
	/**/
	DECLARE Crsr CURSOR LOCAL FOR
		SELECT LanguageID
		FROM [dbo].[Languages]		
		WHERE LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
		AND ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1
	/**/
	OPEN Crsr
	FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
		While @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN	 
			DECLARE @bordTotalAmountStrLang NVARCHAR(1000)	
			/*-------------------------------------------*/				
			IF(@LangId = 1065)
			BEGIN
				SET @bordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
				IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
				BEGIN
					SET @bordTotalAmountStrLang = @bordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
				END
				SET @bordTotalAmountStrLang = @bordTotalAmountStrLang /*+ N' ����'*/
			END
			ELSE IF(@LangId = 1025)
			BEGIN
				SET @bordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
				IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
				BEGIN
					SET @bordTotalAmountStrLang = @bordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
				END
				SET @bordTotalAmountStrLang = @bordTotalAmountStrLang
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @bordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
				IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
				BEGIN
					SET @bordTotalAmountStrLang = @bordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
				END							
				SET @bordTotalAmountStrLang = REPLACE(@bordTotalAmountStrLang,'-',' ')
			END
			/**/
			DECLARE @PreOrdIdLang INT
			SET @PreOrdIdLang = (SELECT TOP(1) [WSBuyOrderLangID] FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder_Lang] WHERE BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderId AND LanguageID = @LangId)
			IF(@PreOrdIdLang IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				UPDATE [dbo].[WSBuyOrder_Lang]
					SET [bordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr] = @bordTotalAmountStrLang
				WHERE [WSBuyOrderLangID] = @PreOrdIdLang						
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSBuyOrder_Lang]
					([BuyOrderID]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[bordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr])
				VALUES
					(@BuyOrderId
					,@LangId
					,@bordTotalAmountStrLang)
			END
			/*-------------------------------------------*/
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
		END
	CLOSE Crsr
	DEALLOCATE Crsr	
END
/*-----------------------------------*/
/*------------ Update Items Amount in Currency -----------*/
EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateBuyOrderItemAmountsCurrency]
@BuyOrderId = @BuyOrderId,
@BuyOrderItemIds = ''
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts]




 

 /*RZ980416*/
/*RZ14020324- Change sp Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/ 
/*RZ14020807- Change status*/
/*RZ14020823- Add condition for update ordStatus*/
/*RZ14021030- Re-Calculate items tax*/
/*RZ14030126- Add calculate OrderOtherAmounts*/
/*RZ14030301- Clac and update Amount in currencyI*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts]
/* 	
	exec ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts 40784,3,1065,1065	
*/
(
@OrderId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
/*----------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14021030- Re-Calculate items tax*/
DECLARE @CalcItemTaxResult INT = 0
EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderItemsTax]
@OrderId = @OrderId,
@Month1 = @Month1,
@Result = @CalcItemTaxResult OUTPUT	
/*RZ14021030- END*/
/*----------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14020324- Change sp Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
DECLARE @cuUnitIsDecimal BIT
SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = (SELECT ordCurrencyUnitId 
												  FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] 
												  WHERE OrderID = @OrderId))
/*RZ14020324- End*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*Calculate & Update the order*/	
/**/
DECLARE @SumPriceAmount NUMERIC(18,2)
/*RZ14020324- Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
DECLARE @SumQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@TotalDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TotalTaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
/*RZ14020324- End*/

SELECT  /*@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(/*Round(*/
								CAST(SUM(
									(ISNULL(ordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL( ordIFCR, 1)* ISNULL( ordIFCRI, 1)) *
									(ISNULL(ordIPrice, 0) - ( ISNULL(ordIDiscount, 0) + ISNULL(ordIDiscountI, 0) + ISNULL(ordIRound, 0)) 
									+ ISNULL(ordIRoundPlus, 0) )		
									+ ISNULL(ordITax, 0) * ISNULL(ordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL( ordIFCR, 1)* ISNULL( ordIFCRI, 1)		
								) AS NUMERIC(18,2))
								/*, 0)*/ ,0)*/
		/*RZ14020324- Change sp Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
		@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(CAST(SUM( ISNULL([ordIPriceSum],0) + ISNULL([ordITaxSum],0)) AS NUMERIC(18,2)),0)
		,@SumQuantity = SUM(ordIQuantity)
		,@TotalDiscountSum = SUM(ordIDiscountSum)
		,@TotalTaxSum = SUM(ordITaxSum)
		/*RZ14020324- End*/	
FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
WHERE OrderId = @OrderID
AND ISNULL(ordINotInSum, 0) = 0
IF(@SumPriceAmount IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @ordItemCount INT, @ordItemsAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@ordInstallAmount NUMERIC(18,2), @ordDiscount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@ordSumAmount NUMERIC(18,2), @ordTax NUMERIC(18,2), @ordTaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2),
	@ordCalculateTax BIT,@ordTotalAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@ordTransportation NUMERIC(18,2),
	@ordDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2),@ordRound NUMERIC(18,2),@ordStatusId INT,
	@ordTotalAmountStr NVARCHAR(1000)
	/**/
	SELECT 
       @ordInstallAmount =[ordInstallAmount],
       @ordDiscount = [ordDiscount],
       @ordTaxIndex = [ordTaxIndex],
       @ordCalculateTax = [ordCalculateTax],
       @ordDiscountPercent = [ordDiscountPercent],
       @ordTransportation = [ordTransportation],
       @ordRound = [ordRound],
	   @ordStatusId= [ordStatusId] 
	FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O
	WHERE O.[OrderID] = @OrderId
	/**/
	SET @ordItemsAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0)
	IF(@ordDiscountPercent IS NOT NULL AND @ordDiscountPercent > 0)
	BEGIN
		SET @ordDiscount = (ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) * ISNULL(@ordDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
	END
	/**/
	SET @ordSumAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) + ISNULL(@ordInstallAmount,0) - ISNULL(@ordDiscount,0)
	/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@ordCalculateTax,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
		SET @ordTax = ISNULL(@ordSumAmount,0) * ISNULL(@ordTaxIndex,0) / 100
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @ordTax = 0
	END
	/**/
	SET @ordTotalAmount = ISNULL(ISNULL(@ordSumAmount,0) + ISNULL(@ordTax,0) + ISNULL(@ordTransportation,0) - ISNULL(@ordRound,0),0)
	/**/
	SET @ordItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @OrderId)
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	/*RZ14020324- Change sp Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
	IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
	BEGIN							
		/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/ 
		SET @ordItemsAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordItemsAmount))
		SET @ordSumAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordSumAmount))		
		SET @ordTax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordTax))		
		SET @ordTotalAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordTotalAmount))		
		SET @ordDiscount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordDiscount))		
		SET @TotalDiscountSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)))
		SET @TotalTaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)))
		/*RZ14020626-END*/ 
	END
	/*RZ14020324- End*/
	/*************** TotalAmountStr *******************/
	DECLARE @TotalAmoutI NVARCHAR(MAX),@TotalAmoutII NVARCHAR(MAX)
	IF(@ordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN			
		SET @TotalAmoutI = SUBSTRING(CAST(@ordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), 1, CHARINDEX('.',CAST(@ordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))-1)                                    
		SET @TotalAmoutII = SUBSTRING(CAST(@ordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), CHARINDEX('.', CAST(@ordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))+1, 2)
		/**/
		IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1065)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @ordTotalAmountStr = @ordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = @ordTotalAmountStr /*+ N' ����' */
		END
		ELSE IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1025)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @ordTotalAmountStr = @ordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = @ordTotalAmountStr
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @ordTotalAmountStr = @ordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
			END
			SET @ordTotalAmountStr = REPLACE(@ordTotalAmountStr,'-',' ') 
		END
	END
	/**************************************************/
	/*Update*/
	UPDATE [dbo].[WSOrder]
	SET  [ordItemCount] = ISNULL(@ordItemCount,0)
		,[ordItemsAmount] = ISNULL(@ordItemsAmount,0)
		,[ordSumAmount] = ISNULL(@ordSumAmount,0)
		,[ordTax] = ISNULL(@ordTax,0)
		,[ordTotalAmount] = @ordTotalAmount
		,[ordTotalAmountStr] = @ordTotalAmountStr
		,[ordDiscount] = ISNULL(@ordDiscount,0)
		,[Month1] = @Month1
		/*RZ14020324- Change sp Add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
		,[ordItemsQty] = ISNULL(@SumQuantity,0)
		,[ordItemsDiscount] = ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)
		,[ordItemsTax] = ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)
		/*RZ14020324- End*/
	WHERE OrderID = @OrderId
	/**/
	IF(@ordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @LangId INT
		/**/
		DECLARE Crsr CURSOR FOR
			SELECT LanguageID
			FROM [dbo].[Languages]		
			WHERE LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			AND ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1
		/**/
		OPEN Crsr
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			While @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN	 
				DECLARE @ordTotalAmountStrLang NVARCHAR(1000)	
				/*-------------------------------------------*/				
				IF(@LangId = 1065)
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang /*+ N' ����'*/
				END
				ELSE IF(@LangId = 1025)
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
					END							
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = REPLACE(@ordTotalAmountStrLang,'-',' ')
				END
				/**/
				DECLARE @OrdIdLang INT
				SET @OrdIdLang = (SELECT [WSOrderLangID] FROM [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang] WHERE OrderID = @OrderId AND LanguageID = @LangId)
				IF(@OrdIdLang IS NOT NULL)
				BEGIN
					UPDATE [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang]
						SET [ordTotalAmountStr] = @ordTotalAmountStrLang
					WHERE [WSOrderLangID] = @OrdIdLang						
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang]
						([OrderID]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[ordTotalAmountStr])
					VALUES
						(@OrderId
						,@LangId
						,@ordTotalAmountStrLang)
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			END
		CLOSE Crsr
		DEALLOCATE Crsr	
	END							
	/*==================================================*/
	/*================= Update status ==================*/	
	DECLARE @SumOrdPayAmount NUMERIC(18,2)
	SET @SumOrdPayAmount = ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL( SUM(ordpayAmount), 0) sumordpayAmount
									FROM dbo.WSOrderPayments
									WHERE ordpayOrderId = @OrderId
									AND ISNULL(ordpayDeActive,0) = 0/*RZ14020823- Add condition for update ordStatus*/),0)
	/*IF(@SumOrdPayAmount IS NOT NULL AND (@ordStatusId <> 4 AND @ordStatusId <> 5))*/
	IF(ISNULL(@SumOrdPayAmount,0) <> 0 AND (@ordStatusId <> 4 AND @ordStatusId <> 5))
	BEGIN
	/**/
		IF(@SumOrdPayAmount = 0 AND @ordStatusId <>1)
			SET @ordStatusId = 1
		/**/
		ELSE IF(@SumOrdPayAmount < @ordTotalAmount)/*RZ14020807- Change status - <>*/
			SET @ordStatusId = 2
		/**/
		ELSE IF(@SumOrdPayAmount >= @ordTotalAmount)/*RZ14020807- Change status - =*/
			SET @ordStatusId = 3
		/**/	 		
		/**/
		UPDATE [dbo].[WSOrder]
		SET [ordStatusId] = @ordStatusId 
		WHERE [OrderID] = @OrderId
	/**/
	END
	/*==================================================*/
END	
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14030126- Add calculate OrderOtherAmounts*/
DECLARE @ResultCalcOrderOtherAmounts  INT = 0
EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderOtherAmounts]
@OrderId = @OrderId,
@Month1 = @Month1,
@CurrentDate = NULL,
@Result = @ResultCalcOrderOtherAmounts OUTPUT	
/*RZ14030126- End*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14030301- Clac and update Amount in currencyI*/
EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderAmountsCurrency]	
@OrderId = @OrderId,
@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID
/*RZ14030301- End*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END









GO
-- [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderAmountsCurrency]






/*RZ14030301*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderAmountsCurrency]
/* 	
	exec ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderAmountsCurrency 43109,1033,1065	
*/
(
@OrderId INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @ordItemsAmount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@ordInstallAmount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@ordDiscount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@ordSumAmount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@ordTax NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@ordTotalAmount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,		
@ordTransportation NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@ordRound NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,	
@ordCurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,@ordItemsDiscount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@ordItemsTax NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@ordGrossProfit NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@ordMoneySleep NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@ordSaleNetProfit NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@ordSumOtherComm NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@ordSumSaleComm NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@ordSumBuyComm NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@ordSumMangComm NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@ordNetProfit NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@ordCurrencyUnitIdI INT = NULL,
@ordCurrencyUnitIRate FLOAT = NULL,@ordItemsAmountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@ordInstallAmountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@ordDiscountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@ordSumAmountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@ordTaxCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@ordTotalAmountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@ordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@ordTransportationCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@ordRoundCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@ordItemsDiscountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@ordItemsTaxCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@ordGrossProfitCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@ordMoneySleepCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@ordSaleNetProfitCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@ordSumOtherCommCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@ordSumSaleCommCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@ordSumBuyCommCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@ordSumMangCommCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@ordNetProfitCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@CurrencyUnitIsDecimal BIT = NULL,@IsDivisionCurrencyRate BIT = NULL,@RialCurrencyUnitId INT = NULL
/*-------------------------------------*/
SELECT TOP (1) 	
	 @ordItemsAmount = [ordItemsAmount]
	,@ordInstallAmount = [ordInstallAmount]
	,@ordDiscount = [ordDiscount]
	,@ordSumAmount = [ordSumAmount]
	,@ordTax = [ordTax]	
	,@ordTotalAmount = [ordTotalAmount]		
	,@ordTransportation = [ordTransportation]
	,@ordRound = [ordRound]
	,@ordCurrencyUnitId = [ordCurrencyUnitId]
	,@ordItemsDiscount = [ordItemsDiscount]
	,@ordItemsTax = [ordItemsTax]
	,@ordGrossProfit = [ordGrossProfit]
	,@ordMoneySleep = [ordMoneySleep]
	,@ordSaleNetProfit = [ordSaleNetProfit]
	,@ordSumOtherComm = [ordSumOtherComm]
	,@ordSumSaleComm = [ordSumSaleComm]
	,@ordSumBuyComm = [ordSumBuyComm]
	,@ordSumMangComm = [ordSumMangComm]
	,@ordNetProfit = [ordNetProfit]
	,@ordCurrencyUnitIdI = [ordCurrencyUnitIdI]
	,@ordCurrencyUnitIRate = [ordCurrencyUnitIRate]	
FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O
WHERE [OrderID] = @OrderId
/*-----------------------------------*/
SET @CurrencyUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
							  FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
							  WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = @ordCurrencyUnitIdI)
SET @RialCurrencyUnitId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyUnitID]            
						   FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits]
						   WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST([CurrencyUnit] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
								  NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
								= (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(N'����' AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
								   NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
							)

IF(ISNULL(@DefaultLanguageID,0) = 0)
BEGIN
	SET @DefaultLanguageID = (SELECT TOP(1) [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])
END
/*-----------------------------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitId,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIdI,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIRate,0) <> 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitId,0) <> ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIdI,0))
	BEGIN
	/**/	
		/*--------------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@RialCurrencyUnitId,0) = ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitId,0))
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*Change rate when main currency unit is rial*/
			SET @ordCurrencyUnitIRate = CAST((1.0/ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIRate,1)) AS FLOAT)
		/**/
		END		
		/*--------------------------------------------------*/			
		IF(ISNULL(@ordItemsAmount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordItemsAmountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@ordItemsAmount,0) * ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordInstallAmount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordInstallAmountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@ordInstallAmount,0) * ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordDiscount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordDiscountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@ordDiscount,0) * ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordSumAmount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordSumAmountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@ordSumAmount,0) * ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordTax,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordTaxCurrencyI = ISNULL(@ordTax,0) * ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordTotalAmount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordTotalAmountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@ordTotalAmount,0) * ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordTransportation,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordTransportationCurrencyI = ISNULL(@ordTransportation,0) * ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordRound,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordRoundCurrencyI = ISNULL(@ordRound,0) * ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordItemsDiscount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordItemsDiscountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@ordItemsDiscount,0) * ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordItemsTax,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordItemsTaxCurrencyI = ISNULL(@ordItemsTax,0) * ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordGrossProfit,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordGrossProfitCurrencyI = ISNULL(@ordGrossProfit,0) * ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordMoneySleep,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordMoneySleepCurrencyI = ISNULL(@ordMoneySleep,0) * ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordSaleNetProfit,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordSaleNetProfitCurrencyI = ISNULL(@ordSaleNetProfit,0) * ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordSumOtherComm,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordSumOtherCommCurrencyI = ISNULL(@ordSumOtherComm,0) * ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordSumSaleComm,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordSumSaleCommCurrencyI = ISNULL(@ordSumSaleComm,0) * ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordSumBuyComm,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordSumBuyCommCurrencyI = ISNULL(@ordSumBuyComm,0) * ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordSumMangComm,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordSumMangCommCurrencyI = ISNULL(@ordSumMangComm,0) * ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordNetProfit,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordNetProfitCurrencyI = ISNULL(@ordNetProfit,0) * ISNULL(@ordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/		
		/*--------------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@CurrencyUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
		BEGIN	
		/**/
			SET @ordItemsAmountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordItemsAmountCurrencyI))	
			SET @ordInstallAmountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordInstallAmountCurrencyI))	
			SET @ordDiscountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordDiscountCurrencyI))	
			SET @ordSumAmountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordSumAmountCurrencyI))	
			SET @ordTaxCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordTaxCurrencyI))	
			SET @ordTotalAmountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordTotalAmountCurrencyI))	
			SET @ordTransportationCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordTransportationCurrencyI))	
			SET @ordRoundCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordRoundCurrencyI))	
			SET @ordItemsDiscountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordItemsDiscountCurrencyI))	
			SET @ordItemsTaxCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordItemsTaxCurrencyI))	
			SET @ordGrossProfitCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordGrossProfitCurrencyI))	
			SET @ordMoneySleepCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordMoneySleepCurrencyI))
			SET @ordSaleNetProfitCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordSaleNetProfitCurrencyI))	
			SET @ordSumOtherCommCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordSumOtherCommCurrencyI))	
			SET @ordSumSaleCommCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordSumSaleCommCurrencyI))	
			SET @ordSumBuyCommCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordSumBuyCommCurrencyI))	
			SET @ordSumMangCommCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordSumMangCommCurrencyI))	
			SET @ordNetProfitCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordNetProfitCurrencyI))			
		/**/
		END
		/*--------------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END
/**/
END
/*-----------------------------------*/
/*************** TotalAmountStr *******************/
DECLARE @TotalAmoutI NVARCHAR(MAX),@TotalAmoutII NVARCHAR(MAX)
IF(@ordTotalAmountCurrencyI IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN			
	SET @TotalAmoutI = SUBSTRING(CAST(@ordTotalAmountCurrencyI AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), 1, CHARINDEX('.',CAST(@ordTotalAmountCurrencyI AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))-1)                                    
	SET @TotalAmoutII = SUBSTRING(CAST(@ordTotalAmountCurrencyI AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), CHARINDEX('.', CAST(@ordTotalAmountCurrencyI AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))+1, 2)
	/**/
	IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1065)
	BEGIN
		SET @ordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
		IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
		BEGIN
			SET @ordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = @ordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
		END
		SET @ordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = @ordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr 
	END
	ELSE IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1025)
	BEGIN
		SET @ordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
		IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
		BEGIN
			SET @ordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = @ordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
		END
		SET @ordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = @ordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @ordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
		IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
		BEGIN
			SET @ordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = @ordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
		END
		SET @ordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = REPLACE(@ordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr,'-',' ') 
	END
END
/*-----------------------------------*/
UPDATE [dbo].[WSOrder]
SET  [ordItemsAmountCurrencyI] = @ordItemsAmountCurrencyI
    ,[ordInstallAmountCurrencyI] = @ordInstallAmountCurrencyI
    ,[ordDiscountCurrencyI] = @ordDiscountCurrencyI
    ,[ordSumAmountCurrencyI] = @ordSumAmountCurrencyI
    ,[ordTaxCurrencyI] = @ordTaxCurrencyI
    ,[ordTotalAmountCurrencyI] = @ordTotalAmountCurrencyI
    ,[ordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr] = @ordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr
    ,[ordTransportationCurrencyI] = @ordTransportationCurrencyI
    ,[ordRoundCurrencyI] = @ordRoundCurrencyI
    ,[ordItemsDiscountCurrencyI] = @ordItemsDiscountCurrencyI
    ,[ordItemsTaxCurrencyI] = @ordItemsTaxCurrencyI
    ,[ordGrossProfitCurrencyI] = @ordGrossProfitCurrencyI
    ,[ordMoneySleepCurrencyI] = @ordMoneySleepCurrencyI
    ,[ordSaleNetProfitCurrencyI] = @ordSaleNetProfitCurrencyI
    ,[ordSumOtherCommCurrencyI] = @ordSumOtherCommCurrencyI
    ,[ordSumSaleCommCurrencyI] = @ordSumSaleCommCurrencyI
    ,[ordSumBuyCommCurrencyI] = @ordSumBuyCommCurrencyI
    ,[ordSumMangCommCurrencyI] = @ordSumMangCommCurrencyI
    ,[ordNetProfitCurrencyI] = @ordNetProfitCurrencyI
WHERE [OrderID] = @OrderId
/*-----------------------------------*/
/*************** TotalAmountStr in other language *******************/
IF(@ordTotalAmountCurrencyI IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @LangId INT
	/**/
	DECLARE Crsr CURSOR LOCAL FOR
		SELECT LanguageID
		FROM [dbo].[Languages]		
		WHERE LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
		AND ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1
	/**/
	OPEN Crsr
	FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
		While @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN	 
			DECLARE @ordTotalAmountStrLang NVARCHAR(1000)	
			/*-------------------------------------------*/				
			IF(@LangId = 1065)
			BEGIN
				SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
				IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
				END
				SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang /*+ N' ����'*/
			END
			ELSE IF(@LangId = 1025)
			BEGIN
				SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
				IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
				END
				SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
				IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
				BEGIN
					SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = @ordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
				END							
				SET @ordTotalAmountStrLang = REPLACE(@ordTotalAmountStrLang,'-',' ')
			END
			/**/
			DECLARE @PreOrdIdLang INT
			SET @PreOrdIdLang = (SELECT TOP(1) [WSOrderLangID] FROM [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang] WHERE OrderID = @OrderId AND LanguageID = @LangId)
			IF(@PreOrdIdLang IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				UPDATE [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang]
					SET [ordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr] = @ordTotalAmountStrLang
				WHERE [WSOrderLangID] = @PreOrdIdLang						
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang]
					([OrderID]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[ordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr])
				VALUES
					(@OrderId
					,@LangId
					,@ordTotalAmountStrLang)
			END
			/*-------------------------------------------*/
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
		END
	CLOSE Crsr
	DEALLOCATE Crsr	
END
/*-----------------------------------*/
/*------------ Update Items Amount in Currency -----------*/
EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderItemAmountsCurrency]
@OrderId = @OrderId,
@OrderItemIds = ''
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END







GO
-- [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderItemsTax]





/*RZ14021030*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderItemsTax]
/* 	
	declare @Result INT = 0
	exec ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderItemsTax 	
	@OrderId = 42842,
	@Month1 = 10,
	@Result = @Result OUTPUT
	select @Result 
*/
(
@OrderId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@Result INT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*--------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*------------------------ Define variables --------------------*/
DECLARE @TspPrdSendMethod INT = NULL
,@TspSettingId INT = NULL,@TspIdentify NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,@TspPrivateKey NVARCHAR(20) = NULL
,@cuUnitIsDecimal BIT = NULL


/*------------------------ Set variables ----------------------*/
SET @TspPrdSendMethod = (SELECT TOP(1) [TspPrdSendMethod] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])

SELECT TOP (1) 
	 @TspSettingId = [TspSettingId]
	,@TspIdentify = [TspIdentify]
    ,@TspPrivateKey = [TspPrivateKey]      
FROM [dbo].[TspSetting]

SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = (SELECT ordCurrencyUnitId 
												  FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] 
												  WHERE OrderID = @OrderId))
/*-------------------------------------------------------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@TspSettingId,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@TspIdentify,'') <> '' AND ISNULL(@TspPrivateKey,'') <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	/*-----------------------------------------------------------*/
	/*----------------------- Declare table temp ----------------*/
	DECLARE @Tmp_OrdItemSumTax TABLE(		
		[ordITaxSum] NUMERIC(18,2),
		[TspGroupIndentify] NVARCHAR(100)
	)
	DECLARE @Tmp_OrdItems TABLE(		
		 [OrderItemID] INT	
		,[ordIPrdId] INT				
		,[ordITaxIndex] NUMERIC(18,2)			   
		,[ordIPriceSum] NUMERIC(18,2)	        
        ,[ordITaxSum] NUMERIC(18,2)	
		,[TspPrdIndentify] NVARCHAR(100)
		,[TspFGIndentify] NVARCHAR(100)
		,[TspSGIndentify] NVARCHAR(100)		
	)
	/*------------------- Fill table temp -----------------------*/
	INSERT INTO @Tmp_OrdItems([OrderItemID],[ordIPrdId],[ordITaxIndex],[ordIPriceSum],[ordITaxSum],[TspPrdIndentify],[TspFGIndentify],[TspSGIndentify])
	SELECT 
		 [OrderItemID]
		,[ordIPrdId]
		,[ordITaxIndex]		
		,[ordIPriceSum]
        ,[ordITaxSum]
		,(SELECT TOP(1) [TspPrdIndentify] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P WHERE P.ProductID = OI.ordIPrdId) [TspPrdIndentify]
		,(SELECT [TspFGIndentify] FROM [dbo].[WSProductFirstGroup] WHERE PrdFirstGroupId = 
			(SELECT FirstGroupId FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] WHERE PrdSecondGroupId = 
				(SELECT PrdGroupId FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] p WHERE ProductID = ordIPrdId))) [TspFGIndentify]
		,(SELECT [TspSGIndentify] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] WHERE PrdSecondGroupId = 
			(SELECT PrdGroupId FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] p WHERE ProductID = ordIPrdId)) [TspSGIndentify]		
	FROM dbo.WSOrderItems OI
	WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
	AND ISNULL(ordINotInSum,0) = 0
	AND ISNULL([ordITaxSum],0) > 0
	ORDER BY ordIOrderView
	/*-----------------------------------------------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL(@TspPrdSendMethod,0) = 1)/*Product first group*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		INSERT INTO @Tmp_OrdItemSumTax([ordITaxSum],[TspGroupIndentify])
		SELECT 			 
			 [ordITaxSum]
			,LTRIM(RTRIM([TspFGIndentify])) 
		FROM @Tmp_OrdItems
		WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspFGIndentify])),'') <> ''		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@TspPrdSendMethod,0) = 3)/*Product second group*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		INSERT INTO @Tmp_OrdItemSumTax([ordITaxSum],[TspGroupIndentify])
		SELECT 			 
			 [ordITaxSum]
			,LTRIM(RTRIM([TspSGIndentify])) 
		FROM @Tmp_OrdItems
		WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspSGIndentify])),'') <> ''	
	/**/
	END
	/*-----------------------------------------------------------*/
	BEGIN TRY
	BEGIN TRANSACTION /*CalcOrdITax*/
	/**/
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE @OrderItemID INT = NULL,@TspGroupIndentify NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,@PrdGroupOrdITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@SumOrdITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@DiffOrdITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@SumOrdIPriceSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@SumOrdITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@NewOrdITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@OrdICountInGroup INT = NULL,
		@CalcTxt BIT = NULL
		
		DECLARE Crsr_CalcOrdITax CURSOR LOCAL FOR				
			SELECT DISTINCT
				[TspGroupIndentify]
			FROM @Tmp_OrdItemSumTax
		OPEN Crsr_CalcOrdITax
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_CalcOrdITax INTO @TspGroupIndentify
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
			/*-----------------------------------------------*/
			SET @OrderItemID = NULL
			SET @PrdGroupOrdITaxSum = NULL
			SET @SumOrdITaxSum = NULL
			SET @DiffOrdITaxSum = NULL
			SET @SumOrdIPriceSum = NULL
			SET @SumOrdITaxIndex = NULL
			SET @NewOrdITaxIndex = NULL
			SET @OrdICountInGroup = NULL
			SET @TspGroupIndentify = LTRIM(RTRIM(@TspGroupIndentify))
			SET @CalcTxt = NULL
			/*-----------------------------------------------*/
			SET @PrdGroupOrdITaxSum = ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL([ordITaxSum],0)) 
											  FROM @Tmp_OrdItemSumTax 
											  WHERE LTRIM(RTRIM([TspGroupIndentify])) = @TspGroupIndentify),0)

			IF(ISNULL(@TspPrdSendMethod,0) = 1)/*Product first group*/
			BEGIN
			/**/				
				SELECT 
					@SumOrdIPriceSum = SUM(ISNULL([ordIPriceSum],0)),
					@SumOrdITaxIndex = SUM(ISNULL([ordITaxIndex],0))
				FROM @Tmp_OrdItems
				WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspFGIndentify])),'') = @TspGroupIndentify

				SET @OrdICountInGroup = ISNULL((SELECT COUNT(*)
											    FROM @Tmp_OrdItemSumTax 
											    WHERE LTRIM(RTRIM([TspGroupIndentify])) = @TspGroupIndentify),0)
				
				IF((SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT [ordITaxIndex]) FROM   @Tmp_OrdItems WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspFGIndentify])),'') = @TspGroupIndentify) = 1)
				BEGIN
					SET @CalcTxt = 1
				END
				
				IF(ISNULL(@OrdICountInGroup,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @NewOrdITaxIndex =  ISNULL(@SumOrdITaxIndex,0) / @OrdICountInGroup					
				END
				
				IF(ISNULL(@SumOrdIPriceSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@NewOrdITaxIndex,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @SumOrdITaxSum = (ISNULL(@SumOrdIPriceSum,0) * ISNULL(@NewOrdITaxIndex,0)) / 100
				END				
				SET @OrderItemID = (SELECT TOP(1) [OrderItemID]
									FROM @Tmp_OrdItems
									WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspFGIndentify])),'') = @TspGroupIndentify)
			/**/
			END
			ELSE IF(ISNULL(@TspPrdSendMethod,0) = 3)/*Product second group*/
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SELECT 
					@SumOrdIPriceSum = SUM(ISNULL([ordIPriceSum],0)),
					@SumOrdITaxIndex = SUM(ISNULL([ordITaxIndex],0))
				FROM @Tmp_OrdItems
				WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspSGIndentify])),'') = @TspGroupIndentify

				SET @OrdICountInGroup = ISNULL((SELECT COUNT(*)
											    FROM @Tmp_OrdItemSumTax 
											    WHERE LTRIM(RTRIM([TspGroupIndentify])) = @TspGroupIndentify),0)
				
				IF((SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT [ordITaxIndex]) FROM   @Tmp_OrdItems WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspSGIndentify])),'') = @TspGroupIndentify) = 1)
				BEGIN
					SET @CalcTxt = 1
				END

				IF(ISNULL(@OrdICountInGroup,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @NewOrdITaxIndex =  ISNULL(@SumOrdITaxIndex,0) / @OrdICountInGroup					
				END
				
				IF(ISNULL(@SumOrdIPriceSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@NewOrdITaxIndex,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @SumOrdITaxSum = (ISNULL(@SumOrdIPriceSum,0) * ISNULL(@NewOrdITaxIndex,0)) / 100
				END

				SET @OrderItemID = (SELECT TOP(1) [OrderItemID]
									FROM @Tmp_OrdItems
									WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspSGIndentify])),'') = @TspGroupIndentify)

			/**/
			END
			/**/
			IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @SumOrdITaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@SumOrdITaxSum))									
			END
			/*-----------------------------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@SumOrdITaxSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@PrdGroupOrdITaxSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@OrderItemID,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@CalcTxt,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @DiffOrdITaxSum = ISNULL(@SumOrdITaxSum,0) - ISNULL(@PrdGroupOrdITaxSum,0)
				
				IF(ISNULL(@DiffOrdITaxSum,0) <> 0 )
				BEGIN
				/**/
					/*---------------------------------------------*/
					DECLARE @ordIQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@ordITotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
					@ordIFCR FLOAT = NULL, @ordITax NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
					@ordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL,@ordIPriceSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
					@ordIQuantityTotal FLOAT = NULL,@PrdQtyNoFCR BIT = NULL,@TmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
					/*---------------------------------------------*/
					SELECT TOP (1) 
						 @ordIQuantity = [ordIQuantity]						
						,@ordITotalPrice = [ordITotalPrice]												
						,@ordIFCR = [ordIFCR]
						,@ordITax = [ordITax]
						,@ordITaxIndex = [ordITaxIndex]						
						,@ordIFCRI = [ordIFCRI]						
						,@ordIPriceSum = [ordIPriceSum]						
						,@ordITaxSum = [ordITaxSum]
						,@ordIQuantityTotal = [ordIQuantityTotal]
						,@PrdQtyNoFCR = (SELECT TOP(1) [PrdQtyNoFCR] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] WHERE ProductId = ordIPrdId)	
					FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] 
					WHERE [OrderItemID] = @OrderItemID
					/*---------------------------------------------*/
					SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCRI,1)
					IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1)
					/**/
					END
					IF(ISNULL(@ordIQuantityTotal,0) > 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantityTotal,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCRI,1)
						IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
						BEGIN
						/**/
							SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantityTotal,1)
						/**/
						END
					/**/
					END
					/*------------------------------------*/
					/*----------------- Calculate -----------------*/
					SET @ordITaxSum = ISNULL(@ordITaxSum,0) + ISNULL(@DiffOrdITaxSum,0)					
					IF(ISNULL(@ordITaxSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@ordIPriceSum,0) > 0)
					BEGIN
						SET @ordITaxIndex = (ISNULL(@ordITaxSum,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@ordIPriceSum,0)
						SET @ordITax = (ISNULL(@ordITaxSum,0) / ISNULL(@TmpI,0))
					END
					SET @ordITotalPrice = ISNULL(@ordIPriceSum,0) + ISNULL(@ordITaxSum,0)
					/*--------- Calculate item by currency unit ------*/
					IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
					BEGIN
						SET @ordITaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITaxSum))
						SET @ordITax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITax))									
						SET @ordITotalPrice = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITotalPrice))						
					END
					/*--------------------- Update item --------------*/					
					UPDATE [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
					SET  [ordITotalPrice] = @ordITotalPrice
						,[ordITax] = @ordITax
						,[ordITaxIndex] = @ordITaxIndex
						,[ordITaxSum] = @ordITaxSum			
						,[Month1] = @Month1						
					WHERE OrderItemID = @OrderItemID	
					/*------------------------------------------------*/
				/**/
				END							   
			/**/
			END			
			/*-----------------------------------------------*/
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_CalcOrdITax INTO @TspGroupIndentify
		END
		CLOSE Crsr_CalcOrdITax
		DEALLOCATE Crsr_CalcOrdITax		
		/*-----------------------------------------------------------------*/	
		/*-----------------------------------------------------------------*/		
		COMMIT /*CalcOrdITax*/
		SET @Result = 1
		RETURN @Result
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
			ROLLBACK /*CalcOrdITax*/	
		/**/
		SET @Result = -1
		CLOSE Crsr_CalcOrdITax
		DEALLOCATE Crsr_CalcOrdITax
		/**/		
	END CATCH	   
	/*-----------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END
/*--------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
/**/
END

















GO
-- [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderOtherAmounts]




/*RZ14030126*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderOtherAmounts]
/* 	
	declare @Result INT = 0
	exec ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderOtherAmounts 
	@OrderId = 42962,
	@Month1 = 1,
	@CurrentDate = NULL,
	@Result = @Result OUTPUT
	SELECT @Result
*/
(
@OrderId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@CurrentDate DATETIME = NULL,
@Result INT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*----------------- Define transaction -----------------*/
BEGIN TRANSACTION CalcAndUpdateOrdOtherAmount
/*------------------------------------------------*/	
BEGIN TRY
/**/
	/*===========================================================================*/
	IF(@CurrentDate IS NULL)
	BEGIN
		SET @CurrentDate = GETDATE()
	END
	/*----------- Get setting -------------*/
	DECLARE @OrderSetting NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,@ordGrossProfitFormula NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ordMoneySleepFormula NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,@ordSaleNetProfitFormula NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@ordNetProfitFormula NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL


	SELECT TOP (1)
		@OrderSetting = [OrderSetting]
	FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting]

	IF( ISNULL(@OrderSetting, '') = '')
	BEGIN
		SET @OrderSetting = '[]'
	END
	IF (ISNULL(@OrderSetting,'[]') <> '[]' AND ISNULL(@OrderSetting, '') <> '' AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM OPENJSON(ISNULL(@OrderSetting,'[]'))) > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT DISTINCT TOP(1) 
			 @ordGrossProfitFormula = [ordGrossProfitFormula]
			,@ordMoneySleepFormula = [ordMoneySleepFormula]
			,@ordSaleNetProfitFormula = [ordSaleNetProfitFormula]
			,@ordNetProfitFormula =[ordNetProfitFormula] 
		FROM OPENJSON (ISNULL(@OrderSetting,'[]'))
		WITH(
			[ordGrossProfitFormula] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.ordGrossProfitFormula',
			[ordMoneySleepFormula] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.ordMoneySleepFormula',
			[ordSaleNetProfitFormula] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.ordSaleNetProfitFormula',
			[ordNetProfitFormula] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.ordNetProfitFormula'
		)JS
	/**/
	END
	/*--------------- Get Order Info ----------------------*/
	DECLARE @ordTotalAmount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordStatusId INT = NULL,@ordDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
	@ordCurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,@BuyOrderIds NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,@ordGrossProfit NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ordMoneySleep NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordSaleNetProfit NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordSumOtherComm NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ordSumSaleComm NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordSumBuyComm NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordSumMangComm NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
	@ordNetProfit NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@cuUnitIsDecimal BIT = NULL,@BuyOrderItemIds NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
	@bordTotalAmount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
	
	SELECT TOP(1)
		 @ordTotalAmount = [ordTotalAmount]
		,@ordStatusId = [ordStatusId]
		,@ordDateTime = [ordDateTime]
		,@ordCurrencyUnitId = [ordCurrencyUnitId]
		,@BuyOrderIds = [BuyOrderIds]
		,@ordGrossProfit = [ordGrossProfit]
		,@ordMoneySleep = [ordMoneySleep]
		,@ordSaleNetProfit = [ordSaleNetProfit]
		,@ordSumOtherComm = [ordSumOtherComm]
		,@ordSumSaleComm = [ordSumSaleComm]
		,@ordSumBuyComm = [ordSumBuyComm]
		,@ordSumMangComm = [ordSumMangComm]
		,@ordNetProfit = [ordNetProfit]
	FROM [dbo].[WSOrder]
	WHERE OrderID = @OrderId
	/*-------- Calculate some amount --------------*/
	SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
							FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
							WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = @ordCurrencyUnitId)
	/*--------- Commission START --------*/	
	SET @ordSumSaleComm = (SELECT SUM(ISNULL([CommAmount],0))       
							FROM [dbo].[WSOrderCommissions]
							WHERE [CommOrderId] = @OrderId
							AND [CommKindId] = 1)
	/**/
	SET @ordSumBuyComm = (SELECT SUM(ISNULL([CommAmount],0))       
							FROM [dbo].[WSOrderCommissions]
							WHERE [CommOrderId] = @OrderId
							AND [CommKindId] = 2)
	/**/
	SET @ordSumMangComm = (SELECT SUM(ISNULL([CommAmount],0))       
							FROM [dbo].[WSOrderCommissions]
							WHERE [CommOrderId] = @OrderId
							AND [CommKindId] = 3)
	/**/
	SET @ordSumOtherComm = (SELECT SUM(ISNULL([CommAmount],0))       
							FROM [dbo].[WSOrderCommissions]
							WHERE [CommOrderId] = @OrderId
							AND [CommKindId] = 4)
	/*--------- Commission END ----------*/	
	/*--------- BuyOrder STRAT ----------*/
	SET @BuyOrderItemIds = ((STUFF((SELECT ',' + ISNULL(BuyOrderItemIds, '')  		
							 FROM  [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI
							 WHERE OrderId = @OrderId
							 AND ISNULL(BuyOrderItemIds,'') <> '' 
							 FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, '')))

	DECLARE @TmpOrdItem TABLE(
		[OrderItemID] INT,
		[ordIPrdId] INT,
		[ordIQuantity] FLOAT,
		[ordITotalPrice] NUMERIC(18,2),
		[ordIFCR] FLOAT,
		[ordIFCRI] FLOAT,
		[ordIQuantityTotal] FLOAT,
		[ordIPriceSum] NUMERIC(18,2),
		[ordIDiscountISum] NUMERIC(18,2),
		[ordIDiscountSum] NUMERIC(18,2),
		[ordITaxSum] NUMERIC(18,2),
		[PreOrderItemId] INT,
		[BuyOrderItemId] INT,
		[BuyOrderId] INT,
		[bordITotalCost] NUMERIC(18,2)
	)
	/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@BuyOrderItemIds,'') <>'')/*Issue Order from buyOrder*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		INSERT INTO @TmpOrdItem
			(
			 [OrderItemID]
			,[ordIPrdId]
			,[ordIQuantity] 
			,[ordITotalPrice]
			,[ordIFCR]
			,[ordIFCRI]
			,[ordIQuantityTotal]
			,[ordIPriceSum]
			,[ordIDiscountISum]
			,[ordIDiscountSum]
			,[ordITaxSum]
			,[PreOrderItemId]
			,[BuyOrderItemId]
			)
		SELECT  
			 [OrderItemID]
			,[ordIPrdId]
			,[ordIQuantity] 
			,[ordITotalPrice]
			,[ordIFCR]
			,[ordIFCRI]
			,[ordIQuantityTotal]
			,[ordIPriceSum]
			,[ordIDiscountISum]
			,[ordIDiscountSum]
			,[ordITaxSum]
			,[PreOrderItemId]
			,CAST(ISNULL(bordItems.bordItemId,'0') AS INT)
		FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI
		CROSS APPLY ((SELECT CAST(ISNULL([value],'0') AS INT) bordItemId FROM STRING_SPLIT(OI.[BuyOrderItemIds], ',') L WHERE CAST(ISNULL([value],'0') AS INT) <> 0)) AS bordItems	
		WHERE OrderId = @OrderID
		AND ISNULL(BuyOrderItemIds,'') <> '' 
		AND ISNULL(ordINotInSum, 0) = 0
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@BuyOrderItemIds,'') = '')/*Issue Order from perOrder with stockSupply*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		INSERT INTO @TmpOrdItem
			(
			 [OrderItemID]
			,[ordIPrdId]
			,[ordIQuantity] 
			,[ordITotalPrice]
			,[ordIFCR]
			,[ordIFCRI]
			,[ordIQuantityTotal]
			,[ordIPriceSum]
			,[ordIDiscountISum]
			,[ordIDiscountSum]
			,[ordITaxSum]
			,[PreOrderItemId]
			,[BuyOrderItemId]
			)
		SELECT  
			 [OrderItemID]
			,[ordIPrdId]
			,[ordIQuantity] 
			,[ordITotalPrice]
			,[ordIFCR]
			,[ordIFCRI]
			,[ordIQuantityTotal]
			,[ordIPriceSum]
			,[ordIDiscountISum]
			,[ordIDiscountSum]
			,[ordITaxSum]
			,[PreOrderItemId]
			,CAST(ISNULL(bordItems.bordItemId,'0') AS INT)			
		FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI
		CROSS APPLY ((SELECT CAST(ISNULL([value],'0') AS INT) bordItemId FROM STRING_SPLIT((SELECT TOP(1) [stsupIBuyOrderItemIds] 
																							FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems] SI
																							WHERE SI.stsupIPreOrderItemId = OI.[PreOrderItemId] 
																							AND SI.[stsupIPrdId] = OI.[ordIPrdId]), ',') L 
																		  WHERE CAST(ISNULL([value],'0') AS INT) <> 0)) AS bordItems	
		WHERE OrderId = @OrderID
		AND ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) [stsupIBuyOrderItemIds] 
					FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupplyItems] SI
					WHERE SI.stsupIPreOrderItemId = OI.[PreOrderItemId] 
					AND SI.[stsupIPrdId] = OI.[ordIPrdId]),'') <> ''
		AND ISNULL(ordINotInSum, 0) = 0
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM @TmpOrdItem),0) = 0)/*Issue Order from perOrder with costSheet*/
		BEGIN
		/**/
			INSERT INTO @TmpOrdItem
				(
				 [OrderItemID]
				,[ordIPrdId]
				,[ordIQuantity] 
				,[ordITotalPrice]
				,[ordIFCR]
				,[ordIFCRI]
				,[ordIQuantityTotal]
				,[ordIPriceSum]
				,[ordIDiscountISum]
				,[ordIDiscountSum]
				,[ordITaxSum]
				,[PreOrderItemId]
				,[BuyOrderItemId]
				)
			SELECT  
				 [OrderItemID]
				,[ordIPrdId]
				,[ordIQuantity] 
				,[ordITotalPrice]
				,[ordIFCR]
				,[ordIFCRI]
				,[ordIQuantityTotal]
				,[ordIPriceSum]
				,[ordIDiscountISum]
				,[ordIDiscountSum]
				,[ordITaxSum]
				,[PreOrderItemId]
				,CAST(ISNULL(bordItems.bordItemId,'0') AS INT)			
			FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI
			CROSS APPLY ((SELECT CAST(ISNULL([value],'0') AS INT) bordItemId FROM STRING_SPLIT((STUFF((SELECT ',' + CAST(BuyOrderItemId AS NVARCHAR(50))						
																									   FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] BI
																									   WHERE BI.[bordICostSheetItemId] IN(SELECT [pordICostSheetItemId]  
																																		  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] PPI
																																		  WHERE PPI.[PreOrderItemID] = OI.[PreOrderItemId]) 
																									   AND BI.[bordIPrdId] = OI.[ordIPrdId] 							
																									   FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, '')), ',') L 
																			  WHERE CAST(ISNULL([value],'0') AS INT) <> 0)) AS bordItems	
			WHERE OrderId = @OrderID
			AND ISNULL((STUFF((SELECT ',' + CAST(BuyOrderItemId AS NVARCHAR(50))						
							   FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] BI
							   WHERE BI.[bordICostSheetItemId] IN(SELECT [pordICostSheetItemId]  
																  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] PPI
																  WHERE PPI.[PreOrderItemID] = OI.[PreOrderItemId])  
							   AND BI.[bordIPrdId] = OI.[ordIPrdId] 							
							   FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, '')),'') <> ''
			AND ISNULL(ordINotInSum, 0) = 0
		/**/
		END
	/**/
	END

	/*----------------- Calculate BuyOrderItems ------------------------*/
	DECLARE @CrsrOrderItemId INT = NULL,@CrsrBuyOrderItemId INT = NULL,@CrsrPrdId INT = NULL,
	@CrsrQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@CrsrFCR FLOAT = NULL,@CrsrFCRI FLOAT = NULL,@CrsrQuantityTotal FLOAT = NULL
	DECLARE CrsrCalcBordIAmount CURSOR LOCAL FOR
	/**/				
		SELECT 
			 [OrderItemID]
			,[BuyOrderItemId]
			,[ordIPrdId]
			,[ordIQuantity]
			,[ordIFCR]
			,[ordIFCRI]
			,[ordIQuantityTotal]
		FROM @TmpOrdItem
