		WHERE ISNULL([BuyOrderItemId],0) <> 0
	/**/
	OPEN CrsrCalcBordIAmount
	FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrCalcBordIAmount INTO @CrsrOrderItemId,@CrsrBuyOrderItemId,@CrsrPrdId,
											@CrsrQuantity,@CrsrFCR,@CrsrFCRI,@CrsrQuantityTotal				
	WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE @BuyOrderId INT = NULL,@PrdQtyNoFCR BIT = NULL,
		@bordIQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@bordICost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@bordITotalCost NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordIDiscount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@bordIDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordIFCR FLOAT = NULL,
		@bordITax NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@bordICalculateTax BIT = NULL, @bordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL,
		@bordICostSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordIDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@bordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL, @bordIQuantityTotal FLOAT = NULL,
		@TmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TmpII NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@TmpIII NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TmpPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
		/**/
		SELECT TOP(1)
			 @BuyOrderId = [BuyOrderId]
			,@bordIQuantity = [bordIQuantity]
			,@bordICost = [bordICost]
			,@bordITotalCost = [bordITotalCost]			
			,@bordIDiscount = [bordIDiscount]
			,@bordIDiscountPercent = [bordIDiscountPercent]			
			,@bordIFCR = [bordIFCR]
			,@bordITax = [bordITax]
			,@bordITaxIndex = [bordITaxIndex]
			,@bordICalculateTax = [bordICalculateTax]
			,@bordIFCRI = [bordIFCRI]						
			,@bordICostSum = [bordICostSum]
			,@bordIDiscountSum = [bordIDiscountSum]
			,@bordITaxSum = [bordITaxSum]			
			,@bordIQuantityTotal = [bordIQuantityTotal]		
		FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems]
		WHERE [BuyOrderItemId] = @CrsrBuyOrderItemId
		AND [bordIPrdId] = @CrsrPrdId
		AND ISNULL(bordINotInSum,0) = 0
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@BuyOrderId,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*----------------------------------*/
			SELECT 	TOP(1) 			
				@PrdQtyNoFCR = [PrdQtyNoFCR]
			FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
			WHERE ProductId = @CrsrPrdId
			/*---*/
			/*------------------*/
			/*IF(ISNULL(@CrsrQuantityTotal,0) <> 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @CrsrQuantityTotal = @CrsrQuantity * ISNULL(@CrsrFCR,1)
			END*/
			/*-------------------------------------------*/
			SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@CrsrQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@CrsrFCR,1) * ISNULL(@CrsrFCRI,1)
			/*--------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@CrsrQuantity,1) 					
			/**/
			END
			/*--------------*/
			IF (ISNULL(@CrsrQuantityTotal,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@CrsrQuantityTotal,1) 					
				/**/
				END
				ELSE IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@CrsrQuantityTotal,1) * ISNULL(@CrsrFCRI,1)					
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			END
			/*--------------*/
			/*-------------------------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@bordIDiscountPercent,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@bordICost,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @bordIDiscountSum = ((ISNULL(@bordICost,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0)) * ISNULL(@bordIDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
				SET @bordIDiscount = (ISNULL(@bordICost,0) * ISNULL(@bordIDiscountPercent,0)) / 100					
			/**/
			END				
			ELSE IF(ISNULL(@bordIDiscount,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@bordICost,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/	
				SET @bordIDiscountSum = (ISNULL(@bordIDiscount,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0))
				IF(ISNULL(@bordIDiscountSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@bordICost,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @bordIDiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@bordIDiscountSum,0) * 100) / (ISNULL(@bordICost,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0))
					/*SET @bordIDiscount = (ISNULL(@bordICost,0) * ISNULL(@bordIDiscountPercent,0)) / 100*/
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			END
			/*-------------------------------------------*/			
			SET @TmpPrice = ISNULL(@bordICost,0) - ISNULL(@bordIDiscount,0) 			
			SET @TmpIII = ((ISNULL(@bordICost,0)) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0)) - ISNULL(@bordIDiscountSum,0)	
			/*-------------------------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@bordICalculateTax,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				IF(ISNULL(@TmpIII,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@bordITaxIndex,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @bordITaxSum = ((ISNULL(@TmpIII,0)) * ISNULL(@bordITaxIndex,0) / 100)
					SET @bordITax = (ISNULL(@TmpPrice,0) * ISNULL(@bordITaxIndex,0) / 100)
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			END
			/*-------------------------------------------*/
			SET @bordICostSum = @TmpIII
			SET @bordITotalCost = ISNULL(@bordICostSum,0) + ISNULL(@bordITaxSum,0)
			/*-------------------------------------------*/
			/*-------------------------------------------*/
			/*IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
			BEGIN				
				SET @bordITotalCost = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@bordITotalCost))					
			END*/
			/*-------------------------------------------*/
			/*----- Update tmp table -----*/
			UPDATE @TmpOrdItem 
			SET [BuyOrderId] = @BuyOrderId,
				[bordITotalCost] = @bordITotalCost
			WHERE [OrderItemID] = @CrsrOrderItemId
			AND [ordIPrdId] = @CrsrPrdId
			AND [BuyOrderItemId] = @CrsrBuyOrderItemId
			/*AND [ordIQuantity] = @CrsrQuantity
			AND [ordIFCR] = @CrsrFCR
			AND [ordIFCRI] = @CrsrFCRI
			AND [ordIQuantityTotal] = @CrsrQuantityTotal*/			
			/*----------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END			
		/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
	/**/	
	FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrCalcBordIAmount INTO @CrsrOrderItemId,@CrsrBuyOrderItemId,@CrsrPrdId,
											@CrsrQuantity,@CrsrFCR,@CrsrFCRI,@CrsrQuantityTotal
	END
	CLOSE CrsrCalcBordIAmount
	DEALLOCATE CrsrCalcBordIAmount
	/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
	/*----------------- Calculate BuyOrders -------------------------------*/
	DECLARE @CrsrBuyOrderId INT 
	DECLARE CrsrCalcBordAmount CURSOR LOCAL FOR
	/**/				
		SELECT DISTINCT
			 [BuyOrderId]
		FROM @TmpOrdItem
		WHERE ISNULL([BuyOrderId],0) <> 0
	/**/
	OPEN CrsrCalcBordAmount
	FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrCalcBordAmount INTO @CrsrBuyOrderId
				
	WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE @SumBordPriceAmount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@bordItemCount INT = NULL, @bordItemsAmount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordDiscount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@bordSumAmount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL, @bordTax NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL, @bordTaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@bordCalculateTax BIT = NULL,@bordTotalAmountI NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordTransportation NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@bordDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordRound NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@bordStatusId INT = NULL
		/**/
		SET @SumBordPriceAmount = ISNULL((SELECT  SUM(ISNULL([bordITotalCost],0)) FROM @TmpOrdItem WHERE ISNULL([BuyOrderId],0) = @CrsrBuyOrderId),0)		
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@SumBordPriceAmount,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SELECT 
				@bordDiscount = [bordDiscount],
				@bordTaxIndex = [bordTaxIndex],
				@bordCalculateTax = [bordCalculateTax],
				@bordDiscountPercent = [bordDiscountPercent],
				@bordTransportation = [bordTransportation],
				@bordRound = [bordRound]
			FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrder] BO
			WHERE BO.[BuyOrderID] = @CrsrBuyOrderId
			/**/
			SET @bordItemsAmount = ISNULL(@SumBordPriceAmount,0)
			IF(@bordDiscountPercent IS NOT NULL AND @bordDiscountPercent > 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @bordDiscount = (ISNULL(@SumBordPriceAmount,0) * ISNULL(@bordDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
			END
			/**/
			SET @bordSumAmount = ISNULL(@SumBordPriceAmount,0) - ISNULL(@bordDiscount,0)
			/**/
			IF(ISNULL(@bordCalculateTax,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
				SET @bordTax = ISNULL(@bordSumAmount,0) * ISNULL(@bordTaxIndex,0) / 100
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @bordTax = 0
			END
			/**/
			SET @bordTotalAmountI = ISNULL(ISNULL(@bordSumAmount,0) + ISNULL(@bordTax,0) + ISNULL(@bordTransportation,0) - ISNULL(@bordRound,0),0)			
			SET @bordTotalAmount = ISNULL(@bordTotalAmount,0) + ISNULL(@bordTotalAmountI,0)
		/**/
		END				
		/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
	/**/	
	FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrCalcBordAmount INTO @CrsrBuyOrderId
	END
	CLOSE CrsrCalcBordAmount
	DEALLOCATE CrsrCalcBordAmount

	/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
	/*--------- BuyOrder END ------------*/
	/*--------- OrderPayment START ------------*/	   
	DECLARE @ordpayDocDateTime DATETIME = NULL,@ordpayBankDocDate DATETIME = NULL,@ordpayDocDateTimeTemp DATETIME = NULL,
	@LastOrdpayDocDate DATETIME = NULL,@LastOrdpayDocDayNoSettlement INT = NULL	
	SELECT TOP (1) 
		@ordpayDocDateTime = [ordpayDocDateTime],
		@ordpayBankDocDate = [ordpayBankDocDate],
		@ordpayDocDateTimeTemp = [ordpayDocDateTimeTemp]
	FROM [dbo].[WSOrderPayments]
	WHERE ordpayOrderId = @OrderId
	AND ISNULL([ordpayDeActive],0) = 0
	ORDER By [ordpayDocDateTime]DESC,[OrderPaymentId] DESC
	/**/
	SET @LastOrdpayDocDate = ISNULL(@ordpayBankDocDate,@ordpayDocDateTime)
	IF(ISNULL(@ordStatusId,0) = 3 AND @LastOrdpayDocDate IS NOT NULL)/*����� ���*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @LastOrdpayDocDayNoSettlement = (DATEDIFF(day, CAST(@ordDateTime AS DATE), CAST(@LastOrdpayDocDate AS DATE)))		
		IF(ISNULL(@LastOrdpayDocDayNoSettlement,0) < 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @LastOrdpayDocDayNoSettlement = 0
		END
	/**/
	END
	/*--------- OrderPayment END --------------*/
	/*--------- Calculate Formula START -------------*/
	IF(ISNULL((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM @TmpOrdItem),0) > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*----------------------------------*/
		DECLARE @StrCom NVARCHAR(MAX) = N''					
		/*-----*/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordGrossProfitFormula,'') <> '')
		BEGIN
		/**/			
			SET @ordGrossProfitFormula = REPLACE(@ordGrossProfitFormula, '[ordTotalAmount]', (CAST(ISNULL(@ordTotalAmount,0) AS NVARCHAR(200))))
			SET @ordGrossProfitFormula = REPLACE(@ordGrossProfitFormula, '[bordTotalAmount]', (CAST(ISNULL(@bordTotalAmount,0) AS NVARCHAR(200))))

			BEGIN TRY
			/**/
				/*--------- Calculate formula -------------*/
				SET @StrCom = N''
				SET @StrCom ='SELECT @Result = ' + @ordGrossProfitFormula
				EXECUTE sp_executesql @StrCom, N'@Result NUMERIC(18,2) OUTPUT',@Result = @ordGrossProfit OUTPUT
				/*-----------------------------------------*/				
			/**/
			END TRY
			BEGIN CATCH
				SET @ordGrossProfit = 0							
			END CATCH
		/**/
		END
		/*-----*/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordMoneySleepFormula,'') <> '')
		BEGIN
		/**/			
			SET @ordMoneySleepFormula = REPLACE(@ordMoneySleepFormula, '[bordTotalAmount]', (CAST(ISNULL(@bordTotalAmount,0) AS NVARCHAR(200))))
			SET @ordMoneySleepFormula = REPLACE(@ordMoneySleepFormula, '[LastOrdpayDocDayNoSettlement]', (CAST(ISNULL(@LastOrdpayDocDayNoSettlement,0) AS NVARCHAR(200))))
			BEGIN TRY
			/**/
				/*--------- Calculate formula -------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@LastOrdpayDocDayNoSettlement,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @StrCom = N''
					SET @StrCom ='SELECT @Result = ' + @ordMoneySleepFormula
					EXECUTE sp_executesql @StrCom, N'@Result NUMERIC(18,2) OUTPUT',@Result = @ordMoneySleep OUTPUT
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @ordMoneySleep = 0
				END				
				/*-----------------------------------------*/				
			/**/
			END TRY
			BEGIN CATCH
				SET @ordMoneySleep = 0							
			END CATCH
		/**/
		END
		/*-----*/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordSaleNetProfitFormula,'') <> '')
		BEGIN
		/**/			
			SET @ordSaleNetProfitFormula = REPLACE(@ordSaleNetProfitFormula, '[ordGrossProfit]', (CAST(ISNULL(@ordGrossProfit,0) AS NVARCHAR(200))))
			SET @ordSaleNetProfitFormula = REPLACE(@ordSaleNetProfitFormula, '[ordSumOtherComm]', (CAST(ISNULL(@ordSumOtherComm,0) AS NVARCHAR(200))))
			SET @ordSaleNetProfitFormula = REPLACE(@ordSaleNetProfitFormula, '[ordMoneySleep]', (CAST(ISNULL(@ordMoneySleep,0) AS NVARCHAR(200))))			
			BEGIN TRY
			/**/
				/*--------- Calculate formula -------------*/
				SET @StrCom = N''
				SET @StrCom ='SELECT @Result = ' + @ordSaleNetProfitFormula
				EXECUTE sp_executesql @StrCom, N'@Result NUMERIC(18,2) OUTPUT',@Result = @ordSaleNetProfit OUTPUT
				/*-----------------------------------------*/				
			/**/
			END TRY
			BEGIN CATCH
				SET @ordSaleNetProfit = 0							
			END CATCH
		/**/
		END
		/*-----*/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordNetProfitFormula,'') <> '')
		BEGIN
		/**/			
			SET @ordNetProfitFormula = REPLACE(@ordNetProfitFormula, '[ordSaleNetProfit]', (CAST(ISNULL(@ordSaleNetProfit,0) AS NVARCHAR(200))))
			SET @ordNetProfitFormula = REPLACE(@ordNetProfitFormula, '[ordSumSaleComm]', (CAST(ISNULL(@ordSumSaleComm,0) AS NVARCHAR(200))))
			SET @ordNetProfitFormula = REPLACE(@ordNetProfitFormula, '[ordSumBuyComm]', (CAST(ISNULL(@ordSumBuyComm,0) AS NVARCHAR(200))))
			SET @ordNetProfitFormula = REPLACE(@ordNetProfitFormula, '[ordSumMangComm]', (CAST(ISNULL(@ordSumMangComm,0) AS NVARCHAR(200))))			
			BEGIN TRY
			/**/
				/*--------- Calculate formula -------------*/
				SET @StrCom = N''
				SET @StrCom ='SELECT @Result = ' + @ordNetProfitFormula
				EXECUTE sp_executesql @StrCom, N'@Result NUMERIC(18,2) OUTPUT',@Result = @ordNetProfit OUTPUT
				/*-----------------------------------------*/				
			/**/
			END TRY
			BEGIN CATCH
				SET @ordNetProfit = 0							
			END CATCH
		/**/
		END
		/*-----*/
		/*----------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END
	/*--------- Calculate Formula END -------------*/
	/*---------------------------------------------------------------------*/
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
	BEGIN	
		SET @ordGrossProfit = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordGrossProfit))					
		SET @ordMoneySleep = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordMoneySleep))					
		SET @ordSaleNetProfit = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordSaleNetProfit))					
		SET @ordSumOtherComm = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordSumOtherComm))					
		SET @ordSumSaleComm = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordSumSaleComm))					
		SET @ordSumBuyComm = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordSumBuyComm))					
		SET @ordSumMangComm = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordSumMangComm))					
		SET @ordNetProfit = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordNetProfit))					
	END
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	UPDATE [dbo].[WSOrder]
	SET  [Month1] = @Month1			
		,[ordGrossProfit] = ISNULL(@ordGrossProfit,0)
		,[ordMoneySleep] = ISNULL(@ordMoneySleep,0)
		,[ordSaleNetProfit] = ISNULL(@ordSaleNetProfit,0)
		,[ordSumOtherComm] = ISNULL(@ordSumOtherComm,0)
		,[ordSumSaleComm] = ISNULL(@ordSumSaleComm,0)
		,[ordSumBuyComm] = ISNULL(@ordSumBuyComm,0)
		,[ordSumMangComm] = ISNULL(@ordSumMangComm,0)
		,[ordNetProfit] = ISNULL(@ordNetProfit,0)
	WHERE OrderId = @OrderId	   
	/*------------------------------------------------*/
	COMMIT TRANSACTION CalcAndUpdateOrdOtherAmount
	SET @Result = 1
	RETURN @Result
	/*===========================================================================*/	
/**/
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
/**/
	IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
		ROLLBACK /*CalcAndUpdateOrdOtherAmount*/	
	/*ROLLBACK TRANSACTION CalcAndUpdateOrdOtherAmount*/
	SET @Result = 0
	RETURN @Result
/**/
END CATCH	
/*------------------------------------------------*/

/**/
END











GO
-- [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts]




 



 /*RZ990206*/
/*RZ14020324- Cahnge sp and add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
/*RZ14021030- Re-Calculate items tax*/
/*RZ14030301- Clac and update Amount in currencyI*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts]
/* 	
	exec ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts 3507,3,1033,1065	
*/
(
@PreOrderId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
/*----------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14021030- Re-Calculate items tax*/
DECLARE @CalcItemTaxResult INT = 0
EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderItemsTax]
@PreOrderId = @PreOrderId,
@Month1 = @Month1,
@Result = @CalcItemTaxResult OUTPUT	
/*RZ14021030- END*/
/*----------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14020324- Cahnge sp and add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
DECLARE @cuUnitIsDecimal BIT
SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = (SELECT pordCurrencyUnitId 
												  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] 
												  WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId))
/*RZ14020324- End*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*Calculate & Update the order*/	
/**/

DECLARE @SumQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@TotalDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TotalTaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
/*RZ14020324- End*/
DECLARE @SumPriceAmount NUMERIC(18,2)
SELECT 
	/*@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(/*ROUND(*/
							CAST(SUM(
							 (ISNULL(pordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL(pordIFCR, 1) * ISNULL(pordIFCRI, 1)) 
							 * 
							 (( ISNULL(pordIPrice, 0) - ( ISNULL(pordIDiscount, 0) + ISNULL(pordIDiscountI, 0) + ISNULL(pordIRound, 0))
							 /*ADBF960120*/
							 + ISNULL(pordIRoundPlus, 0)
							 ))	  
	 							+ (ISNULL( pordITax, 0) * ISNULL(pordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL(pordIFCR, 1) * ISNULL(pordIFCRI, 1))
							) AS NUMERIC(18,2))
							/*, 0)*/,0)*/
	/*RZ14020324- Cahnge sp and add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
	@SumPriceAmount = ISNULL(CAST(SUM(ISNULL([pordIPriceSum],0) + ISNULL([pordITaxSum],0)) AS NUMERIC(18,2)),0)
	,@SumQuantity = SUM(pordIQuantity)
	,@TotalDiscountSum = SUM(pordIDiscountSum)
	,@TotalTaxSum = SUM(pordITaxSum)
	/*RZ14020324- End*/	
FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems
WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderId
AND ISNULL(pordINotInSum, 0) = 0
/**/
IF(@SumPriceAmount IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @pordItemCount  INT, @pordItemsAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordInstallAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@pordDiscount NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordSumAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@pordTax NUMERIC(18,2),@pordTaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordCalculateTax BIT,@pordTotalAmount NUMERIC(18,2),@pordTransportation NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2),@pordRound NUMERIC(18,2),
	@pordTotalAmountStr NVARCHAR(1000)	
	/**/			
	SELECT 
       @pordInstallAmount =[pordInstallAmount],
       @pordDiscount = [pordDiscount],
       @pordTaxIndex = [pordTaxIndex],
       @pordCalculateTax = [pordCalculateTax],
       @pordDiscountPercent = [pordDiscountPercent],
       @pordTransportation = [pordTransportation],
       @pordRound = [pordRound]      
	FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] PO
	WHERE PO.[PreOrderID] = @PreOrderId
	/**/
	SET @pordItemsAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0)
	IF(@pordDiscountPercent IS NOT NULL AND @pordDiscountPercent > 0)
	BEGIN
		SET @pordDiscount = (ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) * ISNULL(@pordDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
	END
	/**/
	SET @pordSumAmount = ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) + ISNULL(@pordInstallAmount,0) - ISNULL(@pordDiscount,0)
	/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@pordCalculateTax,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
		SET @pordTax = ISNULL(@pordSumAmount,0) * ISNULL(@pordTaxIndex,0) / 100
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @pordTax = 0
	END
	/**/
	SET @pordTotalAmount = ISNULL(ISNULL(@pordSumAmount,0) + ISNULL(@pordTax,0) + ISNULL(@pordTransportation,0) - ISNULL(@pordRound,0),0)
	/**/
	SET @pordItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] POI WHERE POI.PreOrderId = @PreOrderId)
	/*---------------------------------------------------*/
	/*RZ14020324- Cahnge sp and add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
	IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
	BEGIN	
		/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
		SET @pordItemsAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordItemsAmount))					
		SET @pordSumAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordSumAmount))		
		SET @pordTax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordTax))		
		SET @pordTotalAmount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordTotalAmount))		
		SET @pordDiscount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordDiscount))					
		SET @TotalDiscountSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)))	
		SET @TotalTaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)))			
		/*RZ14020626-END*/
	END
	/*RZ14020324- End*/

	


	/*************** ToTalAmountStr *******************/
	DECLARE @TotalAmoutI NVARCHAR(MAX),@TotalAmoutII NVARCHAR(MAX)
	IF(@pordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN			
		SET @TotalAmoutI = SUBSTRING(CAST(@pordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), 1, CHARINDEX('.',CAST(@pordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))-1)                                    
		SET @TotalAmoutII = SUBSTRING(CAST(@pordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), CHARINDEX('.', CAST(@pordTotalAmount AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))+1, 2)
		/**/
		IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1065)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr /*+ N' ����'*/
		END
		ELSE IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1025)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
			END
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
			IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStr = @pordTotalAmountStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
			END
			SET @pordTotalAmountStr = REPLACE(@pordTotalAmountStr,'-',' ') 
		END
	END
	/**************************************************/
	/*Update*/
	UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
	SET  [pordItemCount] = ISNULL(@pordItemCount,0)
		,[pordItemsAmount] = ISNULL(@pordItemsAmount,0)
		,[pordSumAmount] = ISNULL(@pordSumAmount,0)
		,[pordTax] = ISNULL(@pordTax,0)
		,[pordTotalAmount] = @pordTotalAmount
		,[pordTotalAmountStr] = @pordTotalAmountStr
		,[pordDiscount] = ISNULL(@pordDiscount,0)
		,[Month1] = @Month1 
		/*RZ14020324- Cahnge sp and add ItemsQty and ItemsDiscount and ItemsTax*/
		,[pordItemsQty] = ISNULL(@SumQuantity,0)
		,[pordItemsDiscount] = ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0)
		,[pordItemsTax] = ISNULL(@TotalTaxSum,0)
		/*RZ14020324- End*/
	WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId
	/**/
	IF(@pordTotalAmount IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @LangId INT
		/**/
		DECLARE Crsr CURSOR FOR
			SELECT LanguageID
			FROM [dbo].[Languages]		
			WHERE LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			AND ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1
		/**/
		OPEN Crsr
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			While @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN	 
				DECLARE @pordTotalAmountStrLang NVARCHAR(1000)	
				/*-------------------------------------------*/				
				IF(@LangId = 1065)
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang /*+ N' ����'*/
				END
				ELSE IF(@LangId = 1025)
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
					END
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
					IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
					BEGIN
						SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
					END							
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = REPLACE(@pordTotalAmountStrLang,'-',' ')
				END
				/**/
				DECLARE @PreOrdIdLang INT
				SET @PreOrdIdLang = (SELECT [WSPreOrderLangID] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang] WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId AND LanguageID = @LangId)
				IF(@PreOrdIdLang IS NOT NULL)
				BEGIN
					UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang]
						SET [pordTotalAmountStr] = @pordTotalAmountStrLang
					WHERE [WSPreOrderLangID] = @PreOrdIdLang						
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang]
						([PreOrderID]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[pordTotalAmountStr])
					VALUES
						(@PreOrderId
						,@LangId
						,@pordTotalAmountStrLang)
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
			END
		CLOSE Crsr
		DEALLOCATE Crsr	
	END							
	/*==================================================*/			
END	

/*==================================================*/	
/*RZ14030301- Clac and update Amount in currencyI*/
EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmountsCurrency]	
@PreOrderId = @PreOrderId,
@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID
/*RZ14030301- End*/
/*==================================================*/
/**/
END







GO
-- [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmountsCurrency]





/*RZ14030301*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmountsCurrency]
/* 	
	exec ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmountsCurrency 4841,1033,1065	
*/
(
@PreOrderId INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @pordItemsAmount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@pordInstallAmount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
@pordDiscount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@pordSumAmount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@pordTax NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@pordTotalAmount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,		
@pordTransportation NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@pordRound NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,	
@pordCurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,	@pordItemsDiscount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@pordItemsTax NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,	@pordCurrencyUnitIdI INT = NULL,
@pordCurrencyUnitIRate FLOAT = NULL,@pordItemsAmountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@pordInstallAmountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@pordDiscountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@pordSumAmountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@pordTaxCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@pordTotalAmountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@pordTransportationCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@pordRoundCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@pordItemsDiscountCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,@pordItemsTaxCurrencyI NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@CurrencyUnitIsDecimal BIT = NULL,@IsDivisionCurrencyRate BIT = NULL,@RialCurrencyUnitId INT = NULL
/*-------------------------------------*/
SELECT TOP (1)     
     @pordItemsAmount = [pordItemsAmount]
    ,@pordInstallAmount = [pordInstallAmount]
    ,@pordDiscount = [pordDiscount]
    ,@pordSumAmount = [pordSumAmount]
    ,@pordTax = [pordTax]    
    ,@pordTotalAmount = [pordTotalAmount]        
    ,@pordTransportation = [pordTransportation]
    ,@pordRound = [pordRound]    
    ,@pordCurrencyUnitId = [pordCurrencyUnitId]    
    ,@pordItemsDiscount = [pordItemsDiscount]
    ,@pordItemsTax = [pordItemsTax]    
    ,@pordCurrencyUnitIdI = [pordCurrencyUnitIdI]
    ,@pordCurrencyUnitIRate = [pordCurrencyUnitIRate]    
FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] PO
WHERE [PreOrderID] = @PreOrderId
/*-----------------------------------*/
SET @CurrencyUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
							  FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
							  WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = @pordCurrencyUnitIdI)
SET @RialCurrencyUnitId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyUnitID]            
						   FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits]
						   WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST([CurrencyUnit] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
								  NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
								= (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(N'����' AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
								   NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
							)

IF(ISNULL(@DefaultLanguageID,0) = 0)
BEGIN
	SET @DefaultLanguageID = (SELECT TOP(1) [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])
END
/*-----------------------------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitId,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIdI,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0) <> 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitId,0) <> ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIdI,0))
	BEGIN
	/**/	
		/*--------------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@RialCurrencyUnitId,0) = ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitId,0))
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*Change rate when main currency unit is rial*/
			SET @pordCurrencyUnitIRate = CAST((1.0/ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,1)) AS FLOAT)
		/**/
		END		
		/*--------------------------------------------------*/			
		IF(ISNULL(@pordItemsAmount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordItemsAmountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@pordItemsAmount,0) * ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@pordInstallAmount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordInstallAmountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@pordInstallAmount,0) * ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@pordDiscount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordDiscountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@pordDiscount,0) * ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@pordSumAmount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordSumAmountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@pordSumAmount,0) * ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@pordTax,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTaxCurrencyI = ISNULL(@pordTax,0) * ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@pordTotalAmount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@pordTotalAmount,0) * ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@pordTransportation,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTransportationCurrencyI = ISNULL(@pordTransportation,0) * ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@pordRound,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordRoundCurrencyI = ISNULL(@pordRound,0) * ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@pordItemsDiscount,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordItemsDiscountCurrencyI = ISNULL(@pordItemsDiscount,0) * ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@pordItemsTax,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @pordItemsTaxCurrencyI = ISNULL(@pordItemsTax,0) * ISNULL(@pordCurrencyUnitIRate,0)
		END
		/**/
		/*--------------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@CurrencyUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
		BEGIN	
		/**/
			SET @pordItemsAmountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordItemsAmountCurrencyI))					
			SET @pordInstallAmountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordInstallAmountCurrencyI))					
			SET @pordDiscountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordDiscountCurrencyI))					
			SET @pordSumAmountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordSumAmountCurrencyI))					
			SET @pordTaxCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordTaxCurrencyI))					
			SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordTotalAmountCurrencyI))					
			SET @pordTransportationCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordTransportationCurrencyI))					
			SET @pordRoundCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordRoundCurrencyI))	
			SET @pordItemsDiscountCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordItemsDiscountCurrencyI))					
			SET @pordItemsTaxCurrencyI = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordItemsTaxCurrencyI))								
		/**/
		END
		/*--------------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END
/**/
END
/*-----------------------------------*/
/*************** TotalAmountStr *******************/
DECLARE @TotalAmoutI NVARCHAR(MAX),@TotalAmoutII NVARCHAR(MAX)
IF(@pordTotalAmountCurrencyI IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN			
	SET @TotalAmoutI = SUBSTRING(CAST(@pordTotalAmountCurrencyI AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), 1, CHARINDEX('.',CAST(@pordTotalAmountCurrencyI AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))-1)                                    
	SET @TotalAmoutII = SUBSTRING(CAST(@pordTotalAmountCurrencyI AS NVARCHAR(MAX)), CHARINDEX('.', CAST(@pordTotalAmountCurrencyI AS NVARCHAR(MAX)))+1, 2)
	/**/
	IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1065)
	BEGIN
		SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
		IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
		END
		SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr 
	END
	ELSE IF(@DefaultLanguageID = 1025)
	BEGIN
		SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
		IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
		END
		SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
		IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
		BEGIN
			SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
		END
		SET @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr = REPLACE(@pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr,'-',' ') 
	END
END
/*-----------------------------------*/
UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
SET  [pordItemsAmountCurrencyI] = @pordItemsAmountCurrencyI
    ,[pordInstallAmountCurrencyI] = @pordInstallAmountCurrencyI
    ,[pordDiscountCurrencyI] = @pordDiscountCurrencyI
    ,[pordSumAmountCurrencyI] = @pordSumAmountCurrencyI
    ,[pordTaxCurrencyI] = @pordTaxCurrencyI
    ,[pordTotalAmountCurrencyI] = @pordTotalAmountCurrencyI
    ,[pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr] = @pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr
    ,[pordTransportationCurrencyI] = @pordTransportationCurrencyI
    ,[pordRoundCurrencyI] = @pordRoundCurrencyI
    ,[pordItemsDiscountCurrencyI] = @pordItemsDiscountCurrencyI
    ,[pordItemsTaxCurrencyI] = @pordItemsTaxCurrencyI
WHERE [PreOrderID] = @PreOrderId
/*-----------------------------------*/
/*************** TotalAmountStr in other language *******************/
IF(@pordTotalAmountCurrencyI IS NOT NULL)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @LangId INT
	/**/
	DECLARE Crsr CURSOR LOCAL FOR
		SELECT LanguageID
		FROM [dbo].[Languages]		
		WHERE LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
		AND ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1
	/**/
	OPEN Crsr
	FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
		While @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN	 
			DECLARE @pordTotalAmountStrLang NVARCHAR(1000)	
			/*-------------------------------------------*/				
			IF(@LangId = 1065)
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
				IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToFaWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
				END
				SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang /*+ N' ����'*/
			END
			ELSE IF(@LangId = 1025)
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
				IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToArWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS NUMERIC(38,0)))),'')
				END
				SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutI AS BIGINT))),'')
				IF(@TotalAmoutII IS NOT NULL AND CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS INT) > 0 )
				BEGIN
					SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = @pordTotalAmountStrLang + '.' + ISNULL((SELECT [dbo].[fnNumberToEnWords](CAST(@TotalAmoutII AS BIGINT))),'')
				END							
				SET @pordTotalAmountStrLang = REPLACE(@pordTotalAmountStrLang,'-',' ')
			END
			/**/
			DECLARE @PreOrdIdLang INT
			SET @PreOrdIdLang = (SELECT TOP(1) [WSPreOrderLangID] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang] WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId AND LanguageID = @LangId)
			IF(@PreOrdIdLang IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
				UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang]
					SET [pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr] = @pordTotalAmountStrLang
				WHERE [WSPreOrderLangID] = @PreOrdIdLang						
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang]
					([PreOrderID]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[pordTotalAmountCurrencyIStr])
				VALUES
					(@PreOrderId
					,@LangId
					,@pordTotalAmountStrLang)
			END
			/*-------------------------------------------*/
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @LangId
		END
	CLOSE Crsr
	DEALLOCATE Crsr	
END
/*-----------------------------------*/
/*------------ Update Items Amount in Currency -----------*/
EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderItemAmountsCurrency]
@PreOrderId = @PreOrderId,
@PreOrderItemIds = ''
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END









GO
-- [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderItemsTax]







/*RZ14021030*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderItemsTax]
/* 	
	declare @Result INT = 0
	exec ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderItemsTax 	
	@PreOrderId = 42842,
	@Month1 = 10,
	@Result = @Result OUTPUT
	select @Result 
*/
(
@PreOrderId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@Result INT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*--------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*------------------------ Define variables --------------------*/
DECLARE @TspPrdSendMethod INT = NULL
,@TspSettingId INT = NULL,@TspIdentify NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,@TspPrivateKey NVARCHAR(20) = NULL
,@cuUnitIsDecimal BIT = NULL
/*------------------------ Set variables ----------------------*/
SET @TspPrdSendMethod = (SELECT TOP(1) [TspPrdSendMethod] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])

SELECT TOP (1) 
	 @TspSettingId = [TspSettingId]
	,@TspIdentify = [TspIdentify]
    ,@TspPrivateKey = [TspPrivateKey]      
FROM [dbo].[TspSetting]

SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = (SELECT pordCurrencyUnitId 
												  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] 
												  WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId))

/*-------------------------------------------------------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@TspSettingId,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@TspIdentify,'') <> '' AND ISNULL(@TspPrivateKey,'') <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	/*-----------------------------------------------------------*/
	/*----------------------- Declare table temp ----------------*/
	DECLARE @Tmp_PordItemSumTax TABLE(		
		[pordITaxSum] NUMERIC(18,2),
		[TspGroupIndentify] NVARCHAR(100)
	)
	DECLARE @Tmp_PordItems TABLE(		
		 [PreOrderItemID] INT	
		,[pordIPrdId] INT				
		,[pordITaxIndex] NUMERIC(18,2)			   
		,[pordIPriceSum] NUMERIC(18,2)	        
        ,[pordITaxSum] NUMERIC(18,2)	
		,[TspPrdIndentify] NVARCHAR(100)
		,[TspFGIndentify] NVARCHAR(100)
		,[TspSGIndentify] NVARCHAR(100)		
	)
	/*------------------- Fill table temp -----------------------*/
	INSERT INTO @Tmp_PordItems([PreOrderItemID],[pordIPrdId],[pordITaxIndex],[pordIPriceSum],[pordITaxSum],[TspPrdIndentify],[TspFGIndentify],[TspSGIndentify])
	SELECT 
		 [PreOrderItemID]
		,[pordIPrdId]
		,[pordITaxIndex]		
		,[pordIPriceSum]
        ,[pordITaxSum]
		,(SELECT TOP(1) [TspPrdIndentify] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P WHERE P.ProductID = POI.pordIPrdId) [TspPrdIndentify]
		,(SELECT [TspFGIndentify] FROM [dbo].[WSProductFirstGroup] WHERE PrdFirstGroupId = 
			(SELECT FirstGroupId FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] WHERE PrdSecondGroupId = 
				(SELECT PrdGroupId FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] p WHERE ProductID = pordIPrdId))) [TspFGIndentify]
		,(SELECT [TspSGIndentify] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] WHERE PrdSecondGroupId = 
			(SELECT PrdGroupId FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] p WHERE ProductID = pordIPrdId)) [TspSGIndentify]		
	FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems POI
	WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderId
	AND ISNULL(pordINotInSum,0) = 0
	AND ISNULL([pordITaxSum],0) > 0
	ORDER BY pordIOrderView
	/*-----------------------------------------------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL(@TspPrdSendMethod,0) = 1)/*Product first group*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		INSERT INTO @Tmp_PordItemSumTax([pordITaxSum],[TspGroupIndentify])
		SELECT 			 
			 [pordITaxSum]
			,LTRIM(RTRIM([TspFGIndentify])) 
		FROM @Tmp_PordItems
		WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspFGIndentify])),'') <> ''		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@TspPrdSendMethod,0) = 3)/*Product second group*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		INSERT INTO @Tmp_PordItemSumTax([pordITaxSum],[TspGroupIndentify])
		SELECT 			 
			 [pordITaxSum]
			,LTRIM(RTRIM([TspSGIndentify])) 
		FROM @Tmp_PordItems
		WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspSGIndentify])),'') <> ''	
	/**/
	END
	/*-----------------------------------------------------------*/
	BEGIN TRY
	BEGIN TRANSACTION /*CalcPordITax*/
	/**/
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE @PreOrderItemID INT = NULL,@TspGroupIndentify NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,@PrdGroupPordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@SumPordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@DiffPordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@SumPordIPriceSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@SumPordITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@NewPordITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@PordICountInGroup INT = NULL,
		@CalcTxt BIT = NULL
		
		DECLARE Crsr_CalcPordITax CURSOR LOCAL FOR				
			SELECT DISTINCT
				[TspGroupIndentify]
			FROM @Tmp_PordItemSumTax
		OPEN Crsr_CalcPordITax
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_CalcPordITax INTO @TspGroupIndentify
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
			/*-----------------------------------------------*/
			SET @PreOrderItemID = NULL
			SET @PrdGroupPordITaxSum = NULL
			SET @SumPordITaxSum = NULL
			SET @DiffpordITaxSum = NULL
			SET @SumPordIPriceSum = NULL
			SET @SumPordITaxIndex = NULL
			SET @NewPordITaxIndex = NULL
			SET @PordICountInGroup = NULL
			SET @TspGroupIndentify = LTRIM(RTRIM(@TspGroupIndentify))
			SET @CalcTxt = NULL
			/*-----------------------------------------------*/
			SET @PrdGroupPordITaxSum = ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL([pordITaxSum],0)) 
											  FROM @Tmp_PordItemSumTax 
											  WHERE LTRIM(RTRIM([TspGroupIndentify])) = @TspGroupIndentify),0)

			IF(ISNULL(@TspPrdSendMethod,0) = 1)/*Product first group*/
			BEGIN
			/**/				
				SELECT 
					@SumPordIPriceSum = SUM(ISNULL([pordIPriceSum],0)),
					@SumPordITaxIndex = SUM(ISNULL([pordITaxIndex],0))
				FROM @Tmp_PordItems
				WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspFGIndentify])),'') = @TspGroupIndentify

				SET @PordICountInGroup = ISNULL((SELECT COUNT(*)
											    FROM @Tmp_PordItemSumTax 
											    WHERE LTRIM(RTRIM([TspGroupIndentify])) = @TspGroupIndentify),0)

				IF((SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT [pordITaxIndex]) FROM   @Tmp_PordItems WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspFGIndentify])),'') = @TspGroupIndentify) = 1)
				BEGIN
					SET @CalcTxt = 1
				END

				IF(ISNULL(@PordICountInGroup,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @NewPordITaxIndex =  ISNULL(@SumPordITaxIndex,0) / @PordICountInGroup					
				END
				
				IF(ISNULL(@SumPordIPriceSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@NewPordITaxIndex,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @SumPordITaxSum = (ISNULL(@SumPordIPriceSum,0) * ISNULL(@NewPordITaxIndex,0)) / 100					
				END				
				SET @PreOrderItemID = (SELECT TOP(1) [PreOrderItemID]
									FROM @Tmp_PordItems
									WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspFGIndentify])),'') = @TspGroupIndentify)
			/**/
			END
			ELSE IF(ISNULL(@TspPrdSendMethod,0) = 3)/*Product second group*/
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SELECT 
					@SumPordIPriceSum = SUM(ISNULL([pordIPriceSum],0)),
					@SumPordITaxIndex = SUM(ISNULL([pordITaxIndex],0))
				FROM @Tmp_PordItems
				WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspSGIndentify])),'') = @TspGroupIndentify

				SET @PordICountInGroup = ISNULL((SELECT COUNT(*)
											    FROM @Tmp_PordItemSumTax 
											    WHERE LTRIM(RTRIM([TspGroupIndentify])) = @TspGroupIndentify),0)
				
				IF((SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT [pordITaxIndex]) FROM   @Tmp_PordItems WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspSGIndentify])),'') = @TspGroupIndentify) = 1)
				BEGIN
					SET @CalcTxt = 1
				END

				IF(ISNULL(@PordICountInGroup,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @NewPordITaxIndex =  ISNULL(@SumPordITaxIndex,0) / @PordICountInGroup					
				END
				
				IF(ISNULL(@SumPordIPriceSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@NewPordITaxIndex,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @SumPordITaxSum = (ISNULL(@SumPordIPriceSum,0) * ISNULL(@NewPordITaxIndex,0)) / 100
					
				END

				SET @PreOrderItemID = (SELECT TOP(1) [PreOrderItemID]
									FROM @Tmp_PordItems
									WHERE ISNULL(LTRIM(RTRIM([TspSGIndentify])),'') = @TspGroupIndentify)
			/**/
			END
			/**/
			IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @SumPordITaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@SumPordITaxSum))									
			END
			/*-----------------------------------------------*/			
			IF(ISNULL(@SumPordITaxSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@PrdGroupPordITaxSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@PreOrderItemID,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@CalcTxt,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/				
				SET @DiffPordITaxSum = ISNULL(@SumPordITaxSum,0) - ISNULL(@PrdGroupPordITaxSum,0)				
				IF(ISNULL(@DiffPordITaxSum,0) <> 0 )
				BEGIN
				/**/
					/*---------------------------------------------*/
					DECLARE @pordIQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@pordITotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
					@pordIFCR FLOAT = NULL, @pordITax NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@pordITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
					@pordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL,@pordIPriceSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@pordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
					@pordIQuantityTotal FLOAT = NULL,@PrdQtyNoFCR BIT = NULL,@TmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
					/*---------------------------------------------*/
					SELECT TOP (1) 
						 @pordIQuantity = [pordIQuantity]						
						,@pordITotalPrice = [pordITotalPrice]												
						,@pordIFCR = [pordIFCR]
						,@pordITax = [pordITax]
						,@pordITaxIndex = [pordITaxIndex]						
						,@pordIFCRI = [pordIFCRI]						
						,@pordIPriceSum = [pordIPriceSum]						
						,@pordITaxSum = [pordITaxSum]
						,@pordIQuantityTotal = [pordIQuantityTotal]
						,@PrdQtyNoFCR = (SELECT TOP(1) [PrdQtyNoFCR] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] WHERE ProductId = pordIPrdId)	
					FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] 
					WHERE [PreOrderItemID] = @PreOrderItemID
					/*---------------------------------------------*/
					SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@pordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@pordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@pordIFCRI,1)
					IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@pordIQuantity,1)
					/**/
					END
					IF(ISNULL(@pordIQuantityTotal,0) > 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@pordIQuantityTotal,1) * ISNULL(@pordIFCRI,1)
						IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
						BEGIN
						/**/
							SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@pordIQuantityTotal,1)
						/**/
						END
					/**/
					END
					/*------------------------------------*/
					/*----------------- Calculate -----------------*/
					SET @pordITaxSum = ISNULL(@pordITaxSum,0) + ISNULL(@DiffPordITaxSum,0)					
					IF(ISNULL(@pordITaxSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@pordIPriceSum,0) > 0)
					BEGIN
						SET @pordITaxIndex = (ISNULL(@pordITaxSum,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@pordIPriceSum,0)
						SET @pordITax = (ISNULL(@pordITaxSum,0) / ISNULL(@TmpI,0))
					END
					SET @pordITotalPrice = ISNULL(@pordIPriceSum,0) + ISNULL(@pordITaxSum,0)
					/*--------- Calculate item by currency unit ------*/
					IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
					BEGIN
						SET @pordITaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordITaxSum))
						SET @pordITax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordITax))									
						SET @pordITotalPrice = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@pordITotalPrice))						
					END
					/*--------------------- Update item --------------*/					
					UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
					SET  [pordITotalPrice] = @pordITotalPrice
						,[pordITax] = @pordITax
						,[pordITaxIndex] = @pordITaxIndex
						,[pordITaxSum] = @pordITaxSum			
						,[Month1] = @Month1						
					WHERE PreOrderItemID = @PreOrderItemID	
					/*------------------------------------------------*/
				/**/
				END							   
			/**/
			END			
			/*-----------------------------------------------*/
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_CalcPordITax INTO @TspGroupIndentify
		END
		CLOSE Crsr_CalcPordITax
		DEALLOCATE Crsr_CalcPordITax		
		/*-----------------------------------------------------------------*/	
		/*-----------------------------------------------------------------*/		
		COMMIT /*CalcPordITax*/
		SET @Result = 1
		RETURN @Result
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
			ROLLBACK /*CalcPordITax*/	
		/**/
		SET @Result = -1
		CLOSE Crsr_CalcPordITax
		DEALLOCATE Crsr_CalcPordITax
		/**/		
	END CATCH	   
	/*-----------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END
/*--------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[ApispDeleteBuyOrder]







/*NKR14030306*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispDeleteBuyOrder]
(		
	/*
		EXEC [ApispDeleteBuyOrder] 17193 
	*/
	@BuyOrderId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @Res INT = 0

	BEGIN TRY
		/**/
		BEGIN TRANSACTION 
			/**/
			DECLARE @IsFixed BIT
			SET @IsFixed = (SELECT TOP 1 bordIsFixed FROM dbo.WSBuyOrder WHERE BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderId)
			IF(ISNULL(@IsFixed,0) <> 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				EXEC ApispDyDeleteFormFieldMasterValuesBySysPart @BuyOrderId, 'bord'

				DELETE WSBuyOrder_Lang  WHERE BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderId
				DELETE WSBuyOrder  WHERE BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderId

				/*Check for delete*/
				DECLARE @FindRecordId INT
				SET @FindRecordId = (SELECT TOP 1 BuyOrderID FROM WSBuyOrder  WHERE BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderId)

				IF(@FindRecordId IS NULL) /*delete*/
					SET @Res = 1
				ELSE /*not delete*/
					SET @Res = 0
			/**/
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @Res = -2
			END
			/**/
		COMMIT
		/**/
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		/**/
		IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
			ROLLBACK
		SET @Res = 0
		/**/
	END CATCH
	/**/

	SELECT @Res AS Result

	/**/
END






GO
-- [dbo].[ApispDeleteBuyOrderItems]







/*NKR14030306*/
CREATE Procedure [dbo].[ApispDeleteBuyOrderItems]
(
	@BuyOrderItemID INT,
	@Month1 SMALLINT,
	@LanguageID INT,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @Res INT = 0

	BEGIN TRY
		/**/
		BEGIN TRANSACTION
			DECLARE @IsFixed BIT = 0,
					@BuyOrderId INT = 0
			
			SELECT
				@IsFixed = O.bordIsFixed,
				@BuyOrderId = O.BuyOrderID
			FROM dbo.WSBuyOrder O WHERE O.BuyOrderID = 
									(SELECT OI.BuyOrderID FROM dbo.WSBuyOrderItems OI WHERE OI.BuyOrderItemId = @BuyOrderItemID)

			IF (ISNULL(@BuyOrderId, 0) = 0)
			BEGIN
				/**/
				SET @Res = -1
				/**/
			END
			ELSE IF(ISNULL(@IsFixed, 0) = 1)
			BEGIN
				/**/
				SET @Res = -2
				/**/
			END
			ELSE 
			BEGIN
				/**/
				DELETE WSBuyOrderItems_Lang  WHERE BuyOrderItemId = @BuyOrderItemID
				DELETE WSBuyOrderItems  WHERE BuyOrderItemId = @BuyOrderItemID

				/*=========================== Calculate Order ====================*/
				IF(ISNULL(@BuyOrderId,0) > 0)							
					EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateBuyOrderAmounts] @BuyOrderId, @Month1, @LanguageID, @DefaultLanguageID
					
				SET @Res = 1
			END
		COMMIT
		/**/
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		/**/
		IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
			ROLLBACK
		SET @Res = 0
		/**/
	END CATCH
	/**/

	SELECT @Res AS Result

	/**/
END

-- [dbo].[ApispDeleteCompanyBackDependencies]






/*NKR990509- CREATE FROM CRMspDeleteCompanyBackDependencies*/
/*RZ14011123- Add CrmCompanyChangeHistory*/
Create procedure [dbo].[ApispDeleteCompanyBackDependencies]
(
	/*
		exec [dbo].[ApispDeleteCompanyBackDependencies] 2324
	*/
@CompanyId INT
)
AS 
BEGIN
/**/
	DELETE SmsSendDetail
	WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId

	DELETE SmsReceive
	WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId

	DELETE SmsTempMobileNumbers
	WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId

	DELETE MobCompanyAppInfo
	WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId

	DELETE EmailUserTempTank
	WHERE EmailCmpId = @CompanyId

	DELETE EmailSendDetail
	WHERE EmailCmpId = @CompanyId

	DELETE CrmTelegContacts
	WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId

	DELETE CrmTelegBotContacts
	WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId

	DELETE CrmCallHistory
	WHERE CompanyId = @CompanyId

	
	DELETE FROM [SmsTempMobileNumbers]
	WHERE [CompanyId] = @CompanyId

	DELETE FROM [EmailUserTempTank]
	WHERE [EmailCmpId] = @CompanyId
	
	/*RZ14011123- Add CrmCompanyChangeHistory*/
	DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyChangeHistory_Lang] 
	WHERE [CompanyChangeHistoryId] IN(SELECT CCH.[CompanyChangeHistoryId] 
									  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyChangeHistory] CCH
								      WHERE [CompanyID] = @CompanyId)
	DELETE FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyChangeHistory] 
	WHERE [CompanyID] = @CompanyId
	/*RZ14011123- End*/

/**/	
END

-- [dbo].[ApispDeleteCompanyCampaign]






/*RZ14010913*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[ApispDeleteCompanyCampaign]
(		
/*
	exec ApispDeleteCompanyCampaign 6
*/
@CompanyCampaignId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
/**/
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		DECLARE @CompanyId INT
		SET @CompanyId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyId] 
						  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] 
						  WHERE @CompanyCampaignId IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL([value],'0') AS INT) 
													  FROM STRING_SPLIT(cmpCampaignIds, ',') 
													  WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) <> 0))		
		DECLARE @ProjectId INT
		SET @ProjectId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProjectID] 
						  FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] 
						  WHERE @CompanyCampaignId IN(SELECT CAST(ISNULL([value],'0') AS INT) 
													  FROM STRING_SPLIT(prjcmpCampaignIds, ',') 
													  WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) <> 0))

		
		IF(ISNULL(@CompanyId,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			DECLARE @ChildId INT,@ParentId INT
			SET @ChildId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyCampaignId] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyCampaign] WHERE ParentId = @CompanyCampaignId)
			IF(ISNULL(@ChildId,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @ParentId  = (SELECT TOP 1 [ParentId] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyCampaign] WHERE [CompanyCampaignId] = @CompanyCampaignId)
				DELETE [dbo].[CrmCompanyCampaign_Lang]  WHERE [CompanyCampaignId] = @CompanyCampaignId
				DELETE [dbo].[CrmCompanyCampaign]  WHERE [CompanyCampaignId] = @CompanyCampaignId
				/*================= Upadte CampaignIsLeaf for parent =================*/
				DECLARE @ParentChildId INT
				SET @ParentChildId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyCampaignId] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyCampaign] WHERE ParentId = @ParentId)
				IF(ISNULL(@ParentChildId,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					UPDATE [dbo].[CrmCompanyCampaign]
					   SET [CampaignIsLeaf] = 1					  
					WHERE [CompanyCampaignId] = @ParentId
					AND ISNULL([CampaignIsLeaf],0) = 0				
				/**/
				END
				/*===================== NeedUpdateUsersMobBaseInfo ===================*/
				EXEC [dbo].[ApispNeedUpdateUsersMobBaseInfo]
				@UpdateDetail = N'CrmCompanyCampaign',
				@UserId = NULL,
				@RollId = NULL
				/*-----------------------------------------------*/
				/*============================================================*/
				COMMIT
				SELECT 1 AS Result
			/**/
			END
			ELSE IF(ISNULL(@ChildId,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				COMMIT
				SELECT -1 AS Result
			/**/
			END 
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF(ISNULL(@CompanyId,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			COMMIT
			SELECT -2 AS Result
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF(ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			COMMIT
			SELECT -3 AS Result
		/**/
		END
/**/
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
/**/
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 AS Result
/**/
END CATCH
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[ApispDeleteDoc]





/*NKR990514*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[ApispDeleteDoc]
(		
	/*
		exec ApispDeleteDoc 293
	*/
	@DocID INT
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		/*RZ990516- Check isFixed*/
		DECLARE @IsFixed BIT
		SET @IsFixed = (SELECT TOP 1 IsFix FROM dbo.WSDoc WHERE DocID = @DocID)
		IF(ISNULL(@IsFixed,0) <> 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			DECLARE @Part NVARCHAR(200)='', @OprTypeId INT=0, @CanDelete BIT=0

			/* FIND OprType WITH @DocID */
			SELECT @OprTypeId = ISNULL(DO.OprType, 0) FROM WSDocOpr DO WHERE (SELECT D.OprId FROM WSDoc D WHERE D.DocID = @DocID) = DO.DocOprID


			IF (@OprTypeId = 1)
				SET @Part = 'WhDoc'
			ELSE IF (@OprTypeId = 2)
				SET @Part = 'WhDraft'
			ELSE IF (@OprTypeId = 3)
				SET @Part = 'WhMovement'
			ELSE
				SET @Part = ''
		
			IF (ISNULL(@Part, '') <> '')
			BEGIN

				EXEC ApispDyDeleteFormFieldMasterValuesBySysPart @DocID, @Part

				DELETE [dbo].[WSDoc_Lang]  WHERE DocID = @DocID
				DELETE [dbo].[WSDoc]  WHERE DocID = @DocID
				SELECT 1 AS Result

			END
			ELSE			
				SELECT 0 AS Result
		/**/
		END
		ELSE 
		BEGIN
			SELECT -2 AS Result
		END
		/*RZ990516- End*/
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/**/
END





GO

/*NKR990513*/
/*RZ990515- Modify this sp*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispDeleteOrder]
(		
/*
	EXEC ApispDeleteOrder 17193 
*/
	@OrderId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	/*RZ990515- Modify this sp*/
	BEGIN TRY
		BEGIN TRANSACTION 
			/*RZ990515- Check isFixed*/
			DECLARE @IsFixed BIT
			SET @IsFixed = (SELECT TOP 1 ordIsFixed FROM dbo.WSOrder WHERE OrderID = @OrderID)
			IF(ISNULL(@IsFixed,0) <> 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				EXEC ApispDyDeleteFormFieldMasterValuesBySysPart @OrderID, 'Ord'

				DELETE WSOrder_Lang  WHERE OrderID = @OrderID
					DELETE WSOrder  WHERE OrderID = @OrderID

				/*Check for delete*/
				DECLARE @FindRecordId INT
				SET @FindRecordId = (SELECT TOP 1 OrderID FROM WSOrder  WHERE OrderID = @OrderID)
				IF(@FindRecordId IS NULL) /*delete*/
					SELECT 1 as Result 
				ELSE
					SELECT 0 as Result /*not delete*/
			/**/
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SELECT -2 as Result
			END
			/*RZ990515- End*/
		COMMIT
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
			ROLLBACK
		SELECT 0 as Result
	END CATCH
	/**/
END

		exec ApispDeleteOrderItems 1756
	*/

	/*RZ990515- Modify this sp*/
	@Month1 SMALLINT,
	@LanguageID INT,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT
	/*RZ990515- End*/
)
As
BEGIN
	/**/
	BEGIN TRY
		BEGIN TRANSACTION
			/*RZ990515- Check isFixed*/
			DECLARE @IsFixed BIT
			SET @IsFixed = (SELECT TOP 1 ordIsFixed FROM dbo.WSOrder WHERE OrderID = 
									(SELECT TOP 1 OrderId FROM dbo.WSOrderItems WHERE OrderItemID = @OrderItemID))
			IF(ISNULL(@IsFixed,0) <> 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				DECLARE @OrderId INT
				SET @OrderId = (SELECT TOP 1 [OrderID] FROM [dbo].WSOrderItems  WHERE OrderItemID = @OrderItemID)/*RZ990515- Modify this sp*/

				/**/
			 
				DELETE WSOrderItems_Lang  WHERE OrderItemID = @OrderItemID
				DELETE WSOrderItems  WHERE OrderItemID = @OrderItemID
				/*=========================== Calculate Order ====================*/
				IF(ISNULL(@OrderId,0) > 0)							
					EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts] @OrderId, @Month1, @LanguageID, @DefaultLanguageID


				SELECT 1 AS Result
			/**/
			END
			ELSE 
			BEGIN
				SELECT -2 AS Result
			END
			/*RZ990515- End*/			
		COMMIT
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
			ROLLBACK
		SELECT 0 AS Result
	END CATCH
	/**/
END

-- [dbo].[ApispDocVerify]






/*NKR14020802*/
CREATE Procedure [dbo].[ApispDocVerify]	
(
	/*
		exec [ApispDocVerify] 867,3,'2022-12-17','14010926'
	*/
	@DocId INT,
	@VerifierUserId INT,
	@VerifiedDate DATETIME,
	@DefLangCollation NVARCHAR(50) = 'fa-IR'
)
As
BEGIN
	/**/
	UPDATE dbo.WSDoc
	   SET [IsFix] = 1,
		   [IsVerified] = 1,
		   [VerifierId] = @VerifierUserId,
		   [VerifiedRegisterDate] = @VerifiedDate,
		   [VerifiedRegisterDatePersian] = FORMAT(@VerifiedDate, 'yyyyMMdd', @DefLangCollation)
	WHERE [DocID] = @DocId
	/**/
END

-- [dbo].[ApispDocVerifyExit]







CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispDocVerifyExit]
(
	/* 
		exec [ApispDocVerifyExit]  867
	*/
	@DocID INT
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	UPDATE [dbo].[WSDoc]
	   SET [IsFix] = 0,
		   [IsVerified] = NULL,
		   [VerifierId] = NULL,
		   [VerifiedRegisterDate] = NULL,
		   [VerifiedRegisterDatePersian] = NULL	
	 WHERE DocID = @DocID 
	/**/
END






GO
-- [dbo].[ApispDyDeleteFormFieldMasterValue]






/*NKR14020824*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispDyDeleteFormFieldMasterValue]
(		
	/*
		exec CRMspDyDeleteFormFieldMasterValue 86
	*/
	@MasterValueId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	BEGIN TRY
		BEGIN TRANSACTION 
			DELETE dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang]  WHERE FieldValueId IN 
				(SELECT FormFieldValueId FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues]  WHERE MasterValueId = @MasterValueId)
			DELETE dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues] WHERE MasterValueId = @MasterValueId
			DELETE dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails] WHERE MasterValueId = @MasterValueId
			DELETE dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]  WHERE FormFieldMasterValueId = @MasterValueId
			
			Select 1 as Result
		COMMIT
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
			ROLLBACK
		Select 0 as Result
	END CATCH
	/**/
END

-- [dbo].[ApispDyDeleteFormFieldMasterValuesBySysPart]






/*NKR990509 - CREATE FROM CRMspDyDeleteFormFieldMasterValuesBySysPart*/
CREATE Procedure [dbo].[ApispDyDeleteFormFieldMasterValuesBySysPart]
(		
	/*
		exec ApispDyDeleteFormFieldMasterValuesBySysPart 6875,N'Cmp'
	*/
	@ObjectId INT,
	@SysPart NVARCHAR(50)
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @Qry NVARCHAR(MAX)='' , @Trem NVARCHAR(MAX)
	IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'cmp')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [CompanyId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'prs')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [CompanyPersonId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'prj')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [ProjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'cnvs')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [ConversationId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'pord')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'ord')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'bord')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'ssec')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'rsec')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'whdoc')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'whdraft')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'whmovement')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'prd')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'cms')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	/**/
	SET @Qry = @Qry + N'DELETE dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang]  WHERE FieldValueId IN 
			(SELECT FormFieldValueId 
			 FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues] 
			 WHERE MasterValueId IN(
									SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
									FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] 
									WHERE ' + @Trem +
									' AND [FormId] IN (
											SELECT [FormId]
											FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
											WHERE [CrmSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))
											OR [FOSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))  
											)
									) ) /**/ '

	SET @Qry = @Qry + N'DELETE dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues] 
						WHERE MasterValueId IN(
								SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
								FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
								WHERE ' + @Trem +
								' AND [FormId] IN (
										SELECT [FormId]
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
										WHERE [CrmSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))
										OR [FOSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))  
										)
							) /**/ '

	SET @Qry = @Qry + N'DELETE dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails] 
						WHERE MasterValueId IN(
								SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
								FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
								WHERE ' + @Trem +
								' AND [FormId] IN (
										SELECT [FormId]
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
										WHERE [CrmSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))
										OR [FOSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))  
										)
							)/**/ '

	SET @Qry = @Qry + N'DELETE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
						WHERE ' + @Trem +
						' AND [FormId] IN (
						SELECT [FormId]
						FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
						WHERE [CrmSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))
						OR [FOSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))  
					)'
	/**/
	EXECUTE(@Qry)
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[ApispDyUpdateToDeleteFormFieldMasterValue]






/*NKR14020913*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispDyUpdateToDeleteFormFieldMasterValue]
(		
	/*
		exec [ApispDyUpdateToDeleteFormFieldMasterValue] 30
	*/
	@MasterValueId INT
)
As
BEGIN
	/**/
	BEGIN TRY
		BEGIN TRANSACTION 
			UPDATE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldValues]
			   SET [IsDeleted] = 1
			WHERE MasterValueId = @MasterValueId
			/**/
			UPDATE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails]
			   SET [IsDeleted] = 1
			 WHERE MasterValueId = @MasterValueId
			/**/
			UPDATE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
			   SET [IsDeleted] = 1
			 WHERE FormFieldMasterValueId = @MasterValueId
				
			SELECT 1 AS Result
		COMMIT
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
			ROLLBACK	
		SELECT 0 AS Result
	END CATCH
	/**/
END






GO
-- [dbo].[ApispInsertOrderItemFromLinkedPrd]






/*RZ14010919*/
/*RZ14020904- Calculate RateResult in fiexd PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[ApispInsertOrderItemFromLinkedPrd]
(		
/*
	exec ApispInsertOrderItemFromLinkedPrd 
	@ProductId = 7927,
	@OrderId = 2685, 
	@ProductPriceGroupId = 1,
	@OrdIWHId  = 2,
	@UserId = 3,
	@FPId = 1,
	@Month1 = 12,
	@RegisterDate = '2021-03-10',
	@RegisterDatePersian = '13991220',
	@AppType = 'dasti',
	@LanguageID = 1065,
	@LinkedPrdIds = NULL
*/
@ProductId INT,
@OrderId INT, 
@ProductPriceGroupId INT,
@OrdIWHId INT,
@UserId INT,
@FPId INT,
@Month1 INT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(20),
@LanguageID INT ,
@LinkedPrdIds NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*-------------------------- Begin define variables -----------------------*/
DECLARE @LinkedProductId INT,@LinkedPrdId INT,
@LinkedPrdQuantity FLOAT,@LinkedPrdNotVisible BIT,
@LinkedPrdNotInSum BIT,@LinkedPrdAddQuantity BIT,
@LinkedPrdAddPrice BIT,
@Price NUMERIC(18,2),
@TotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2),
@PrdDescription NVARCHAR(MAX),
@Discount NUMERIC(18,2),
@DiscountPrecent NUMERIC(18,2),
@OrdIPriceSum NUMERIC(18,2),
@OrdIDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2),			
@prdUnitId INT,@OrdItemId INT,
@FCR FLOAT,@FCRI FLOAT,@Quantity FLOAT,
@OrdIOrderView INT,
@OrdCurrencyUnitId INT,
@MainOrdIQuantity FLOAT, 
@LinkedPrdAddDiscount BIT,
@PrdColorId INT

,@PrdUnitIdI INT,@PrdUnitIRate FLOAT,
@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto BIT,@PrdQtyNoFCR BIT,
@PrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT

/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
SET @OrdCurrencyUnitId = (SELECT [ordCurrencyUnitId] FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] WHERE OrderID = @OrderId)

SET @MainOrdIQuantity = (SELECT TOP 1 ordIQuantity FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] 
						  WHERE OrderId = @OrderId 
						  AND ordIPrdId = @ProductId
						  AND ordIWHId = @OrdIWHId
						  AND ordIPrdPriceGroupID = @ProductPriceGroupId)

DECLARE @OrdDateTime DATETIME
SET @OrdDateTime = (SELECT ordDateTime FROM dbo.WSOrder WHERE OrderID = @OrderId)

/*-------------------------- End define variables --------------------------*/
/*-------------------------------------------------------------------*/

IF(ISNULL(@LinkedPrdIds,'') ='')
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @LinkedPrdIds = (STUFF((SELECT ',' + CAST(LP.[LinkedProductId] AS NVARCHAR(200)) 		
						FROM  [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts] LP
						/*WHERE ISNULL([LinkedPrdIsSelect],0) = 1*//*This part use in insert| edit item in pord,ord,bord, because not set LinkedPrdIsSelect*/
						FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, ''))
/**/
END

/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
BEGIN TRY	
/*--------------------------------------*/
	BEGIN TRANSACTION
	/*--------------------------------------*/
	DECLARE Crsr_Add_Item CURSOR LOCAL FOR	
		SELECT 
			 [LinkedProductId]
			/*,[LinkedPrdId]*/							
			,(CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId 
				   THEN LinkedPrdId
				   ELSE PrdId									
			 END) [LinkedPrdId]			 
			,[LinkedPrdQuantity]
			,[LinkedPrdNotVisible]
			,[LinkedPrdNotInSum]
			,[LinkedPrdAddQuantity]
			,[LinkedPrdAddPrice]
			,[LinkedPrdAddDiscount]
		FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]	
		WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId	OR 	[LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)					
		AND [LinkedProductId] IN (SELECT CAST(ISNULL(splitdata,0) AS INT) FROM [dbo].[fnSplitString](@LinkedPrdIds,','))
		ORDER BY [OrderView]					
	OPEN Crsr_Add_Item
	FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Item INTO @LinkedProductId,@LinkedPrdId,
									   @LinkedPrdQuantity,@LinkedPrdNotVisible,
									   @LinkedPrdNotInSum,@LinkedPrdAddQuantity,
									   @LinkedPrdAddPrice,@LinkedPrdAddDiscount
	WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
	BEGIN
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		SET @OrdItemId = NULL				
		SET @PrdDescription = NULL		
		SET @prdUnitId = NULL		
		SET @FCR = NULL
		SET @FCRI = NULL
		SET @Quantity = NULL
		SET @Price = NULL
		SET @Discount = NULL
		SET @DiscountPrecent = NULL
		SET @OrdIPriceSum = NULL
		SET @OrdIDiscountSum = NULL
		SET @TotalPrice = NULL
		SET @OrdIOrderView = NULL	
		SET @PrdColorId = NULL
		SET @PrdUnitIdI = NULL
		SET @PrdUnitIRate = NULL
		SET @PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = NULL
		SET @PrdQtyNoFCR = NULL
		SET @PrdUnitIRateResult = NULL		
		/*-------------- Set variables -------------------*/		
		SELECT 
			@PrdDescription = PrdDescription,
			@prdUnitId = prdUnitId,
			@FCR = (SELECT FCR FROM [dbo].[WSProductUnits] WHERE ProductUnitId = prdUnitId),
			@FCRI = (SELECT FCRI FROM [dbo].[WSProductUnits] WHERE ProductUnitId = prdUnitId),
			@PrdColorId = PrdColorId,
			@PrdUnitIdI = [PrdUnitIdI],
			@PrdUnitIRate = [PrdUnitIRate],
			@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = [PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto],
			@PrdQtyNoFCR = [PrdQtyNoFCR]
			
		FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
		WHERE ProductId = @LinkedPrdId		
		/**/
		SET @Quantity = ISNULL(@LinkedPrdQuantity,1)
		IF(ISNULL(@LinkedPrdAddQuantity,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			
			SET @Quantity = ISNULL(@Quantity,1) * ISNULL(@MainOrdIQuantity,1)
		/**/
		END
		/*SET @Quantity = ISNULL(@Quantity,1) * ISNULL(@MainOrdIQuantity,1)*/
		
		DECLARE @FCRExtra FLOAT = NULL,@FCRExtraI FLOAT = NULL,@QtySum FLOAT = NULL
		SET @FCRExtra = @FCR
		SET @FCRExtraI = @FCRI
		IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @FCRExtra = 1
			SET @FCRExtraI = 1
		/**/
		END
		/*----------------------------------------------------*/
		SET @QtySum = ISNULL(@Quantity,1) * ISNULL(@FCRExtra,1) * ISNULL(@FCRExtraI,1)
		IF(/*ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto,0) = 1 AND*/ ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0) <> 0)/*RZ14020904- Calculate RateResult in fiexd PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto*/
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*SET @PrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@QtySum,1)*/
			SET @PrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@Quantity,1)

			IF(ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @PrdUnitIRateResult = CAST((FORMAT(@PrdUnitIRateResult, '000.000')) AS FLOAT) 
			/**/
			END
		/**/
		END
		/*RZ14010619- End*/

		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@LinkedPrdAddPrice,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @Price = ISNULL(((SELECT TOP 1 ISNULL(Price, 0) 
								  FROM dbo.WSProductPrice 
								  WHERE PrdId = @LinkedPrdId 
								  AND ProductPriceGroupId = @ProductPriceGroupId 
								  ORDER BY DocDateTime DESC)),0)			
			
			
			DECLARE @CurrencyPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@CurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,@CurrencyRate FLOAT = NULL
			SET @CurrencyPrice = ISNULL(((SELECT TOP 1 ISNULL(CurrencyPrice, 0) 
								  FROM dbo.WSProductPrice 
								  WHERE PrdId = @LinkedPrdId 
								  AND ProductPriceGroupId = @ProductPriceGroupId 
								  ORDER BY DocDateTime DESC)),0)

			SET @CurrencyUnitId = ISNULL(((SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyUnitId] 
								  FROM dbo.WSProductPrice 
								  WHERE PrdId = @LinkedPrdId 
								  AND ProductPriceGroupId = @ProductPriceGroupId 
								  ORDER BY DocDateTime DESC)),0)
			
			SET @CurrencyRate = ISNULL(((SELECT TOP 1 ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyRate] 
															  FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnitRate] CUR
															  WHERE ISNULL(CUR.[SrcCurrencyUnitId],0) = ISNULL(PP.CurrencyUnitId,0)
															  AND ISNULL(CUR.[DestCurrencyUnitId],0) = ISNULL(@OrdCurrencyUnitId,0)
															  ORDER BY [RateDate] DESC),0) 
								 FROM dbo.WSProductPrice PP
								 WHERE PrdId = @LinkedPrdId 
								 AND ProductPriceGroupId = @ProductPriceGroupId 
								 ORDER BY DocDateTime DESC)),0)
			/*-------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@Price,0) = 0 AND  ISNULL(@CurrencyPrice,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@CurrencyRate,0) <> 0 AND
				(ISNULL(@OrdCurrencyUnitId,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@CurrencyUnitId,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@OrdCurrencyUnitId,0) <> ISNULL(@CurrencyUnitId,0)))
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @Price = ISNULL(@CurrencyPrice,0) * ISNULL(@CurrencyRate,1)
			/**/
			END			
			
			/**/
			IF(ISNULL(@LinkedPrdAddDiscount,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @Discount = ISNULL(((SELECT TOP 1 ISNULL(Discount, 0) 
								  FROM dbo.WSProductPrice 
								  WHERE PrdId = @LinkedPrdId 
								  AND ProductPriceGroupId = @ProductPriceGroupId 
								  ORDER BY DocDateTime DESC)),0)
				/**/
				SET @DiscountPrecent = ISNULL(((SELECT TOP 1 ISNULL(DiscountPrecent, 0) 
									  FROM dbo.WSProductPrice 
									  WHERE PrdId = @LinkedPrdId 
									  AND ProductPriceGroupId = @ProductPriceGroupId 
									  ORDER BY DocDateTime DESC)),0)
			/**/
			END	
			
			/**/
			DECLARE @TmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = 0, @TmpII NUMERIC(18,2) = 0
			/**/
			/*SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@Quantity,1) * ISNULL(@FCR,1) * ISNULL(@FCRI,1)*/
			SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@QtySum,1)
			

			IF(ISNULL(@DiscountPrecent,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @OrdIDiscountSum = ((ISNULL(@Price,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0)) * ISNULL(@DiscountPrecent,0)) / 100
				SET @Discount = (ISNULL(@Price,0) * ISNULL(@DiscountPrecent,0)) / 100
			/**/
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @OrdIDiscountSum = (ISNULL(@Discount,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0))
			/**/
			END
			SET @TmpII = ISNULL(@Price,0) - ISNULL(@Discount,0)
			
			SET @OrdIPriceSum = (ISNULL(@TmpII,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0))
			SET @TotalPrice = @OrdIPriceSum		
		/**/
		END		
		/**/				
		DECLARE @ItemCount INT = NULL
		SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @OrderId)
		SET @OrdIOrderView = (@ItemCount + 1) + (@ItemCount * 4 ) - 1
		/*-------------------------------------------*/	
		/*----------------------------------------*/		
		DECLARE @ordIValidityExpireDate DATETIME = NULL,@ordIValidityExpireDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL
		,@PrdValidityDay INT = NULL,@PrdValidityTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,@IsCalcValidityExpireDate BIT = NULL
		,@PrdValidityTimeHour INT = NULL,@PrdValidityTimeMinute INT = NULL
		SELECT  
			 @PrdValidityDay = [PrdValidityDay]
			,@PrdValidityTime = [PrdValidityTime]      
		FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
		WHERE [ProductID] = @LinkedPrdId
		AND ISNULL([PrdValidity],0) = 1

		IF((@PrdValidityDay IS NOT NULL))
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @IsCalcValidityExpireDate = 1
		/**/
		END

		IF(ISNULL(@PrdValidityTime,'') <> '')
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @IsCalcValidityExpireDate = 1
			SET @PrdValidityTimeHour = CAST((SUBSTRING(@PrdValidityTime,1,2)) AS INT)
			SET @PrdValidityTimeMinute = CAST((SUBSTRING(@PrdValidityTime,4,2)) AS INT)
		/**/
		END

		IF(ISNULL(@IsCalcValidityExpireDate,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @ordIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(DAY, ISNULL(@PrdValidityDay,0), @OrdDateTime)
			SET @ordIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(HOUR, ISNULL(@PrdValidityTimeHour,0), @ordIValidityExpireDate)
			SET @ordIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(MINUTE, ISNULL(@PrdValidityTimeMinute,0), @ordIValidityExpireDate)
			SET @ordIValidityExpireDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](@ordIValidityExpireDate))
		/**/
		END		
		/*RZ14010319- End*/
		/*----------------------------------------*/

		/*Insert WSOrderItems*/
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
			([OrderId]
			,[ordIPrdId]
			,[ordIQuantity]
			,[ordIPrice]
			,[ordITotalPrice]
			,[ordIDescription]
			,[UserId]
			,[FPId]
			,[Month1]
			,[RegisterDate]
			,[RegisterDatePersian]
			,[ordIDiscount]
			,[ordIDiscountPercent]
			,[ordIPrdUnitId]
			,[ordICurrencyUnitId]
			,[ordIOrderView]
			,[ordIFCR]
			,[ordINotVisible]
			,[ordIFCRI]
			,[ordINotInSum]
			,[ordIWHId]
			,[AppType]
			,[LanguageID]
			,[FirstRegisterUserId]
			,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
			,[FirstRegisterDate]
			,[ordIPrdPriceGroupID]
			,[ordIPriceSum]
			,[ordIDiscountSum]
			
			,[ordIValidityExpireDate]
			,[ordIValidityExpireDatePersian]					
			,[ordIPrdColorId]
			,[ordIPrdUnitIdI]
			,[ordIPrdUnitIRateResult]			
			)
		VALUES
			(@OrderId
			,@LinkedPrdId
			,ISNULL(@Quantity,1)
			,@Price
			,@TotalPrice
			,@PrdDescription
			,@UserId
			,@FPId
			,@Month1
			,@RegisterDate
			,@RegisterDatePersian
			,@Discount
			,@DiscountPrecent
			,@PrdUnitId
			,@OrdCurrencyUnitId
			,@OrdIOrderView
			,@FCR
			,@LinkedPrdNotVisible
			,@FCRI
			,@LinkedPrdNotInSum
			,@OrdIWHId
			,@AppType
			,@LanguageID
			,@UserId
			,@RegisterDatePersian
			,@RegisterDate
			,@ProductPriceGroupId
			,@OrdIPriceSum
			,@OrdIDiscountSum
			,@ordIValidityExpireDate
			,@ordIValidityExpireDatePersian	
			,@PrdColorId
			,@PrdUnitIdI
			,@PrdUnitIRateResult			
			)				
		/*-------------------------------------------*/	
		SET @OrdItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
		/*-------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@OrdItemId,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*-------------------------------------------*/
			/*Insert records in lang table*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItems_Lang] (
				 OrderItemId,
				 LanguageID,
				 ordIDescription			    			  
			) 
			SELECT
				@OrdItemId,
				PLN.LanguageID,
				PrdDescription
			FROM dbo.WSProducts_Lang PLN 
			WHERE  PLN.ProductID = @LinkedPrdId
			/*-------------------------------------------*/							
			/*-------------------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END		
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Item INTO @LinkedProductId,@LinkedPrdId,
										   @LinkedPrdQuantity,@LinkedPrdNotVisible,
										   @LinkedPrdNotInSum,@LinkedPrdAddQuantity,
										   @LinkedPrdAddPrice,@LinkedPrdAddDiscount
	END
	CLOSE Crsr_Add_Item
	DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Item

	/*-------------------------------*/
	SELECT 1 AS Result		
	COMMIT
/*--------------------------------------*/
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
    /*CLOSE Crsr_Add_Item
	DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Item*/
	SELECT 0 AS Result	
END CATCH
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/		
END








GO
-- [dbo].[ApispInsertPreOrder]

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispInsertPreOrder]
/*
	exec ApispInsertPreOrder 					
*/
(

@PordTitle NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@PordNumber BIGINT = NULL,
