@PordDateTime DATETIME,
@PordDay NVARCHAR(50),
@PordTime NVARCHAR(50),
@PordSubject NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@PordInstallAmount NUMERIC(18, 2) = 0,
@PordDiscount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordTax NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordTaxIndex NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordCalculateTax BIT = NULL,
@PordIsVerified BIT = NULL,
@PordVerifierId INT = NULL,
@PordVerifierRequestId INT = NULL,
@PordVerifiedRegisterDate DATETIME = NULL,
@PordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@PordStatusId INT = 1,
@PordIsFixed BIT = NULL,
@PordGuarantyId INT = NULL,
@PordPayConditionId INT = NULL,
@PordDeliveryTimeId INT = NULL,
@PordCreditDurationId INT = NULL,
@PordSpecialWord NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,
@PordDescriptionI NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
@PordDescriptionII NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
@PordDescriptionId INT = NULL,
@PordPrintFormatId INT = NULL,
@PordRLogoId INT = NULL,
@PordLLogoId INT = NULL,
@PordSigningI NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,
@PordSigningII NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,
@PordSigningIII NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,

@PordAccountShabaNumberI NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,

@PordEmail NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,

@PordDiscountTitle NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,

@PordTransportation NUMERIC(18, 2) = 0,
@PordReferUserId INT = NULL,
@PordRound NUMERIC(18, 2) = 0,
@PordcmpTitle NVARCHAR(1000) =  NULL,

/**/
@InsertedPreOrderId INT OUTPUT,

@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT,

/*NKR14020729- Start*/
@PordDeliveryTermId INT = NULL,
@PordDeliveryEndDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@PordDeliveryEndDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@PordDeliveryEndTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,

@PordDeliveryEndDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL
/*NKR14020729- End*/
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/

/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/

DECLARE @DefaultLanguageID INT 
SET @DefaultLanguageID = (SELECT TOP 1 [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])

/********************************************************************************/

DECLARE @FPId INT

DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT
/********************************************************************************/

/********************************************************************************/
/*PordVerifierId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordVerifierId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [UserId] FROM [dbo].[Users] WHERE [UserId] = @PordVerifierId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordVerifierId|'
END
/*PordVerifierRequestId */
IF(ISNULL(@PordVerifierRequestId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [UserId] FROM [dbo].[Users] WHERE [UserId] = @PordVerifierRequestId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordVerifierRequestId|'
END
/*PordStatusId */
IF(ISNULL(@PordStatusId ,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [PreOrderStatusId] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderStatus] WHERE [PreOrderStatusId] = @PordStatusId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordStatusId|'
END
ELSE
	SET @PordStatusId = 1
/*PordGuarantyId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordGuarantyId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [GuarantyID] FROM [dbo].[WSGuaranty] WHERE [GuarantyID] = @PordGuarantyId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordGuarantyId|'
END
/*PordPayConditionId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordPayConditionId,0) > 0)

		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordPayConditionId|'
END
/*PordDeliveryTimeId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordDeliveryTimeId,0) > 0)
BEGIN

		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordDeliveryTimeId|'
END
/*PordCreditDurationId*/

BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CreditDurationID] FROM [dbo].[WSCreditDuration] WHERE [CreditDurationID] = @PordCreditDurationId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordCreditDurationId|'

/*PordPrintFormatId*/

		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordReferUserId|'

BEGIN
	SET @PordReferUserId = @UserId

/*PreOrderOprId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PreOrderOprId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [PreOrderOprID] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderOpr] WHERE [PreOrderOprID] = @PreOrderOprId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PreOrderOprId|'
END
/*PordVerificationWorkFlowId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordVerificationWorkFlowId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [VerificationWorkFlowId] FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlows] WHERE [VerificationWorkFlowId] = @PordVerificationWorkFlowId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordVerificationWorkFlowId|'
END
/*------------------------*/
/*COMPANYID*/
IF(ISNULL(@CompanyId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE [CompanyID] = @CompanyId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpId|'
END
ELSE IF (ISNULL(@CompanyNumber,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	SET @CompanyId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE [cmpNumber] = @CompanyNumber)
END
ELSE IF (ISNULL(@CompanyCode,'') <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @CompanyId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE [cmpCode] = @CompanyCode)
END
IF(ISNULL(@CompanyId,0) <= 0)
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpId|'
/*COMPANYPERSONID*/	
IF(ISNULL(@CompanyPersonId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @PrsId INT
	SET @PrsId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyPersonID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] WHERE [CompanyPersonID] = @CompanyPersonId)
	IF(@PrsId IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsId|'

	BEGIN	
		SET @PrsId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyPersonID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] WHERE [CompanyPersonID] = @CompanyPersonId AND [prsCompanyId] = @CompanyId)
		IF(@PrsId IS NULL)
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsId|'
	END
	/**/	
END
ELSE IF(ISNULL(@CompanyPersonId,0) = 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @CompanyPersonId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyPersonID] FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons WHERE prsCompanyId = @CompanyId ORDER BY ISNULL(prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID))
	IF(ISNULL(@CompanyPersonId,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '')

			   ,N'��������'
		FROM dbo.Languages L
		WHERE ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1 
		AND L.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
	/**/
	END		
/**/
END
/*PROJECTID*/		
IF(ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	DECLARE @PrjId INT
	SET @PrjId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProjectID] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] WHERE [ProjectID] = @ProjectId)
	IF(@PrjId IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrjId|'
	ELSE IF((ISNULL(@CompanyId,0) > 0))
	BEGIN	
		SET @PrjId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProjectID] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] WHERE [ProjectID] = @ProjectId AND [prjCompanyId] = @CompanyId)
		IF(@PrjId IS NULL)

		SET @PordNumber = (SELECT [dbo].[ApifnReturnPreOrderNumber] (@FPId))
	END 
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @DupPreOrdNumber BIGINT
		SET @DupPreOrdNumber = (SELECT TOP 1 [pordNumber] from [dbo].[ApifnCheckDuplicatePreOrderNumber](@PordNumber, 0))
		IF(ISNULL(@DupPreOrdNumber,0) > 0)
			SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'DupPordNumber= ' + CAST(ISNULL(@DupPreOrdNumber,0) AS NVARCHAR(200)) + '|'				
	END
/**/
END
/********************************************************************************/
/******************************* PordExpireDate *********************************/

	DECLARE @CreditDurationHours INT
	SET @CreditDurationHours = (SELECT TOP 1 [CreditDurationHours] FROM [dbo].[WSCreditDuration] WHERE [CreditDurationID] = @PordCreditDurationId)
	IF(ISNULL(@CreditDurationHours,0) <> 0 AND @PordDateTime IS NOT NULL)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @PordExpireDateTime = DATEADD(HOUR,@CreditDurationHours,@PordDateTime)
		SET @PordExpireDate = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](@PordExpireDateTime))		
	/**/
	END	
/**/		
END
/********************************************************************************/
/******************************** Insert PreOrder *******************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRY  
    IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '')/*No Error*/
	BEGIN
		/*============================== Project =========================*/
		BEGIN TRAN
			IF(ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) = 0)
			BEGIN
				DECLARE @prjcnvsStatusGroupId INT			
				SET @prjcnvsStatusGroupId = (SELECT TOP 1 ConversationStatusGroupID FROM dbo.CrmConversationStatusGroups WHERE ConversationStatusGroupID > 0)
				IF(ISNULL(@ProjectTitle,'') = '')
					SET @ProjectTitle = @DefualtProjectTitle
				/**/
				SET @ProjectId =(SELECT TOP 1 P.ProjectID FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] P 
						  WHERE P.prjCompanyId = @CompanyId 
						  AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(P.prjTitle AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
						  NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like '%' + 
						  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@ProjectTitle AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
						  NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'))

				IF(ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @ProjectId = (SELECT TOP 1 ProjectId From dbo.CrmProjects WHERE prjCompanyId = @CompanyId ORDER BY [prjOrderView] DESC,prjStartDate DESC,[ProjectID] DESC)/*RZ14021005- Change orderView in project add desc to prjOrderView*/
					IF(ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) = 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						/*-------------------------------------------------*/
						/*RZ14021004- Add OrderView to project*/
						DECLARE @prjOrderView INT = 0

						/*RZ14021004- End*/

							   ,@RegisterDatePersian

							   ,@UserId
							   ,CAST(@FPId AS SMALLINT)
							   ,@Month1
							   ,@RegisterDate
							   ,@RegisterDatePersian
							   ,@AppType
							   ,@DefaultLanguageID
							   ,@UserId
							   ,@RegisterDate
							   ,@RegisterDatePersian

							   ,@RegisterDate
							   ,@prjcnvsStatusGroupId
							   ,@prjOrderView/*RZ14021004- Add OrderView to project*/)
						/**/
						SET @ProjectId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
						/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmProjects_Lang]
							([ProjectID]
							,[LanguageID]
							,[prjTitle])
						SELECT 
							 @ProjectId
							,L.LanguageID
							,@ProjectTitle			
						FROM [dbo].[Languages] L
						WHERE L.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
						AND ISNULL(L.IsActive,0) = 1
					/**/
					END					
				END
			END	
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert Project' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN
		/*=============================== PreOrder ==========================*/		
		BEGIN TRAN
			/*Main table*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
			   ([pordTitle]
			   ,[pordNumber]
			   ,[pordDate]
			   ,[pordCompanyId]
			   ,[pordCompanyPersonId]
			   ,[pordProjectId]
			   ,[pordSubject]
			   ,[pordInstallAmount]
			   ,[pordDiscount]
			   ,[pordTax]
			   ,[pordTaxIndex]
			   ,[pordCalculateTax]
			   ,[pordIsVerified]
			   ,[pordVerifierId]
			   ,[pordVerifierRequestId]
			   ,[pordVerifiedRegisterDate]
			   ,[pordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian]
			   ,[pordStatusId]
			   ,[pordIsFixed]
			   ,[pordGuarantyId]
			   ,[pordPayConditionId]
			   ,[pordDeliveryTimeId]
			   ,[pordCreditDurationId]

			   ,[pordReferUserId]

			   ,[pordDateTime]

			   ,[pordDeliveryTermId]

			   ,@PordDateTime
			   ,@RegisterDate

			   ,@PordDeliveryDateTime
			   ,@PordDeliveryTime
			   ,@PreOrderOprId
			   ,@AppType
			   ,@DefaultLanguageID
			   ,@UserId
			   ,@RegisterDatePersian
			   ,@RegisterDate
			   ,@PordVerificationWorkFlowId
			   ,@PordExpireDate
			   ,@PordExpireDateTime
			   /*NKR14020730- Start*/
			   ,@PordDeliveryTermId
			   ,@PordDeliveryEndDate
			   ,@PordDeliveryEndDateTime
			   ,@PordDeliveryEndTime
			   ,@PordDeliveryDay
			   ,@PordDeliveryEndDay
			   /*NKR14020730- End*/
				)
			/**/
			SET @InsertedPreOrderId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			SET @InsertedPreOrderNumber = @PordNumber
			/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/						
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrder_Lang]
					([PreOrderID]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[pordTitle]
					,[pordSubject]
					,[pordSpecialWord]
					,[pordDescriptionI]
					,[pordDescriptionII]
					,[pordSigningI]
					,[pordSigningII]
					,[pordSigningIII]
					,[pordAddress]
					,[pordDiscountTitle]
					,[pordcmpTitle])
			 SELECT
				   @InsertedPreOrderId
				   ,LN.LanguageID
				   ,@PordTitle
				   ,@PordSubject				   
				   ,@PordSpecialWord
				   ,@PordDescriptionI
				   ,@PordDescriptionII
				   ,@PordSigningI
				   ,@PordSigningII
				   ,@PordSigningIII
				   ,@PordAddress
				   ,@PordDiscountTitle
				   ,@PordcmpTitle
			 FROM dbo.Languages LN 
			 WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
			 AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1

		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert PreOrder' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 				
		/*=========================== Calculate PreOrder ====================*/
		EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts] 	@InsertedPreOrderId, @Month1, @LanguageID, @DefaultLanguageID						
		/**/
	END
	/**/	
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1

END TRY  
BEGIN CATCH  
	 /*RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)*/
	 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
     SET @InsertedPreOrderId = 0
	 SET @InsertedPreOrderNumber = 0
	 SET @ErrorMessage = 'Error|'
	 IF(ISNULL(@ErrorFrom,'') <> '')
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + @ErrorFrom
 	 SELECT 0	 
END CATCH  	
/********************************************************************************/
/**/
END







GO
-- [dbo].[ApispInsertPreOrderItems]








/*RZ990206*/
/*RZ990520- Add check pordIsFixed*/
/*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/
/*NKR14020727- Add Validity Fields*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispInsertPreOrderItems]
/*
	exec ApispInsertPreOrderItems 								
*/
(
/*PreOrder*/
@PreOrderId INT = NULL,
@PordNumber BIGINT = NULL,
/*PreOrder Item*/
@PordIPrdId INT = NULL,
@PordIPrdCode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PordIQuantity FLOAT,
@PordIPrice Numeric(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordITotalPrice Numeric(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordIDescription NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
@PordIQuantityDesc NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
@PordIDiscount Numeric(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordIDiscountI Numeric(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordIDiscountPercent Numeric(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordIDiscountIPercent Numeric(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordIPrdUnitId INT = NULL,
@PordICurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,
@PordIOrderView INT = NULL,
@PordIFCR FLOAT = NULL,
@PordITax Numeric(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordITaxIndex Numeric(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordICalculateTax BIT = NULL,
@PordINotVisible BIT = NULL,
@PordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL,
@PordINotInSum BIT = NULL,
@PordIWHId INT = NULL,
@PordIRound Numeric(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordIRoundPlus Numeric(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordIPrdPriceGroupId INT = NULL,
/*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/
@PordITracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
/*RZ990818- End*/
/**/
@UserId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20),
@LanguageID INT = NULL, 
/**/
@InsertedPreOrderItemId INT OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT,
/*NKR14020727- Start*/
@PordIValidityExpireDate DATETIME = NULL,
@PordIValidityExpireDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@PordIDeliveryTermId INT = NULL,
@PordIDeliveryDate DATETIME = NULL,
@PordIDeliveryDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@PordIDeliveryTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
@PordIDeliveryDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
@PordIDeliveryEndDate DATETIME = NULL,
@PordIDeliveryEndDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@PordIDeliveryEndTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
@PordIDeliveryEndDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL
/*NKR14020727- End*/
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/**********************************Default LanguageID ***************************/
DECLARE @DefaultLanguageID INT 
SET @DefaultLanguageID = (SELECT TOP 1 [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])

/********************************************************************************/
/************************************ Year1 => FPId *****************************/
DECLARE @FPId INT
SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Error & Variables *******************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT
/********************************************************************************/
/********************************* Check fields *********************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/*PreOrder*/
IF(ISNULL(@PreOrderId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	SET @PreOrderId = (SELECT TOP 1 [PreOrderID] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] WHERE [PreOrderID] = @PreOrderId)
END
ELSE IF (ISNULL(@PordNumber,0) <> 0)
BEGIN
	SET @PreOrderId = (SELECT TOP 1 [PreOrderID] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] WHERE [pordNumber] = @PordNumber)
END
IF(ISNULL(@PreOrderId,0) <= 0)
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordId|'
/*Product*/
IF(@PordIPrdId IS NOT NULL AND @PordIPrdId > 0)
BEGIN
	SET @PordIPrdId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProductID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] WHERE [ProductID] = @PordIPrdId)
END
ELSE IF (ISNULL(@PordIPrdCode,'') <> '')
BEGIN
	SET @PordIPrdId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProductID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] WHERE [PrdCode] = @PordIPrdCode)
END
IF(ISNULL(@PordIPrdId,0) <= 0)
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrdId|'
/*PordIPrdUnitId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordIPrdUnitId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [ProductUnitID] FROM [dbo].[WSProductUnits] WHERE [ProductUnitID] = @PordIPrdUnitId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordIPrdUnitId|'
END
/*PordICurrencyUnitId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordICurrencyUnitId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [CurrencyUnitID] FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = @PordICurrencyUnitId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordICurrencyUnitId|'
END
/*PordIWHId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordIWHId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [WareHouseID] FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse] WHERE [WareHouseID] = @PordIWHId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordIWHId|'
END
ELSE 
	SET @PordIWHId = (SELECT TOP 1 [WareHouseID] FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse] WHERE ISNULL([WrhsDeActive],0) = 1)
/*PordIPrdPriceGroupId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordIPrdPriceGroupId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [ProductPriceGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProductPriceGroup] WHERE [ProductPriceGroupID] = @PordIPrdPriceGroupId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordIPrdPriceGroupId|'
END
ELSE 
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @PordIPrdPriceGroupId = (SELECT TOP 1 ProductPriceGroupId 
												FROM dbo.WSProductPrice PP
												WHERE PP.PrdId = @PordIPrdId AND PP.Price = @PordIPrice)
	IF(ISNULL(@PordIPrdPriceGroupId,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @PordIPrdPriceGroupId = (SELECT TOP 1 ProductPriceGroupId 
									FROM dbo.WSProductPriceGroup PP
									ORDER BY PP.OrderView)													
	/**/
	END
/**/
END

/*RZ990520- Add check pordIsFixed*/
DECLARE @IsFixed BIT
SET @IsFixed = (SELECT TOP 1 pordIsFixed FROM dbo.WSPreOrder WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId)
IF(ISNULL(@IsFixed,0) = 1)
BEGIN
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'IsFixed|'
END
/*RZ990520- End*/
/********************************************************************************/
/******************** Insert PreOrder Items *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRY  
    IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')/*No Error*/
	BEGIN		
		/*======================== Order Item ==========================*/		
		BEGIN TRAN
			/*=============== Discount =================*/
			IF(ISNULL(@PordIDiscount,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@PordIDiscountPercent,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@PordIPrice,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @PordIDiscount = (ISNULL(@PordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@PordIDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
			END	
			IF(ISNULL(@PordIDiscount,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@PordIDiscountPercent,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@PordIPrice,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @PordIDiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@PordIDiscount,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@PordIPrice,0)
			END						
			/*=============== Calculate ================*/
			DECLARE @TmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = 0, @TmpII NUMERIC(18,2) = 0, 
			@TotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = 0
			/*Tmp = Quantity * FCR * FCRI*/
			SET @TmpI = @PordIQuantity * ISNULL(@PordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@PordIFCRI,1)
			/*TempII = price - discount - round + roundPlus*/
			SET @TmpII = ISNULL(@PordIPrice,0) - ISNULL(@PordIDiscount,0) - ISNULL(@PordIRound,0) + ISNULL(@PordIRoundPlus,0)/*- 0 - 0 + 0 */

			/*=============== Tax =================*/
			/*RZ990818*/
			IF(ISNULL(@PordITax,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@PordITaxIndex,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@TmpII,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @PordITax = (ISNULL(@TmpII,0) * ISNULL(@PordITaxIndex,0)) / 100
			END	
			IF(ISNULL(@PordITax,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@PordITaxIndex,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@TmpII,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @PordITaxIndex = (ISNULL(@PordITax,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@TmpII,0)
			END			
			/*RZ990818-End*/
			/*===================================*/
			/*TotalPrice = (@TmpI * @TmpII) + (@TmpI * tax)*/
			SET @TotalPrice = (@TmpI * @TmpII) + (@TmpI * ISNULL(@PordITax,0))/*RZ990818*/
			IF(@pordITotalPrice IS NULL )
				SET @pordITotalPrice = @TotalPrice
			/**********************************************************/				
			/*Set OrderView*/
			IF(@PordIOrderView IS NULL)
			BEGIN
				DECLARE @ItemCount INT
				SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.PreOrderId = @PreOrderId)
				SET @PordIOrderView = (@ItemCount + 1) + (@ItemCount * 4 ) - 1		
			END
			/**********************************************************/
			/*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/
			DECLARE @PordIPriceSum Numeric(18, 2) = 0,
			@PordIDiscountSum Numeric(18, 2) = 0,			
			@PordITaxSum Numeric(18, 2) = 0

			SET @PordIPriceSum = (@TmpI * @TmpII)
			SET @PordIDiscountSum = ISNULL(@PordIDiscount,0) * @TmpI
			SET @PordITaxSum = ISNULL(@PordITax,0) * @TmpI
			/*RZ990818- End*/			
			/**********************************************************/
			/*Main table*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems]
				([PreOrderId]
				,[pordIPrdId]
				,[pordIQuantity]
				,[pordIPrice]
				,[pordITotalPrice]
				,[pordIDescription]
				,[UserId]
				,[FPId]
				,[Month1]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]
				,[pordIQuantityDesc]
				,[pordIDiscount]
				,[pordIDiscountI]
				,[pordIDiscountPercent]
				,[pordIDiscountIPercent]
				,[pordIPrdUnitId]
				,[pordICurrencyUnitId]
				,[pordIOrderView]
				,[pordIFCR]
				,[pordITax]
				,[pordITaxIndex]
				,[pordICalculateTax]
				,[pordINotVisible]
				,[pordIFCRI]
				,[pordINotInSum]
				,[pordIWHId]
				,[pordIRound]
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[FirstRegisterDate]
				,[pordIRoundPlus]
				,[pordIPrdPriceGroupID]
				/*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/
				,[pordITracing]
				,[pordIPriceSum]
				,[pordIDiscountSum]				
				,[pordITaxSum]
				/*RZ990818- End*/
				/*NKR14020727- Start*/
				,[pordIValidityExpireDate]
				,[pordIValidityExpireDatePersian]
				,[pordIDeliveryTermId]
				,[pordIDeliveryDate]
				,[pordIDeliveryDatePersian]
				,[pordIDeliveryTime]
				,[pordIDeliveryDay]
				,[pordIDeliveryEndDate]
				,[pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian]
				,[pordIDeliveryEndTime]
				,[pordIDeliveryEndDay]
				/*NKR14020727- End*/)
			VALUES
				(@PreOrderId
				,@PordIPrdId
				,@PordIQuantity
				,@PordIPrice
				,@PordITotalPrice
				,@PordIDescription
				,@UserId
				,@FPId
				,@Month1
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@PordIQuantityDesc
				,@PordIDiscount
				,@PordIDiscountI
				,@PordIDiscountPercent
				,@PordIDiscountIPercent
				,@PordIPrdUnitId
				,@PordICurrencyUnitId
				,@PordIOrderView
				,@PordIFCR
				,@PordITax
				,@PordITaxIndex
				,@PordICalculateTax
				,@PordINotVisible
				,@PordIFCRI
				,@PordINotInSum
				,@PordIWHId
				,@PordIRound
				,@AppType
				,@DefaultLanguageID
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate
				,@PordIRoundPlus
				,@PordIPrdPriceGroupID
				/*RZ990818- Add sum of tax,discount,price before tax*/
				,@PordITracing
				,@pordIPriceSum
				,@pordIDiscountSum				
				,@pordITaxSum
				/*RZ990818- End*/
				/*NKR14020727- Start*/
				,@PordIValidityExpireDate
				,@PordIValidityExpireDatePersian
				,@PordIDeliveryTermId
				,@PordIDeliveryDate
				,@PordIDeliveryDatePersian
				,@PordIDeliveryTime
				,@PordIDeliveryDay
				,@PordIDeliveryEndDate
				,@PordIDeliveryEndDatePersian
				,@PordIDeliveryEndTime
				,@PordIDeliveryEndDay
				/*NKR14020727- End*/)
			/**/
			SET @InsertedPreOrderItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()			
			/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems_Lang]
				([PreOrderItemID]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[pordIDescription]
				,[pordIQuantityDesc])
			SELECT
				 @InsertedPreOrderItemId
				,LN.LanguageID
				,@PordIDescription
				,@PordIQuantityDesc
			FROM dbo.Languages LN 
			WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
			AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1

		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert PreOrderItem' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 				
		/*==================== PreOrderItemSpecification ===============*/
		EXEC [dbo].[ApispInsertPreOrderItemSpecificationAll]
		@PrdId = @PordIPrdId,
		@PreOrderItemId = @InsertedPreOrderItemId,
		@UserId = @UserId,
		@Month1 = @Month1,
		@FPId = @FPId,
		@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
		@AppType = @AppType,
		@LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID						
		/*=========================== Calculate PreOrder ====================*/		
		EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts] 	@PreOrderId, @Month1, @LanguageID, @DefaultLanguageID				
		/**/
	END
	/**/	
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1

END TRY  
BEGIN CATCH  
	 /*RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)*/
	 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
     SET @InsertedPreOrderItemId = 0	 
	 SET @ErrorMessage = 'Error|'
	 IF(ISNULL(@ErrorFrom,'') <> '')
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + @ErrorFrom
 	 SELECT 0	 
END CATCH  	
/********************************************************************************/
/**/
END








GO
-- [dbo].[ApispInsertPreOrderItemSpecification]




/*RZ990206*/
/*RZ990520- Add check pordIsFixed*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispInsertPreOrderItemSpecification]
/*
	exec ApispInsertPreOrderItemSpecification 								
*/
(
@PreOrderItemId INT,
@Specification NVARCHAR(300),
@SpecificationI NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,
@SpecificationII NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,	
/**/
@UserId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20),
@LanguageID INT = NULL, 
/**/
@InsertedPreOrderItemSpcflId INT OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/**********************************Default LanguageID ***************************/
DECLARE @DefaultLanguageID INT 
SET @DefaultLanguageID = (SELECT TOP 1 [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])

/********************************************************************************/
/************************************ Year1 => FPId *****************************/
DECLARE @FPId INT
SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Error & Variables *******************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT
/********************************************************************************/
/********************************* Check fields *********************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/*@PreOrderItem*/
IF(ISNULL(@PreOrderItemId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	SET @PreOrderItemId = (SELECT TOP 1 [PreOrderItemID] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] WHERE [PreOrderItemID] = @PreOrderItemId)
END
IF(ISNULL(@PreOrderItemId,0) <= 0)
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordItemId|'

/*RZ990520- Add check pordIsFixed*/
DECLARE @IsFixed BIT
SET @IsFixed = (SELECT TOP 1 pordIsFixed FROM dbo.WSPreOrder WHERE PreOrderID = 
						(SELECT TOP 1 PreOrderId FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems WHERE PreOrderItemID = @PreOrderItemID))
IF(ISNULL(@IsFixed,0) = 1)
BEGIN
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'IsFixed|'
END
/*RZ990520- End*/
/********************************************************************************/
/******************** Insert PreOrder Item Specification ************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRY  
    IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')/*No Error*/
	BEGIN		
		/*======================== Order Item ==========================*/		
		BEGIN TRAN			
			/**********************************************************/
			/*Main table*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification]
				([PreOrderItemId]
				,[Specification]
				,[UserId]
				,[Month1]
				,[FPId]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]				
				,[SpecificationI]
				,[SpecificationII]
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[FirstRegisterDate])
			VALUES
				(@PreOrderItemId
				,@Specification
				,@UserId
				,@Month1
				,@FPId
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@SpecificationI
				,@SpecificationII
				,@AppType
				,@DefaultLanguageID
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate)
			/**/
			SET @InsertedPreOrderItemSpcflId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()			
			/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderItemSpecification_Lang]
				([PreOrderItemSpcflID]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[Specification]
				,[SpecificationI]
				,[SpecificationII])
			SELECT
				 @InsertedPreOrderItemSpcflId
				,LN.LanguageID
				,@Specification
				,@SpecificationI
				,@SpecificationII
			FROM dbo.Languages LN 
			WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
			AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1
						
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert PreOrderItem' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 									
		/**/
	END
	/**/	
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1

END TRY  
BEGIN CATCH  
	 /*RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)*/
	 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
     SET @InsertedPreOrderItemSpcflId = 0	 
	 SET @ErrorMessage = 'Error|'
	 IF(ISNULL(@ErrorFrom,'') <> '')
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + @ErrorFrom
 	 SELECT 0	 
END CATCH  	
/********************************************************************************/
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[ApispInsertPreOrderPayments]





/*RZ990206*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispInsertPreOrderPayments]
/*
	exec ApispInsertPreOrderPayments 								
*/
(
/*PreOrder*/
@PreOrderId INT = NULL,
@PordNumber BIGINT = NULL,
/*PreOrder Payment*/
@PordpayTypeId INT = NULL,
@PordpayDocId INT = NULL,
@PordpayDocTypeId INT = NULL,
@PordpayAmount NUMERIC(18, 2),
@PordpayDocNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@PordpayDocDate NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
@PordpayDocDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
@PordpayDocAmount NUMERIC(18, 2) = NULL,
@PordpayDescription NVARCHAR(2000) = NULL,
/**/
@UserId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20),
@LanguageID INT = NULL, 
/**/
@InsertedPreOrderPaymentId INT OUTPUT,
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** TRANSACTION *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRANSACTION Main
/**/
/********************************************************************************/
/**********************************Default LanguageID ***************************/
DECLARE @DefaultLanguageID INT 
SET @DefaultLanguageID = (SELECT TOP 1 [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])

/********************************************************************************/
/************************************ Year1 => FPId *****************************/
DECLARE @FPId INT
SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)
/********************************************************************************/
/****************************** Error & Variables *******************************/
DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT
/********************************************************************************/
/********************************* Check fields *********************************/
/********************************************************************************/
/*PreOrder*/
IF(ISNULL(@PreOrderId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	SET @PreOrderId = (SELECT TOP 1 [PreOrderID] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] WHERE [PreOrderID] = @PreOrderId)
END
ELSE IF (ISNULL(@PordNumber,0) <> 0)
BEGIN
	SET @PreOrderId = (SELECT TOP 1 [PreOrderID] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] WHERE [pordNumber] = @PordNumber)
END
IF(ISNULL(@PreOrderId,0) <= 0)
	SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordId|'
/*PordpayTypeId*/
IF(ISNULL(@PordpayTypeId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
	IF((SELECT TOP 1 [OrderPaymentTypeId] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderPaymentTypes] WHERE [OrderPaymentTypeId] = @PordpayTypeId) IS NULL)
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PordpayTypeId|'
END
ELSE
	SET @PordpayTypeId = (SELECT TOP 1 [OrderPaymentTypeId] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderPaymentTypes] ORDER BY OrderView)
/*PordpayDocDate*/
IF(@PordpayDocDateTime IS NULL)
BEGIN
	SET @PordpayDocDateTime = @RegisterDate
	SET @PordpayDocDate = @RegisterDatePersian
END	
/**/
IF(ISNULL(@PordpayDocAmount,0) = 0)
BEGIN
	SET @PordpayDocAmount = @PordpayAmount
END
/********************************************************************************/
/******************** Insert PreOrder Payments *************************************/
/********************************************************************************/
BEGIN TRY  
    IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')/*No Error*/
	BEGIN		
		/*======================== PreOrder Paymant ==========================*/		
		BEGIN TRAN			
			/*Main table*/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments]
				([ImportDate]
				,[pordpayTypeId]
				,[pordpayPreOrderId]
				,[pordpayDocId]
				,[pordpayDocTypeId]
				,[pordpayAmount]
				,[pordpayDocNumber]
				,[pordpayDocDate]
				,[pordpayDocAmount]
				,[pordpayDescription]
				,[pordpayFirstRegisterUserId]
				,[pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian]
				,[pordpayFirstRegisteDate]
				,[UserId]
				,[FPId]
				,[Month1]
				,[RegisterDate]
				,[RegisterDatePersian]
				,[ImportDateTime]
				,[pordpayDocDateTime]
				,[AppType]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[FirstRegisterUserId]
				,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
				,[FirstRegisterDate])
			VALUES
				(@RegisterDatePersian
				,@PordpayTypeId
				,@PreOrderId
				,@PordpayDocId
				,@PordpayDocTypeId
				,@PordpayAmount
				,@PordpayDocNumber
				,@PordpayDocDate
				,@PordpayDocAmount
				,@PordpayDescription
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate
				,@UserId 
				,@FPId
				,@Month1
				,@RegisterDate
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate
				,@PordpayDocDateTime				
				,@AppType
				,@DefaultLanguageID
				,@UserId
				,@RegisterDatePersian
				,@RegisterDate)
			/**/
			SET @InsertedPreOrderPaymentId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()			
			/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSPreOrderPayments_Lang]
				([PreOrderPaymentId]
				,[LanguageID]
				,[pordpayDescription])
			SELECT
				 @InsertedPreOrderPaymentId
				,LN.LanguageID
				,@PordpayDescription
			FROM dbo.Languages LN 
			WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
			AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1
			
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert PreOrderPayment' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN 							
		/*=========================== Calculate PreOrder ====================*/		
		EXEC [dbo].[ApispCalcAndUpdatePreOrderAmounts] 	@PreOrderId, @Month1, @LanguageID, @DefaultLanguageID		
		/**/
	END
	/**/	
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main 
	SELECT 1

END TRY  
BEGIN CATCH  
	 /*RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)*/
	 ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
     SET @InsertedPreOrderPaymentId = 0	 
	 SET @ErrorMessage = 'Error|'
	 IF(ISNULL(@ErrorFrom,'')  <> '')
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + @ErrorFrom
 	 SELECT 0	 
END CATCH  	
/********************************************************************************/
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[ApispInsertProjectProductHistoryByOrderId]






/*RZ14010919*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[ApispInsertProjectProductHistoryByOrderId]
(		
/*
	exec ApispInsertProjectProductHistoryByOrderId 
	@OrderId = 2685, 
	@UserId = 3,
	@Month1 = 12,
	@RegisterDate = '2021-03-10',
	@RegisterDatePersian = '13991220',
	@AppType = 'dasti'
*/
@OrderId INT, 
@UserId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20)
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*-------------------------- Begin define variables -----------------------*/
/**********************************Default LanguageID ***************************/
DECLARE @DefaultLanguageID INT 
SET @DefaultLanguageID = (SELECT TOP 1 [DefaultLanguageID] FROM [dbo].[CrmsysSetting])
/********************************************************************************/
/************************************ Year1 => FPId *****************************/
DECLARE @Year1 SMALLINT
SET @Year1 = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)
/********************************************************************************/
/************************************ Error *************************************/
DECLARE @CompanyId INT = NULL,
@ProjectId INT = NULL,
@ProductId INT = NULL,
@PrjPrdHOrderId INT = NULL,
@PrjPrdHOrderItemId INT = NULL


SELECT TOP(1)
	@CompanyId = ordCompanyId,
	@ProjectId = ordProjectId,
	@PrjPrdHOrderId = OrderID
FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] 
WHERE OrderID = @OrderId
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/

DECLARE CrsrOrdItem CURSOR LOCAL FOR
/**/				
	SELECT 
		 [OrderItemID]
		,[ordIPrdId]		
	FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI
	WHERE OI.ordIPrdId IN(SELECT P.ProductId FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P WHERE ISNULL([PrdValidityCountingMinIsActive],0) = 1 OR ISNULL([PrdValidityCountingNumIsActive],0) = 1) 
	AND OI.OrderId IN(SELECT O.OrderId FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O WHERE O.[OrderId] = @OrderId AND ISNULL(O.ordIsVerified, 0) = 1)

/**/
OPEN CrsrOrdItem
FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrOrdItem INTO @PrjPrdHOrderItemId,@ProductId
WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
BEGIN
/**/
	/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
	/*Main table*/
	DECLARE @InsertedPrjPrdHistoryId INT = NULL
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory]
		([PrjPrdHCompanyId]
		,[PrjPrdHProjectId]
		,[PrjPrdHProductId]
		,[PrjPrdHOrderId]
		,[PrjPrdHOrderItemId]
		,[PrjPrdHSetDate]
		,[PrjPrdHSetDatePersian]		
		,[FirstRegisterUserId]
		,[FirstRegisterDate]
		,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
		,[UserId]
		,[Year1]
		,[Month1]
		,[RegisterDate]
		,[RegisterDatePersian]			
		,[AppType]
		,[LanguageID])
	VALUES
		(@CompanyId
		,@ProjectId
		,@ProductId
		,@PrjPrdHOrderId
		,@PrjPrdHOrderItemId
		,@RegisterDate
		,@RegisterDatePersian		
		,@UserId
		,@RegisterDate
		,@RegisterDatePersian
		,@UserId
		,@Year1
		,@Month1
		,@RegisterDate
		,@RegisterDatePersian			
		,@AppType
		,@DefaultLanguageID)			
	SET @InsertedPrjPrdHistoryId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
	/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
	IF(ISNULL(@InsertedPrjPrdHistoryId,0) <> 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmProjectProductHistory_Lang]
			([ProjectProductHistoryId]
			,[LanguageID]				
			,[PrjPrdHDesc])
		SELECT
				@InsertedPrjPrdHistoryId
			,LN.LanguageID
			,''
		FROM dbo.Languages LN 
		WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
		AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1	
	/**/
	END					
	/*=============================================*/					
			
	/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
/**/	
FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrOrdItem INTO @PrjPrdHOrderItemId,@ProductId
END
CLOSE CrsrOrdItem
DEALLOCATE CrsrOrdItem
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/		
END

	@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT

AS
BEGIN
	/**/

	BEGIN TRANSACTION Main

	DECLARE @ErrorFrom VARCHAR(50), @ErrorNumber INT

	/********************************************************************************/
	/************************************ FPId **************************************/
	DECLARE @FPId INT
	SET @FPId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ID] FROM [dbo].[WSFinancialPeriod] ORDER BY [ID] DESC)
	/************************************ WHId **************************************/
	/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
	IF(@WHId IS NULL OR @WHId <= 0)
		SET @WHId = (SELECT TOP 1 [WareHouseID] FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse] WHERE ISNULL([WrhsDeActive],0) = 1)
	/*RZ990519- End*/
	/*===================== Start Checks =====================*/
	
	BEGIN/*No DocOprID*/
		IF (ISNULL(@OprId, 0) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 DO.DocOprID FROM WSDocOpr DO WHERE DO.DocOprID = @OprId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No DocOprID|'
		END
		ELSE
		BEGIN
			SET @OprId = (SELECT TOP 1 [DocOprID] FROM dbo.WSDocOpr OO ORDER BY OrderView )/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
		END
	END

	/*========== Doc Checking ==========*/

	BEGIN/*No WHId*/
		IF (ISNULL(@WHId, 0) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 WH.WareHouseID FROM WsWareHouse WH WHERE WH.WareHouseID = @WHId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No WHId|'
		END
	END

	/*========== Doc Checking ==========*/

	BEGIN/*No OrderId*/
		IF (ISNULL(@OrderId, 0) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 O.OrderID FROM WSOrder O WHERE O.OrderID = @OrderId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No OrderId|'
		END
	END

	/*========== Doc Checking ==========*/

	BEGIN/*No BuyOrderId*/
		IF (ISNULL(@BuyOrderId, 0) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 BO.BuyOrderID FROM WSBuyOrder BO WHERE BO.BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No BuyOrderId|'
		END
	END

	/*========== Doc Checking ==========*/

	BEGIN/*No WHIdI*/
		IF (ISNULL(@WHIdI, 0) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 WH.WareHouseID FROM WsWareHouse WH WHERE WH.WareHouseID = @WHIdI) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No WHIdI|'
		END
	END

	/*========== Doc Checking ==========*/

	BEGIN/*No CompanyId*/
		IF (ISNULL(@CompanyId, 0) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 DO.CompanyID FROM CrmCompany DO WHERE DO.CompanyID = @CompanyId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No CmpId|'
		END
		/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
		ELSE IF (@CompanyNumber IS NOT NULL AND @CompanyNumber <> '')
		BEGIN
			SET @CompanyId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyID] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] WHERE [cmpNumber] = @CompanyNumber)
		END
		ELSE IF (@CompanyCode IS NOT NULL AND @CompanyCode <> '')

	END
	
	/*========== Doc Checking ==========*/
	BEGIN/*No CompanyPersonId*/
		DECLARE @PrsId INT

		END

				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrsId|'
		END
		ELSE IF(ISNULL(@CompanyPersonId,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @CompanyPersonId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CompanyPersonID] FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons WHERE prsCompanyId = @CompanyId ORDER BY ISNULL(prsOrderView, CompanyPersonID))
			IF(ISNULL(@CompanyPersonId,0) = 0)
			BEGIN

					   ,N'��������'
				FROM dbo.Languages L
				WHERE ISNULL(IsActive,0) = 1 
				AND L.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			/**/
			END		
		/**/
		END		
		/*RZ990519- End*/
	END
	
	/*========== Doc Checking ==========*/

	BEGIN/*No ProjectId*/
		DECLARE @PrjId INT
		IF (ISNULL(@ProjectId, 0) > 0)
		BEGIN
			IF ((SELECT TOP 1 CCP.ProjectID FROM CrmProjects CCP WHERE CCP.ProjectID = @ProjectId) IS NULL)
				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrjId|'
		END
		/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/

				SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrjId|'
		END

	END
	/*PORODUCT*/
	IF(ISNULL(@DocItemsJson,'') = '')	/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
		SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No Prd|'
	
	/********************************************************************************/
	/********************************* DocNumber ************************************/
	/********************************************************************************/	
	/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
	IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')

		BEGIN
			SET @DocNumber = (SELECT [dbo].[ApifnReturnDocNumber] (@OprId))
		END

	/*====================== End Checks ======================*/
	
	IF(ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') = '')/*No Error*/

			BEGIN TRAN

					/**/
					IF(@ProjectTitle IS NULL OR @ProjectTitle = '')
						SET @ProjectTitle = @DefualtProjectTitle
					/**/
					SET @ProjectId =(SELECT TOP 1 P.ProjectID FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] P

							  NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like '%' + 
							  REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@ProjectTitle AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
							  NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'))

						IF(ISNULL(@ProjectId,0) = 0)
						BEGIN

							/*-------------------------------------------------*/
							/*RZ14021004- Add OrderView to project*/
							DECLARE @prjOrderView INT = 0
							EXEC [dbo].[ApispReturnCompanyProjectOrderView]
							@CompanyId  = @CompanyId,
							@prjOrderView = @prjOrderView  OUTPUT					
							/*RZ14021004- End*/

								   ,[UserId]

								   ,[RegisterDate]

								   ,[LanguageID]

								   ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]

								   ,[FirstRegisterDate]

							 VALUES

								   ,@ProjectTitle
								   ,@RegisterDatePersian
								   ,@RegisterDay
								   ,@RegisterTime
								   ,@RegisterDate
								   ,@UserId
								   ,@RegisterDatePersian
								   ,@UserId
								   ,@UserId
								   ,CAST(@FPId AS SMALLINT)
								   ,@Month1
								   ,@RegisterDate
								   ,@RegisterDatePersian
								   ,@AppType
								   ,@DefaultLanguageID
								   ,@UserId
								   ,@RegisterDate
								   ,@RegisterDatePersian
								   ,@RegisterDate
								   ,@RegisterDate
								   ,@prjcnvsStatusGroupId
								   ,@prjOrderView/*RZ14021004- Add OrderView to project*/)
							/**/

							/*********** Insert to lang table ***********/
							INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmProjects_Lang]

								,L.LanguageID

							FROM [dbo].[Languages] L
							WHERE L.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
							AND ISNULL(L.IsActive,0) = 1
						/**/
						END					
					END
				END	
			IF (@@ERROR > 0)
			BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert Project' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
			ELSE 
			COMMIT TRAN
			/*RZ990519- END*/

			/*========================================= Doc ======================================*/

			INSERT INTO WSDoc(
						[OprId],
						[DocNumber],
						[DocDate],
						[DocDateTime],
						[SenderId],
						[SenderName],
						[FactorNumber],
						[Description],
						[IsFix],

						[OrderId],
						[BuyOrderId],
						[SendAddress],
						[DriverName],
						[DriverTel],
						[FreightName],
						[BijakNumber],
						[CarNumber],
						[WHIdI],
						[CompanyId],
						[CompanyPersonId],
						[ProjectId],
						[cmpTitle],
						[DocLinkAccCodeId],
						[DocLinkAccCode],
						[DocLinkAccDocId],
						[DocLinkAccDocNumber],
						[DocLinkAccVoucherId],
						[DocLinkAccVoucherNumber],
						[FirstRegisterUserId],
						[FirstRegisterDate],
						[FirstRegisterDatePersian],
						[FPId],
						[UserId],
						[RegisterDate],
						[RegisterDatePersian],
						[Month1],
						[AppType],
						[LanguageID])
				VALUES(
						@OprId,
						@DocNumber,
						@DocDate,
						@DocDateTime,
						@SenderId,
						@SenderName,
						@FactorNumber,
						@Description,
						@IsFix,
						@WHId,
						@OrderId,
						@BuyOrderId,
						@SendAddress,
						@DriverName,
						@DriverTel,
						@FreightName,
						@BijakNumber,
						@CarNumber,
						@WHIdI,
						@CompanyId,
						@CompanyPersonId,
						@ProjectId,
						@cmpTitle,
						@DocLinkAccCodeId,
						@DocLinkAccCode,
						@DocLinkAccDocId,
						@DocLinkAccDocNumber,
						@DocLinkAccVoucherId,
						@DocLinkAccVoucherNumber,
						@UserId,
						@RegisterDate,
						@RegisterDatePersian,
						@FPId,
						@UserId,
						@RegisterDate,
						@RegisterDatePersian,
						@Month1,
						@AppType,
						@DefaultLanguageID
				)

			SET @InsertedDocId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
			SET @InsertedDocNumber = @DocNumber/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
			/*====================== Start Multi Language [en] ======================*/

			INSERT INTO WSDoc_Lang(
					[DocID],
					[LanguageID],
					[SenderName],
					[Description],
					[SendAddress],
					[DriverName],
					[FreightName],
					[cmpTitle])
			SELECT
					@InsertedDocId,
					LN.LanguageID,
					@SenderName,
					@Description,
					@SendAddress,
					@DriverName,
					@FreightName,
					@cmpTitle
			FROM Languages LN
			WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
			AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1

			/*======================= End Multi Language [en] =======================*/

		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
			BEGIN SET @ErrorFrom='Error In Insert WSDoc' SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR ROLLBACK TRAN END		
		ELSE 
			COMMIT TRAN

		/*====================== Start Insert Doc Item ======================*/

		DECLARE
		@ItmId INT,
		@PrdCode NVARCHAR(50),
		@ItmDocId INT,
		@ItmPrdId INT,
		@ItmQuantity FLOAT = NULL,
		@ItmCost NUMERIC(18,0) = NULL,
		@ItmDescription NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
		@ItmIsFix SMALLINT = NULL,
		@ItmWHId INT = NULL,
		@ItmNotVisible BIT = NULL,           
		@ItmFCR FLOAT = NULL,
		@ItmFCRI FLOAT = NULL,
		@ItmTracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
		@DocItem NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL

		BEGIN TRAN
		DECLARE CrsrItem CURSOR FOR 
			SELECT 
			(STUFF((SELECT '|' + CAST(PJIN.[Name] AS NVARCHAR(max)) + '=' + CAST(PJIN.[StringValue] AS NVARCHAR(max))							
								FROM [dbo].[fnParseJSON](@DocItemsJson) PJIN
								WHERE PJIN.[Parent_ID] =PJ.[SequenceNO]
								FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, '')) 
			FROM [dbo].[fnParseJSON](@DocItemsJson) PJ
			WHERE PJ.[ValueType] = 'object'
		OPEN CrsrItem
		FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrItem INTO @DocItem
		/**/
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN		
		/**/
			DECLARE @InsertedDocItemId INT
			/**/
			SET @ItmId = NULL
			SET @PrdCode = NULL
			SET @ItmPrdId = NULL
			SET @ItmQuantity = NULL
			SET @ItmCost = NULL
			SET @ItmDescription = NULL
			SET @ItmIsFix = NULL
			SET @ItmWHId = NULL
			SET @ItmNotVisible = NULL
			SET @ItmFCR = NULL
			SET @ItmFCRI = NULL
			SET @ItmTracing = NULL

			/**/
			IF(@DocItem IS NOT NULL AND @DocItem <> '')
			BEGIN
				/**/
				SET @DocItem = @DocItem + '|'/*for last field*/
				DECLARE @ItmField NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
				WHILE LEN(@DocItem) > 0
				BEGIN
					/**/
					IF(PATINDEX('%|%',@DocItem) > 0)
					BEGIN
						/**/
						SET @ItmField = SUBSTRING(@DocItem, 0, PATINDEX('%|%', @DocItem))
						/**/
						DECLARE @ItmFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
						DECLARE @ItmFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
						SET @ItmFieldName = SUBSTRING(@ItmField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@ItmField)-1)                                    
						SET @ItmFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@ItmField, CHARINDEX('=', @ItmField)+1, Len(@ItmField))
						
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'ItemId'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @ItmId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS INT) END									  
						END	
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'PrdId'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @ItmPrdId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS INT) END									  
						END		
						/*--------------------------*/						
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'PrdCode'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @PrdCode = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,'')  = '' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE @ItmFieldValue END
						END
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'Quantity'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @ItmQuantity = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS FLOAT) END
						END
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'Cost'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @ItmCost = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS NUMERIC(18,0)) END
						END
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'Description'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @ItmDescription = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,'')  = '' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(4000)) END
						END
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'IsFix'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @ItmIsFix = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,0) = 0
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS BIT) END
						END
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'WHId'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @ItmWHId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,0)  = 0 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS INT) END
						END
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'NotVisible'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @ItmNotVisible = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,'')  = '' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS BIT) END
						END
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'FCR'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @ItmFCR = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS FLOAT) END
						END
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'FCRI'
						BEGIN						
							SELECT @ItmFCRI = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,'0')  = '0'
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS FLOAT) END
						END
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						IF @ItmFieldName = 'Tracing'
						BEGIN
							SELECT @ItmTracing = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmFieldValue,'')  = ''
									  THEN NULL 
									  ELSE CAST(@ItmFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(200)) END
						END
						/*============ Finding Column ============*/
						SET @DocItem = SUBSTRING(@DocItem,
                                  LEN(@ItmField + '|') + 1,
							      LEN(@DocItem))
						
						
						/**/
						END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						SET @ItmField = @DocItem
						SET @DocItem = NULL						
					END
				END

				/*=========================== Start Item Checks ===========================*/
				/*========== Item Checking ==========*/		
				/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
				BEGIN/*No PrdId*/
					IF(@PrdCode IS NOT NULL AND @PrdCode <> '')
					BEGIN
						SET @ItmPrdId = (SELECT TOP 1 [ProductID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] WHERE [PrdCode] = @PrdCode)
					END
					ELSE IF (ISNULL(@ItmPrdId, 0) > 0)
					BEGIN
						IF (SELECT P.ProductID FROM WSProducts P WHERE P.ProductID = @ItmPrdId) IS NULL
							SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrdId|'
					END
					
					IF (ISNULL(@ItmPrdId, 0) <= 0)
						SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No PrdId|'
				END
				/*RZ990519- End*/
				
				/*========== Item Checking ==========*/

				BEGIN/*No WHId*/
					IF (ISNULL(@ItmWHId, 0) > 0)
					BEGIN
						IF (SELECT P.WareHouseID FROM WsWareHouse P WHERE P.WareHouseID = @ItmWHId) IS NULL
							SET @ErrorMessage = @ErrorMessage + 'No WHId|'
					END
				END

				/*============================ End Item Checks ============================*/

				/*=========================== Start Insert Item ===========================*/

				IF(@ErrorMessage IS NULL OR @ErrorMessage = '')/*No Error*/
				BEGIN
					/**/			
					INSERT INTO WSDocItems(
						[DocId],
						[PrdId],
						[Quantity],
						[CostId],
						[Cost],
						[Description],
						[IsFix],
						[WHId],
						[NotVisible],
						[FCR],
						[FCRI],
						[DocILinkAccItemId],
						[Tracing],
						[FPId],
						[Month1],
						[UserId],
						[RegisterDate],
						[RegisterDatePersian],
						[AppType],
						[LanguageID],
						[FirstRegisterUserId],
						[FirstRegisterDate],
						[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
					)
					VALUES(
						@InsertedDocId,
						@ItmPrdId,
						@ItmQuantity,
						NULL,
						@ItmCost,
						@ItmDescription,
						@ItmIsFix,
						@ItmWHId,
						@ItmNotVisible,
						@ItmFCR,
						@ItmFCRI,
						NULL,
						@ItmTracing,
						@FPId,
						@Month1,
						@UserId,
						@RegisterDate,
						@RegisterDatePersian,
						@AppType,
						@DefaultLanguageID,
						@UserId,
						@RegisterDate,
						@RegisterDatePersian
					)
					SET @InsertedDocItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()					
					/*=================== Start Multi Language [en] ===================*/
					/*======================= Start Add Columns =======================*/			
			
					INSERT INTO WSDocItems_Lang(
						DocItemID,
						LanguageID,
						[Description]
					)				
					SELECT
						@InsertedDocItemId,
						LN.LanguageID,
						@ItmDescription
					FROM Languages LN
					WHERE LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID AND 
					ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1

					/*======================= End Add Columns =======================*/
					/*=================== End Multi Language [en] ===================*/			

					/*=================== Start Insert Doc Item Serial ===================*/

					IF (ISNULL(@InsertedDocItemId, 0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@DocItemSerialsJson,'') <> '')
					BEGIN

						/**/
						DECLARE
							@ItmSnID INT,
							@ItmSnSerial NVARCHAR(50),
							@ItmSnStatusId INT,
							@ItmSnDescription NVARCHAR(300),
							@ItmSnIsFix SMALLINT,
							@ItmSnWHId INT,
							@ItmSnSerialId INT,
							@ItmSnIsAutoBarcode BIT,
							@ItmSntblTmpBarcodeId INT,           
							@ItmSnExpDate DATETIME,
							@ItmSnExpDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
							@ItmSnInfoI NVARCHAR(200),
							@ItmSnInfoII NVARCHAR(200),
							@ItmSnInfoIII NVARCHAR(200),
							@ItmSnInfoIV NVARCHAR(200),
							@ItmSnInfoV NVARCHAR(200),
							@ItmSnInfoVI NVARCHAR(200),
							@DocItemSn NVARCHAR(MAX)

						/*BEGIN TRAN*/
						DECLARE CrsrItemSn CURSOR LOCAL FOR 
							SELECT 
							(STUFF((SELECT '|' + CAST(PJIN.[Name] AS NVARCHAR(max)) + '=' + CAST(PJIN.[StringValue] AS NVARCHAR(max))							
												FROM [dbo].[fnParseJSON](@DocItemSerialsJson) PJIN
												WHERE PJIN.[Parent_ID] =PJ.[SequenceNO]
												FOR XML PATH(''), TYPE).value(N'(./text())[1]', N'NVARCHAR(MAX)'), 1, 1, '')) 
							FROM [dbo].[fnParseJSON](@DocItemSerialsJson) PJ
							WHERE PJ.[ValueType] = 'object'
						OPEN CrsrItemSn
						FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrItemSn INTO @DocItemSn
						/**/
						WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
						BEGIN
							/**/
							SET @ItmSnID = NULL
							SET @ItmSnSerial = NULL
							SET @ItmSnStatusId = NULL
							SET @ItmSnDescription = NULL
							SET @ItmSnIsFix = NULL
							SET @ItmSnWHId = NULL
							SET @ItmSnSerialId = NULL
							SET @ItmSnIsAutoBarcode = NULL
							SET @ItmSntblTmpBarcodeId = NULL
							SET @ItmSnExpDate = NULL
							SET @ItmSnExpDatePersian = NULL
							SET @ItmSnInfoI = NULL
							SET @ItmSnInfoII = NULL
							SET @ItmSnInfoIII = NULL
							SET @ItmSnInfoIV = NULL
							SET @ItmSnInfoV = NULL
							SET @ItmSnInfoVI = NULL

							/**/

							IF(@DocItemSn IS NOT NULL AND @DocItemSn <> '')
							BEGIN
								/**/
								SET @DocItemSn = @DocItemSn + '|'/*for last field*/
								DECLARE @ItmSnField NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''
								WHILE LEN(@DocItemSn) > 0
								BEGIN																		
									IF(PATINDEX('%|%',@DocItemSn) > 0)
									BEGIN
										/*BEGIN TRAN*/
											SET @ItmSnField = SUBSTRING(@DocItemSn, 0, PATINDEX('%|%', @DocItemSn))
											/**/
											DECLARE @ItmSnFieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
											DECLARE @ItmSnFieldName NVARCHAR(200)
											SET @ItmSnFieldName = SUBSTRING(@ItmSnField, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@ItmSnField)-1)                                    
											SET @ItmSnFieldValue = SUBSTRING(@ItmSnField, CHARINDEX('=', @ItmSnField)+1, Len(@ItmSnField))
						
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'ItemId'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnID = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS INT) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'Serial'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnSerial = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(50)) END									  
											END		
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'StatusId'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnStatusId = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS INT) END
											END
											/*============ Finding Column ============*/
											IF @ItmSnFieldName = 'Description'
											BEGIN
												SELECT @ItmSnDescription = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@ItmSnFieldValue,'0')  = '0' 
															THEN NULL 
															ELSE CAST(@ItmSnFieldValue AS NVARCHAR(300)) END
											END
											/*============ Find
